*Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Ziyue Zhu,Jinyang Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCAD 提出面向长程智能体的结构化信用分配与蒸馏框架，将交互拆为规划与有界子任务执行，用跨 rollout 子任务前缀树精炼规划信用，并在局部上下文蒸馏执行步骤，在文本与多模态基准上分别较最强训练基线提升 4.48 和 4.19 个宏平均准确率百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体训练中，稀疏的终端奖励难以区分中间步骤的贡献，而在线策略蒸馏又会因学生生成的历史不断增长而丢失有效的教师指导信号。

Method: SCAD 将智能体的长程交互显式划分为规划阶段与有界子任务执行阶段。规划阶段在完整上下文下生成高层计划或子任务分解；执行阶段则在局部、有界的上下文窗口中完成每个子任务，从而避免学生生成历史不断增长导致教师指导信号稀释。训练时采用两种监督来源：规划器通过跨多个 rollout 构建的子任务前缀树进行信用分配，精确追踪哪些前缀决策最终导向成功，并接收完整的终端奖励信用；执行器接收正的终端信用以及教师模型在局部执行上下文中的蒸馏指导。该架构通过将信用分配与蒸馏分离到规划与执行两个层级，缓解了稀疏终端奖励与在线策略蒸馏的指导丢失问题，无需改动基础模型结构即可端到端训练长程智能体。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "宏平均准确率", "value": "比最强训练基线高 4.48 个百分点", "setting": "文本任务，全部评测基准"}, {"metric": "宏平均准确率", "value": "比最强训练基线高 4.19 个百分点", "setting": "多模态任务，全部评测基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将交互组织为规划与有界子任务执行两个阶段，执行在局部上下文中进行蒸馏；通过跨 rollout 的子任务前缀树精炼规划信用分配；规划接收完整终端信用，执行接收正终端信用与教师指导的联合监督

Abstract: Training long-horizon agents to solve complex tasks requires effective supervision over extended interaction sequences. However, sparse terminal rewards obscure intermediate contributions, while on-policy distillation can lose informative teacher guidance as student-generated histories grow. To address this problem, we introduce SCAD, which organizes interactions into planning and bounded subtask execution, distills execution in local contexts, and refines planning credit through cross-rollout subtask prefix trees, with planning receiving full terminal credit and execution receiving positive terminal credit and teacher guidance. Across all evaluated benchmarks, SCAD improves macro-average accuracy over the strongest training baseline by 4.48 percentage points for text tasks and 4.19 points for multimodal tasks. SCAD effectively combines outcome-based credit assignment with teacher-guided distillation to improve planning and execution in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [23] [Page-EntroKV: Hardware-Aligned, Entropy-Weighted KV-Cache Eviction under Grouped-Query Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03135)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Page-EntroKV提出一种面向GQA和PagedAttention的KV-cache淘汰框架，在物理组粒度上用sink隔离的Renyi-2熵加权池化头重要性，并将分数投影到页帧上。理论证明了并集开销比与组内分歧的关系、预算保持性及针检索保持界。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，相比独立选择将UOR从最高4.75倍降至1.000，消除13倍sink伪装，20%预算下针检索召回100%而均值池化0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态KV-cache淘汰方法按每个查询头独立评分，与GQA共享物理KV缓冲的分配方式不匹配：独立选择会保留各头选择的并集，导致缓存膨胀；而算术平均池化会稀释承担事实召回的专业检索头。

Method: Page-EntroKV面向GQA架构的KV-cache淘汰。输入为prefill阶段产生的各头查询/键表示；首先对每个查询头计算sink隔离的Renyi-2碰撞熵，该熵通过一次内积得到，无需校准，且排除sink头的影响，使sink头无法伪装为检索头。然后，对每个物理GQA组内的多个查询头，按上述熵权重进行加权池化，得到组级重要性分数。池化后的分数被投影到PagedAttention的页帧上，依据页帧粒度进行聚合。淘汰操作在硬件元组（层、组、页）上执行，确保与GQA共享物理KV缓冲的分配方式一致。训练目标/优化：论文为分析性框架，没有训练新的模型参数，而是基于预训练模型进行淘汰策略的评估。推理/服务流程：在prefill阶段计算每头一次内积得到熵权重，池化后映射到页帧，在解码阶段根据预算淘汰页。该设计解决了逐头独立淘汰导致的并集开销膨胀和均值池化稀释检索头的问题，并提供了并集开销比（UOR）的理论界和预算保持性证明。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "union overhead ratio (UOR)", "value": "最高4.75倍（独立选择） vs 恰好1.000（Page-EntroKV）", "setting": "Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，r=6，2%预算，在2,240组测量上进行头独立重放"}, {"metric": "sink masquerade移除", "value": "13倍", "setting": "sink隔离机制，具体设置未详细披露"}, {"metric": "针检索召回率", "value": "100% vs 0%（均值池化）", "setting": "20%预算下的needle recall"}, {"metric": "保留基数精确性", "value": "对每个页大小均精确", "setting": "Page-EntroKV在页帧上的保留基数"}, {"metric": "QA与代码任务可解性", "value": "在20%保留率下仍可解", "setting": "未指定具体数据集与指标"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Page-EntroKV框架，在GQA实际分配的物理组粒度上进行KV-cache淘汰，而非逐查询头独立淘汰。；使用基于sink隔离的Renyi-2碰撞熵为组内各头加权池化，每个头仅需一次内积，在prefill阶段计算一次且无需校准，避免sink头伪装成检索头。；将池化分数投影到PagedAttention页帧上，在硬件元组（层、组、页）粒度执行淘汰。；形式化定义并证明并集开销比（UOR）与组内分歧之间的关系，给出双头组的精确恒等式及任意组比下的双边界。；证明严格预算保持性和有限上下文针检索保持界，并指出算术平均池化可证明违反该界。；给出精确的逐层页记账方法。

Abstract: Serving long-context autoregressive language models is constrained by the key-value (KV) cache. Most dynamic eviction methods score token importance per query head and choose tokens independently. This fits poorly with grouped-query attention (GQA), where several query heads share one physical KV buffer: divergent per-head selections force the serving engine to retain the union of their choices - inflating the cache by up to the group ratio r - while arithmetic mean pooling dilutes the specialized retrieval heads that carry factual recall. We introduce Page-EntroKV, a formal framework for KV-cache eviction operating at the granularity GQA serving actually allocates. Heads within each physical group are pooled by weights derived from sink-isolated collision (Renyi-2) entropy - one inner product per head, computed once at prefill with no calibration - so sink heads cannot masquerade as retrieval heads. Pooled scores are projected onto PagedAttention page frames, and eviction executes at the hardware tuple (layer, group, page). We formalize the union overhead ratio (UOR) and intra-group disagreement, prove an exact identity linking them for two-head groups alongside two-sided bounds at every group ratio, prove strict budget preservation and a finite-context needle-retention bound that arithmetic mean pooling provably violates, and give exact per-layer page accounting. On a pilot architecture (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, r=6), head-independent replay over 2,240 group measurements yields union overhead up to 4.75x at a 2% budget, while Page-EntroKV holds UOR exactly 1.000; sink isolation removes a 13x sink masquerade; needle recall is 100% versus 0% for mean pooling at a 20% budget; retained cardinality is exact for every page size; and QA and code tasks remain solvable at 20% retention.

</details>


### [24] [Zephon: Elastic Determinism for Online, Stateful Foundation Model Data Loading Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03087)
*Maximilian Böther,Josh Wills,Ties Robroek,Sonnet Xu,Paul Burstein,Daniel Zayas,Cody Blakeney,Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Luke Merrick,Pratyush Maini,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt,Ana Klimovic,Bogdan Gaza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Zephon 是面向基础模型训练的生产级数据加载器，通过拓扑无关 lane、串行化顺序决策和仅检查点有界 in-flight 状态，在在线有状态流水线中实现弹性确定性，并在文本与视觉-语言负载上保持竞争吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线、有状态的基础模型数据流水线中，tokenize、pack、mix 等 n-to-m 变换破坏了样本索引，导致在 GPU 拓扑变化、频繁 checkpoint-resume 和不同执行后端下难以保证全局训练数据批次序列的确定性。

Method: Zephon 面向在线、有状态的基础模型数据加载流水线。输入是原始训练数据流，数据经过 tokenization、packing 和 mixing 等有状态 n-to-m 变换，产生全局训练批次序列。核心设计包括：将全局流划分为拓扑无关的 lane，使每个 lane 内的顺序独立于 GPU 拓扑；在 lane 内串行化顺序决策以保证确定性，同时将无状态工作并行分配到可互换的后端执行；仅对有界的 in-flight 状态进行 checkpoint，因此从 checkpoint 恢复时不需要重放整个训练历史，恢复成本不随训练进度增长。训练目标或优化未在摘要中披露。推理/服务路径即数据加载路径：在训练过程中按确定性顺序向训练进程提供全局批次，并在拓扑变化或恢复后继续产生一致的序列。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "吞吐量", "value": "竞争力吞吐（无具体数值）", "setting": "文本与视觉-语言工作负载评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出弹性确定性概念：在 GPU 拓扑变化、频繁恢复和不同数据处理后端下仍保持全局训练数据批次序列确定。；将全局流划分为拓扑无关的 lane，使数据序列与 GPU 拓扑解耦。；串行化顺序决策并并行化无状态工作，支持可互换执行后端。；仅 checkpoint 有界 in-flight 状态，使恢复成本不随训练进度增长。

Abstract: Deterministic data loading is important for foundation model development: model researchers need confidence that differences they observe across costly ablations are caused by the parameter they changed rather than non-determinism in the training data sequence. The data loader must provide elastic determinism, i.e., a deterministic sequence of global training data batches despite changes to the GPU topology across runs (e.g., due to GPU scarcity), frequent checkpoint-resume cycles, and different data processing execution backends. Achieving this is difficult because modern foundation model data pipelines tokenize, pack, and mix samples online, introducing stateful n-to-m transformations that break sample indexing. Existing data loaders largely assume indexable 1-to-1 pipelines, and the common workaround of offline materialization is expensive and, for some modalities such as video, infeasible. We present Zephon, a data loader for foundation models that supports online, stateful pipelines while providing elastic determinism and efficient resumption from checkpoints. It partitions the global stream into topology-independent lanes, serializes ordering decisions while parallelizing stateless work on interchangeable backends, and checkpoints only bounded in-flight state so recovery cost does not grow with training progress. We evaluate Zephon on text and vision-language workloads and show that it achieves competitive throughput while providing a combination of guarantees that no existing loader offers for online, stateful pipelines.

</details>


### [25] [AvoKV-E: Payload-Aware KV Cache Eviction for Long Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03007)
*Han Yu,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Hejian Sang,Han Shi,Menglin Zhou,Xuanzhao Dong,Minzhou Huang,Rui Cai,Hao Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AvoKV-E，一种无需训练的 KV 缓存淘汰策略，针对长推理生成中的缓存瓶颈，通过延迟淘汰资格和融合读取压力、键冗余与值载荷的多维度评分，在相同缓存预算下匹配或超越现有基线，尤其在紧凑缓存场景下提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理过程中，KV缓存瓶颈从固定prompt转移到生成的trace，现有淘汰方法仅从路由角度估计旧key是否会被读取，忽略了低注意力条目可能携带大value载荷以及新生成状态可能被过早判定为陈旧的问题。

Method: AvoKV-E 是一种无需训练即可应用的推理缓存淘汰策略。它处理长链式推理生成过程中累积的 KV 缓存条目。核心流程包括：首先，对最近生成的 KV 状态实施延迟淘汰机制，使它们只有在经过一定步数后才有资格被淘汰，从而避免过早删除尚未被后续查询读取的有效状态。接着，对已具备淘汰资格的缓存条目，计算三个维度的分数：候选归一化读取压力（衡量该条目相对于候选集合的读取需求）、键冗余（条目间的重复程度）以及值载荷潜力（该条目所承载的 value 对后续预测的重要性）。综合这些分数对条目进行排序，并淘汰排名靠后的条目，以维持预设的活跃 KV 预算。该方法完全无需额外训练，可直接集成到现有推理框架中，通过保留对推理轨迹具有高价值的 value 载荷来提升长推理任务中的缓存效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 训练无关的淘汰策略AvoKV-E，延迟最近状态的淘汰资格；基于候选归一化读取压力、键冗余和值载荷潜力的排序机制

Abstract: Long-output reasoning shifts the KV-cache bottleneck from the fixed prompt to the generated trace. Existing reasoning-cache eviction methods largely treat cached entries as routing objects, estimating whether an old key will still be read, will recur, or can be replaced. This routing-only view overlooks two effects: low-attention entries can carry large value payloads whose removal changes future predictions, and newly generated states can appear stale before later queries have had a chance to read them. We introduce AvoKV-E, a training-free eviction policy that first delays eligibility for recent states and then ranks eligible entries using candidate-normalized read pressure, key redundancy, and value-payload potential. According to empirical evaluation across different models and datasets, AvoKV-E matches or exceeds redundancy-aware, recurrence-based, and thought-adaptive eviction baselines at matched active-KV budgets, with its largest gains in the tightest-cache regime. Component and counterfactual analyses further connect these gains to delayed observation, payload-aware scoring, redundancy, and scale-robust normalization. Together, the results show that long-reasoning KV eviction should preserve not only keys that are likely to be read, but also the value payloads that sustain the reasoning trajectory.

</details>


### [26] [Dynamic Expert Pruning for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02951)
*Jabin Koo,Soheil Abbasloo,Sungjae Lee,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEP针对多智能体场景下静态专家剪枝无法适应任务/角色变化的问题，利用系统与任务提示通过轻量预测器动态生成每请求的专家掩码，在多种任务、规模和架构上提升精度，并在低专家保留率时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然减少了计算量，但所有专家仍需驻留内存，导致部署受内存限制；静态专家剪枝使用一个固定掩码应对所有请求，无法适应多智能体系统中不同任务和角色对专家的动态需求，造成精度下降。

Method: DEP工作流程：输入为智能体的系统提示和任务提示；轻量级专家预测器读取这些文本，通过一次前向传播输出每个请求的专家掩码，指定激活哪些专家。该预测器在工作流对话记录上训练一次，无需对每个配置重新校准。推理时，使用该掩码对MoE模型进行剪枝，仅激活被选中的专家，从而减少内存占用。训练目标未在摘要中明确，但核心是利用提示文本与所需专家之间的映射关系。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现智能体的系统提示和任务提示本身足以识别该智能体及其任务所需的专家，因为文本已描述其行为。；提出轻量级预测器，仅需一次前向传播即可将提示转化为每请求的专用专家掩码，无需为每个配置进行离线校准。；DEP在多种任务、角色、模型规模和MoE架构上超越静态剪枝和合并基线，并能泛化到训练中未见的工作流。；在保留专家数量极少时，DEP的优势最大，表明多智能体系统中的角色特化允许比静态剪枝更稀疏的服务。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale language models efficiently by activating only a few experts per token, but the saving is confined to computation: every expert must stay resident on the accelerator, so memory bounds where these models can be deployed. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, yet existing methods are static --- a single mask, calibrated offline, is applied to the model for every subsequent request. This assumption can fail when the workload is heterogeneous, most prominently in multi-agent systems, where one backbone serves many tasks and roles at once: our analysis shows that different tasks and roles recruit different experts, while static methods assign one fixed subset to all of them. We therefore propose Dynamic Expert Pruning (DEP), which rests on a finding we establish here: an agent's system and task prompts are by themselves sufficient to identify the experts that agent and its task require, since that text already describes what the agent will do. A lightweight predictor, trained once on workflow transcripts, turns those prompts into a specialized per-request mask in a single forward pass, with no per-configuration calibration. Across diverse tasks and roles, model scales, and MoE architectures, DEP achieves better overall accuracy than static pruning and merging baselines, and generalizes to workflows unseen in training without retraining. Its margin over those baselines is largest when few experts are retained, suggesting that the role specialization inherent to multi-agent systems permits sparser serving than static pruning allows.

</details>


### [27] [DyRA: Dynamic Residual Approximation for Efficient Matrix Multiplication in DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02882)
*Daewon Chae,Hyunwon Chung,Changwoo Lee,Hun-Seok Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyRA 通过输入自适应的输出残差低秩校正，改进结构化权重近似，使大模型稠密矩阵乘法在同等算力预算下精度更高，DINOv3 上实现 1.5 倍端到端 GPU 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化权重近似（如低秩分解）在权重空间优化，而推理精度由输出激活决定，输入激活会放大小权重误差，导致输出误差大、精度-效率权衡不佳。

Method: DyRA 是一种面向结构化矩阵乘法近似的输入自适应方法。其输入为标准的稠密权重矩阵与推理时的输入激活；核心思路是不再仅在权重空间最小化近似误差，而是直接以输出激活为目标优化低秩因子。推理流程为：首先用高效结构化形式（如低秩分解）近似原始权重矩阵并执行结构化矩阵乘法，快速得到初步输出；随后在推理时动态估计该结构化近似引入的输出残差误差，并利用输入相关的校正项对该残差进行低秩近似与补偿，从而得到更接近全矩阵乘法结果的输出。训练/优化目标直接作用于输出空间的低秩因子，使得在相同计算预算下近似更忠实；校正模块依赖当前输入激活，因而具备输入自适应性。最终在视觉、语音、语言模型上取得更好的精度-效率权衡，报告 DINOv3 端到端 GPU 加速 1.5 倍、精度退化较仅权重基线降低 3 倍以上。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端GPU加速比", "value": "1.5×", "setting": "DINOv3，与稠密矩阵乘法基线相比"}, {"metric": "精度退化降低幅度", "value": ">3×", "setting": "DINOv3，相对仅权重近似基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 指出矩阵乘法可通过对输出进行低秩因子直接优化来更有效地近似；提出输入自适应的动态残差近似与校正机制，在推理时估计并修正结构化权重近似带来的输出误差；在相同计算预算下结合高效结构化计算与输入相关校正，实现更忠实的全矩阵乘法近似

Abstract: Large-scale foundation models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but their size makes inference costly, largely due to dense matrix multiplications. Prior work reduces this cost by replacing dense weight matrices with efficient structured forms such as low-rank factorizations. However, these methods approximate weights rather than the output activations that determine inference accuracy. Consequently, small weight-space errors can be amplified by input activations, producing large output errors. In this work, we propose DyRA, an input-adaptive method that improves structured matrix multiplication approximation by correcting residual output errors during inference. We show that matrix multiplication can be approximated more effectively by directly optimizing low-rank factors of the output. DyRA builds on this insight by dynamically approximating and correcting the output error introduced by structured weight approximations. This combines efficient structured computation with input-dependent correction, yielding a more faithful approximation of full matrix multiplication under the same computational budget. Across vision, speech, and language models, DyRA consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off over structured weight approximations alone. Notably, DyRA achieves a 1.5$\times$ end-to-end GPU speedup for DINOv3 while reducing accuracy degradation by more than 3$\times$ relative to weight-only baselines.

</details>


### [28] [All Work And No Play Makes Jack a Dull Boy: Understanding and Preventing Catastrophic Strategy Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02835)
*Qiyuan Huang,Tianshi Xu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文深入分析了LLM在RLVR后训练中出现的晚期灾难性策略崩溃现象，从优化和信息论角度给出了机制解释，提出了在线预警信号MEI和防崩溃方法Mesh Learning，在多个基准上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO风格RLVR后训练中出现的晚期灾难性策略崩溃（策略容量收缩）及其预防。

Method: 论文针对RLVR后训练中GRPO类算法的晚期崩溃问题。输入为LLM在多推理任务上的轨迹，通过轨迹级策略更新交互定义策略。理论框架结合优化动力学分析概率质量的集中过程与信息论中维持准确率所需的最小策略容量，推导出冲突机制。基于该机制提出镜像纠缠指数（MEI），作为在线预警信号监控策略容量收缩。为防止崩溃，Mesh Learning方法同时暴露多个推理策略并阻止任一策略在优化中占据主导，从而保持策略多样性。训练目标仍为RLVR的奖励最大化，但引入策略级约束/正则以维持容量。推理时使用训练后的策略集合或多样化策略。实验在Qwen和Phi模型系列上、跨五个基准验证，相比强基线获得最高13.4和11.5个百分点的准确率提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "最高提升13.4个百分点", "setting": "Qwen模型系列，AIME26/AIME25/MATH-500/GPQA/LiveCodeBench基准"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "最高提升11.5个百分点", "setting": "Phi模型系列，AIME26/AIME25/MATH-500/GPQA/LiveCodeBench基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 定义了基于轨迹级策略更新交互的策略概念，并建立结合优化动力学与信息论的统一理论框架；证明主要RLVR目标会逐步将概率质量集中于单一策略，而维持任务准确率需要最小策略容量，两者冲突导致崩溃；提出轻量级在线预警信号——镜像纠缠指数（MEI）；提出Mesh Learning方法，通过暴露多种推理策略防止单一策略主导优化

Abstract: During post-training of large language models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), GRPO-style algorithms can exhibit severe late-stage collapse. Prompt-based probing reveals that this is not benign strategic pruning, but a harmful contraction of effective strategy capacity that makes distinct reasoning strategies increasingly inaccessible. To characterize this phenomenon, we define strategies through trajectory-level policy-update interactions and develop a unified theoretical framework combining optimization dynamics and information theory. We prove that major RLVR objectives progressively concentrate probability mass onto a single strategy, while sustaining nontrivial task accuracy requires a minimum strategy capacity. The conflict between these two results provides a mechanistic explanation for catastrophic collapse. We further derive the {Mirrored Entanglement Index (MEI)} as a lightweight online warning signal. To prevent collapse, we propose \textbf{Mesh Learning}, which exposes multiple reasoning strategies and prevents any single strategy from dominating optimization. Across AIME26, AIME25, MATH-500, GPQA, and LiveCodeBench, Mesh Learning consistently outperforms strong baselines across Qwen and Phi model families, with gains of up to 13.4 pp and 11.5 pp, respectively. These results establish strategy preservation as a key principle for stable RLVR. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [29] [Gated Slot Attention-2: Two-Sided Associative Memory Correction in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02816)
*Ruijie Li,Shengnan Ding,Weimin Zhang,Derick Tang,Zhanpeng Zeng,Qinsong Zeng,Ming Chen,Jiaxi Hu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GSA2 通过结合键侧 Gated Oja Rule-2 与值侧 Gated Delta Rule-2，在共享槽位中实现双边记忆校正，并给出硬件高效的分块并行训练算法，在多个基准上持续优于线性注意力基线，同时保持线性时间与恒定内存推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型的固定大小循环记忆难以有效管理，现有 delta-rule 变体与槽位架构（如 Gated Slot Attention）分别从值侧和两阶段结构入手，但未充分结合两者的互补性来同时校正键侧和值侧记忆。

Method: GSA2 在输入序列上维护共享的潜在槽位（latent slots）来表示键和值的关联记忆。前向流程分为两个阶段：首先，键侧使用 Gated Oja Rule-2 对键关联进行校正，该规则基于 Oja 规则并引入解耦的擦除门和写入门，以控制旧记忆的擦除和新记忆的写入；其次，值侧使用 Gated Delta Rule-2 对值关联进行校正，同样具有门控机制。两个阶段通过共享槽位交互，使得键和值的记忆校正互相协调。训练时采用推导的分块（chunkwise）算法，将序列切分为块以并行计算，同时保持与循环形式等价的数学结果。推理时使用循环解码，内存恒定，计算复杂度为线性时间。论文未披露损失函数细节、模型规模、训练数据或具体超参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "一致优于强线性注意力基线", "setting": "多个基准测试，具体指标未披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Gated Oja Rule 用于键侧记忆校正，并扩展为带解耦擦除与写入控制的 Gated Oja Rule-2；提出 Gated Slot Attention-2 (GSA2)，通过共享潜在槽位将键侧 Gated Oja Rule-2 与值侧 Gated Delta Rule-2 结合，实现双边关联记忆校正；推导了面向硬件效率的分块（chunkwise）并行训练算法；保持线性时间序列建模与恒定内存循环解码

Abstract: Linear attention models have emerged as efficient alternatives to standard attention, but effectively managing their fixed-size recurrent memory remains challenging. To improve memory, recent work has explored two distinct directions: delta-rule variants for precise correction of values associated with keys, and slot-based architectures such as Gated Slot Attention for modeling key and value memories in two stages. We observe that these directions are complementary--the delta rule provides effective memory correction, while the two-stage structure provides a natural way to operate on both sides of an association. Building on this insight, we introduce a new Gated Oja Rule for key-side correction and extend it with decoupled erase and write control to obtain Gated Oja Rule-2. We then introduce Gated Slot Attention-2 (GSA2), which combines Gated Oja Rule-2 for key-side correction with Gated Delta Rule-2 for value-side correction through shared latent slots. We further derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise algorithm for parallel training. Experiments demonstrate that GSA2 consistently improves over strong linear-attention baselines across benchmarks while retaining linear-time sequence modeling and constant-memory recurrent decoding.

</details>


### [30] [Exact Memory-Time Optimization for Prefix-Cached Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02766)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR)框架，将前缀缓存保留超时优化建模为精确的最大权重闭包问题，用最小割求解，支持无序、分组、访问重置超时，并用回放实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限请求轨迹下，如何精确优化前缀缓存块的保留超时，使可用前缀收益最大化，同时尊重前缀依赖关系，避免独立优化的过度计数。

Method: 输入是有限请求轨迹（Mooncake公开请求），包含块查找和超时选择。PCR将可用前缀奖励表示为有向图中的节点，节点之间通过前缀依赖和超时阈值形成前提条件。最大化总奖励的问题转化为最大权重闭包问题，进而归约为一次最小割。图的规模与块查找数量和超时选择数量呈线性。断点定理将构造扩展到连续非负超时。对于有序超时，提出基于网格大小的线性时间动态规划，并给出限制有序超时成本的界。推理时，根据轨迹回放和优化结果决定每个块的保留超时，衡量指标为可用前缀块和存储时间，而非GPU延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "无序超时达到无限制训练最优的案例数", "value": "118/120", "setting": "固定网格，有序超时，trace-grouping-price 案例"}, {"metric": "异构保留策略改进的留出内存-时间权衡", "value": "若干权衡点有所改进", "setting": "留出评估，未给出具体数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR)：将可用前缀奖励建模为带前提条件的节点（超时阈值和前序命中证书），形成精确的有限轨迹优化公式。；将最大权重闭包问题归约为一次最小割，图的规模与块查找和超时选择的数量呈线性关系。；证明断点定理，将构造扩展到所有非负超时值而无需离散化误差。；为有序超时场景推导线性时间的动态规划算法，并给出限制该有序超时成本的上界证书。；使用公开Mooncake请求集（39,632条）进行时间回放实验，并验证小实例穷举。

Abstract: Retaining language-model prefix states trades recomputation against storage time. Optimizing each cached block independently can overcount savings: a resident block is usable only when the required preceding prefix is also available. We introduce Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR), an exact finite-trace formulation for static, grouped, reset-on-access timeouts. Usable-prefix rewards become nodes whose prerequisites are timeout thresholds and preceding hit certificates. The resulting maximum-weight closure reduces to one minimum cut, with graph size linear in the number of block lookups and timeout choices. A breakpoint theorem extends the construction to all nonnegative timeouts without discretization error. We also derive a linear-time-in-grid-size dynamic program for ordered timeouts and bounds that certify the cost of this restriction. Exhaustive small-instance checks and chronological replay of 39,632 public Mooncake requests validate the formulation. On the fixed grid, ordered timeouts attain the unrestricted training optimum in 118 of 120 trace-grouping-price cases. Heterogeneous retention improves several held-out memory-time tradeoffs, but finer training optimization does not uniformly improve transfer. The contribution is a tractable optimization model and an auditable benchmark for retention policies; the experiments measure usable prefix blocks and storage time, not GPU latency.

</details>


### [31] [Inner Momentum for Differentially Private Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02738)
*Bishnu Bhusal,Minh Vu,Ben Southworth,Geigh Zollicoffer,Rohit Chadha,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DP-Muon-IM，通过在裁剪前对每个样本的 Muon 梯度在近期模型历史上做内动量平均，缓解差分隐私裁剪对奇异向量几何的扭曲。理论分析给出裁剪后矩阵分解及误差上界，并证明 Newton-Schulz 正交化保留极因子。在 GPT-2 私有微调上一致提升 BLEU 和 ROUGE-L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Differential privacy clipping distorts the relative singular-vector geometry of per-example gradients, which harms Muon's polar-factor update that depends only on singular vectors.

Method: The method modifies the Muon optimizer for differentially private training. Inputs are per-example gradients of the model parameters with respect to the loss. For each sampled example, instead of using the current gradient alone, the method computes an inner momentum by averaging the example's Muon gradient over the current model and a short history of recent model states. This averaged gradient is then clipped radially per example (standard DP clipping). The clipped per-example gradients are summed to form a batch matrix. The authors show that this matrix decomposes into a common rescaling term and a covariance residual R between sampled gradients and clipping values, with Frobenius norm bounded by the product of the standard deviations of clipping factors and gradients. This bound directly limits clipping-induced distortion of singular vectors. The batch matrix is then orthogonalized by a finite Newton-Schulz iteration, which approximates the polar factor UV^T. The authors prove that under the spectral conditions of the bound, the Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor of its input, so the correction from inner momentum survives orthogonalization. Training uses the resulting orthogonalized update. The method is evaluated by fine-tuning GPT-2 on E2E and DART with DP-SGD style training at epsilon values of 1, 2, 4, and 8, comparing DP-Muon-IM to DP-Muon. Non-private diagnostics measure the polar error before noise addition.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "BLEU", "value": "improves over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison", "setting": "private GPT-2 fine-tuning on E2E at epsilon in {1,2,4,8}"}, {"metric": "ROUGE-L", "value": "improves over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison", "setting": "private GPT-2 fine-tuning on DART at epsilon in {1,2,4,8}"}, {"metric": "pre-noise polar error", "value": "2-4% lower", "setting": "non-private diagnostics"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Inner Momentum: averaging each example's Muon gradient over the current model and recent model history before clipping.；Theoretical decomposition showing the clipped batch matrix separates into a common rescaling and a covariance residual R with ||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G.；Proof that finite Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor under the derived spectral conditions.

Abstract: Differentially private training clips each per-example gradient before adding noise. This clipping is radial for each example, yet unequal clipping factors can distort the relative singular-vector geometry of their average. Muon is particularly exposed to this effect, since its update is an approximate polar factor UV^T that depends only on the singular vectors that clipping can shift. To curb this degradation, we propose averaging each sampled example's Muon gradient over the current model and a short history of recent models before clipping. The clipped batch matrix then separates into a common rescaling and a covariance residual R between sampled gradients and clipping values, with ||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G, bounding the clipping-induced distortion directly. We further show that a finite Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor of its input under these spectral conditions, confirming that our correction survives orthogonalization. In private GPT-2 fine-tuning on E2E and DART at epsilon in {1, 2, 4, 8}, DP-Muon-IM improves BLEU and ROUGE-L over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison, and non-private diagnostics show 2-4% lower pre-noise polar error.

</details>


### [32] [MuonIO: Principled Norm-Aware Descent for Embedding Tables and Language Model Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02705)
*Linkai Ma,Xinyu Luo,Mengbo Wang,Ananth Grama,Petros Drineas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonIO为Muon优化器提出统一的范数感知更新规则，同时覆盖embedding表与LM head，替代AdamW；在1B LLaMA预训练中降低50%优化器状态内存I/O、约46%更新FLOPs，并改善验证困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Muon优化器对隐藏线性层有基于谱范数/RMS稳定性的原则性更新，但输入embedding表和输出LM head仍使用AdamW，缺乏统一、几何动机明确的更新规则，且优化器状态内存与更新FLOPs较高。

Method: MuonIO针对LLM中两类特殊大矩阵设计统一的优化更新。输入embedding表E∈R^{d×V}接收one-hot输入几何，输出LM head L∈R^{V×d}位于softmax之前。论文分别以1→2算子范数和2→∞算子范数对局部线性化损失进行惩罚，并利用恒等式‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^T‖_{1→2}把两者映射到同一vocabulary-oriented几何空间。MuonIO随后应用单一归一化向量规则：对embedding表E执行列归一化，对LM head L执行行归一化，从而替代标准Muon对这两层使用的AdamW。该设计在训练1B LLaMA时减少了优化器状态的内存I/O与更新FLOPs，同时改善了验证集困惑度。摘要未披露前向模块、数据流、损失函数细节及推理/服务流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "I/O优化器状态内存", "value": "降低50%", "setting": "1B LLaMA预训练，C4数据集，与Muon对比"}, {"metric": "I/O更新FLOPs", "value": "降低约46%", "setting": "1B LLaMA预训练，C4数据集，与Muon对比"}, {"metric": "验证集困惑度", "value": "相对Muon有所改善（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "1B LLaMA预训练，C4数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出MuonIO：用统一的Muon式归一化更新同时处理embedding表与LM head；为LM head L∈R^{V×d}引入2→∞算子范数，依据softmax输出几何的Lipschitz连续性；为embedding表E∈R^{d×V}引入1→2算子范数，依据one-hot输入几何；利用范数恒等式‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^T‖_{1→2}，将两矩阵统一到同一vocabulary-oriented几何，实现列归一化(E)与行归一化(L)的统一规则；消除大矩阵的AdamW状态，使I/O优化器状态内存降低50%、I/O更新FLOPs降低约46%

Abstract: The Muon optimizer derives its update rule for hidden linear layers by solving a local linearization of the loss penalized by the spectral norm, motivated by an RMS-stability argument for dense linear layers. Standard Muon implementations, however, exclude the input (embedding table) and output (language model head) layers from this principled treatment, for which they use AdamW instead. We present MuonIO, a single Muon-style update for both of these layers. For the language model head $\mathbf{L} \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times d}$, we motivate the use of the $2\to\infty$ operator norm, due to the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax output geometry, while for the embedding table $\mathbf{E} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times V}$, we draw on the $1 \to 2$ operator norm, based on the one-hot input geometry identified by Bernstein & Newhouse (2025). The identity $\lVert\mathbf{L}\rVert_{2\to\infty}=\lVert\mathbf{L}^\top\rVert_{1\to2}$ then puts both matrices in the same vocabulary-oriented geometry: MuonIO applies a single normalized-vector rule, which appears as column normalization for $\mathbf{E}$ and row normalization for $\mathbf{L}$. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with MuonIO reducing I/O optimizer state memory by 50% and I/O update FLOPs by $\sim$46% compared to Muon for 1B LLaMA pretraining on C4, while also improving validation perplexity.

</details>

*Jessica Dierking,Itai Shapira,Niclas Boehmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现并系统刻画了后训练导致模型默认观点坍缩、上下文可引导性下降的现象，并提出以立场分布匹配约束表达的替代目标，对需要服务异质用户群体的LLM系统具有对齐与评估层面的参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练在让模型偏向单一一套价值观的同时，会削弱模型在上下文中根据用户/语境识别并忠实执行未训练偏好视角的能力（默认坍缩，default collapse）。

Method: 论文未披露具体模型架构细节。其研究流程为：将模型向文化价值分歧的某一侧进行微调，并在训练过程中评估多个检查点，观察训练侧观点在普通使用中的主导程度以及模型识别和忠实执行相反观点能力的变化；据此指出单一价值观优化与保留服务异质用户所需技术能力之间的张力；最后提出一个替代目标——在满足预设表达立场分布约束下最大化奖励，并给出立场分布匹配作为实用实现方案。摘要未披露具体模块、数据流、参数规模或推理路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过受控实验系统性揭示后训练导致的默认坍缩：训练侧观点在普通使用中逐渐主导，而对相反观点的识别与执行能力持续下降；提出最大化解码奖励并同时满足表达立场分布约束的替代训练目标；给出立场分布匹配（stance-distribution matching）作为该目标的实用实现，并进行分析

Abstract: AI models serving a heterogeneous population must act on the principles appropriate to each user and context. While post-training has been shown to narrow the views large language models express, prior work has focused on default behavior rather than the ability to adapt to in-context information. We show that post-training also degrades a model's ability to be steered in-context toward perspectives it was not trained to favor. In controlled experiments, we fine-tune models toward one side of cultural-value disagreements and evaluate checkpoints throughout training. The trained side becomes increasingly dominant in ordinary use, while the ability to recognize and faithfully enact the opposing view declines. These findings point to a tension between prioritizing a single set of values and preserving the technical capacity needed to serve diverse stakeholders. Finally, we propose and analyze an alternative objective that maximizes reward subject to a prescribed distribution over expressed perspectives, and present stance-distribution matching as a practical implementation.

</details>


### [34] [Activation Sparsity with Weight Approximation for Faster LLM Decoding on Offloaded Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02598)
*JuneHyung Kim,Sankeerth Durvasula,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpAx将激活稀疏的二元跳过/读取扩展为三选项：保留、近似、省略，按激活幅值动态选择权重精度，在权重卸载到CPU内存或闪存时显著加速LLM解码，同时将WikiText-2困惑度增幅控制在10%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重卸载场景下，激活稀疏跳过权重虽然能减少传输，但随跳过比例增加模型质量急剧下降，需要在模型质量与解码速度之间取得更优权衡。

Method: SpAx在解码阶段处理每一层线性层的权重读取。输入为当前层的激活向量；对每个激活及其对应的权重，根据激活绝对值幅值将其划分为三类：绝对值最大的激活对应权重完全保留并读取原始精度权重；幅值较小的激活对应权重读取压缩后的近似权重表示；最接近零的激活对应权重直接省略，不进行读取。由于小激活对输出贡献被激活本身衰减，近似权重引入的误差被相应抑制；而压缩表示减少需要从CPU内存或闪存传输到GPU的字节数，从而降低对低带宽卸载路径的依赖。训练或优化目标未在摘要中披露；推理流程是逐层按激活幅值动态选择权重读取策略，未披露具体模型规模、压缩格式、阈值选择或端到端系统调度细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解码加速（权重卸载到CPU内存，16-bit权重）", "value": "平均3.86倍，最高5.57倍", "setting": "WikiText-2困惑度增加最多10%"}, {"metric": "解码加速（权重卸载到CPU内存，4-bit权重）", "value": "平均2.06倍，最高2.74倍", "setting": "WikiText-2困惑度增加最多10%"}, {"metric": "解码加速（权重卸载到闪存，16-bit权重）", "value": "平均3.31倍，最高4.81倍", "setting": "未明确披露困惑度约束"}, {"metric": "解码加速（权重卸载到闪存，4-bit权重）", "value": "平均1.54倍，最高2.03倍", "setting": "未明确披露困惑度约束"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将权重处理从二元选择（读取/跳过）扩展为三选项：完全保留、用压缩权重表示近似、完全省略。；按激活绝对值大小分配策略：最接近零的激活直接跳过权重，较小幅值激活读取近似权重，最大幅值激活读取原始权重。；利用小激活对近似误差的衰减效应，同时压缩表示减少传输字节，从而改善质量-速度权衡。；在CPU内存和闪存两种卸载目标上均验证了速度提升。

Abstract: Deploying LLMs on consumer-grade GPUs with insufficient memory to hold their weights can result in prohibitively slow inference, because decoding repeatedly transfers offloaded weights from system RAM or flash storage into GPU at much lower bandwidth than local GPU-memory access. Activation sparsity reduces these transfers by skipping weights associated with zero or near-zero activations. However, as more activation contributions are omitted, model quality eventually degrades rapidly, indicating that weights associated with small-magnitude activations collectively influence model quality sharply. In this work, we improve the trade-off between model quality and decoding performance when exploiting activation sparsity. Our key idea is to replace the binary choice of whether or not to read a weight with three options: fully retain it, approximate it using a compressed weight representation, or omit it entirely. SpAx skips weights associated with activations closest to zero, reads approximate weights for smaller-magnitude activations, and reads original weights for the largest-magnitude activations. Smaller-magnitude activations attenuate the errors introduced by approximate weights, while compressed weight representations require fewer bytes to be transferred. With weights offloaded to CPU memory, SpAx speeds up decoding by 3.86X on average (up to 5.57X) with 16-bit weights and 2.06X (up to 2.74X) with 4-bit weights, at a WikiText-2 perplexity increase of at most 10%. With weights offloaded to flash storage, the speedups are 3.31X on average (up to 4.81X) and 1.54X (up to 2.03X).

</details>


### [35] [Fisher-Guided Submodular Data Selection for Continual Pre-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02593)
*Zhenghao Zhao,Gaowen Liu,Zhiling Lan,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种Fisher引导的子模数据选择方法用于LLM持续预训练，通过分解梯度为锚点和前沿分量并在子模目标下流式选择，在1B token下达到10B token回放策略的适应和遗忘控制效果，实现10倍token效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续预训练中，目标领域语料的选择不当会覆盖预训练模型已编码的能力，导致灾难性遗忘；如何在有限token预算下选择数据以同时提升目标域质量并控制遗忘。

Method: 论文未提供完整端到端架构描述，但根据摘要，其方法包括：1）输入：候选数据样本、预训练模型参数；2）对每个候选样本，计算其梯度，并将其分解为锚点分量（测量沿预训练模型已承诺的高Fisher参数方向的扰动）和前沿分量（测量在未约束的低Fisher子空间中的更新容量）；3）使用对数行列式子模目标函数聚合这些信号，以选择子集；4）采用可扩展的流式数据选择管道，单次遍历完成优化；5）在TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3.1-8B上进行医疗领域CPT，评估目标域质量和预训练基准上的遗忘程度。训练目标旨在最大化目标域适应同时最小化对预训练能力的遗忘。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "token效率", "value": "1B selected tokens outperform replay with 10B tokens on adaptation and forgetting", "setting": "TinyLlama-1.1B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 在医疗数据上的持续预训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现基于损失的选型导致后CPT Fisher对角在高Fisher坐标下移，而低Fisher坐标几乎不变，揭示了参数空间中的遗忘机制；提出Fisher感知的CPT选择器，将每个候选的梯度分解为锚点分量（沿已承诺参数方向的扰动）和前沿分量（未约束低Fisher子空间中的更新容量）；使用对数行列式子模目标聚合这些信号，并通过一次可扩展的流式数据选择管道进行优化；在保持遗忘边界的同时提升目标域质量，且相比于遗忘感知回放策略具有显著token效率优势

Abstract: Data selection is already a central bottleneck in large-language-model training, where web-scale corpora are noisy and token budgets are finite. In continual pre-training (CPT), it becomes a forgetting-control problem: a poorly chosen target-domain corpus can overwrite capabilities encoded in the pretrained checkpoint. Existing CPT practice either scores candidates with parameter-agnostic scalars such as perplexity, or mitigates forgetting by spending many extra general-domain replay tokens. Neither strategy directly asks how training on a candidate will move the model parameters. We show that loss-based selection causes the post-CPT Fisher diagonal to drift downward on exactly the high-Fisher coordinates the pretrained model had committed to, while leaving low-Fisher coordinates largely untouched. This asymmetry exposes a parameter-space mechanism for catastrophic forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we propose a Fisher-aware CPT selector that decomposes each candidate's gradient into an anchor component, which measures perturbation along committed parameter directions, and a frontier component, which measures update capacity in unconstrained low-Fisher subspaces. We aggregate these signals with a log-determinant submodular objective and optimize it in a single pass using a scalable streaming data selection pipeline. On TinyLlama-1.1B and Llama-3.1-8B CPT over medical data, our selector improves target-domain quality while bounding forgetting on held-out pretraining benchmarks. Most importantly, it is substantially more token-efficient than forgetting-aware replay. 1B selected tokens already outperform the replay strategy trained with 10B tokens on both adaptation and forgetting, giving a 10x token-efficiency advantage.

</details>


### [36] [Dense Mixture-of-Experts as a Reparameterized Wide FFN: A Granularity Sweep at Fixed Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02584)
*Vu Quang Hoang,Nghia Hieu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在固定总 FFN 宽度下研究稠密软门控专家混合（所有 SwiGLU 专家对每个 token 激活）的专家数量 K 对验证损失的影响，发现 K=2 最优而 K=4、K=6 变差，并证明该结构等价于带 token 依赖组缩放的稠密 SwiGLU。结果基于单次运行，缺乏大规模或生产验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定总 FFN 宽度下，稠密软门控专家混合（所有专家对每个 token 激活）的专家数量 K 如何影响语言模型验证损失，以及动态专家组合本身相对于普通稠密 FFN 的贡献是什么。

Method: 输入为 token 序列，每个 token 的表示进入固定总宽度的 FFN 模块。该模块由 K 个并行的 SwiGLU 专家组成，每个专家对同一 token 计算输出；所有专家均被激活。一个 softmax 门控网络根据 token 表示产生 K 维单纯形权重，对各专家输出进行加权求和。总可训练参数/宽度保持与 K=1 的稠密 SwiGLU 基线一致，K=1 时退化为普通单专家 FFN。训练目标为标准语言建模损失，通过单次运行比较不同 K 下的验证损失。推理时所有专家都参与计算，因此计算量固定，不涉及稀疏选择或路由到部分专家。论文进一步证明该结构可重参数化为带 token 依赖、单纯形约束的组缩放稠密 SwiGLU：门控权重对每个专家子空间进行缩放，且 K=1 的稠密基线位于其函数类中。首层 K=4 模型出现近 one-hot 路由，通过强制均匀门控的诊断实验表明这种集中路由对模型功能重要。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "验证损失相对变化", "value": "K=2 比 K=1 基线改善 0.0048", "setting": "固定总 FFN 宽度，单次运行，K 扫描"}, {"metric": "验证损失相对变化", "value": "K=4 比基线恶化 0.0053", "setting": "固定总 FFN 宽度，单次运行，K 扫描"}, {"metric": "验证损失相对变化", "value": "K=6 比基线恶化 0.0197", "setting": "固定总 FFN 宽度，单次运行，K 扫描"}, {"metric": "诊断子集损失增加", "value": "强制 K=4 首层门控为均匀分布导致损失增加 2.4 nats", "setting": "K=4 模型首层，诊断子集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出固定计算量下的稠密 MoE 类比：K 个 SwiGLU 专家全部激活，由 softmax 门控组合，总 FFN 宽度固定，排除更大专家池和离散选择的影响。；通过 K 扫描发现非单调的验证损失趋势（K=2 最优，K=4、K=6 变差），并定位首层 K=4 模型的近 one-hot 路由及其功能重要性。；证明该架构等价于带 token 依赖、单纯形约束组缩放的稠密 SwiGLU，且函数类包含普通稠密基线。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models combine learned routing with selection from a large expert pool. We isolate the contribution of dynamic expert combination using a dense analogue: $K$ SwiGLU experts, all active for every token and combined by a softmax gate, at fixed total FFN width. With no larger pool or discrete selection, the dense baseline is the $K=1$ case. Validation loss varies non-monotonically with $K$: $K=2$ improves over the baseline by $0.0048$, whereas $K=4$ and $K=6$ worsen it by $0.0053$ and $0.0197$, respectively. Routing generally remains soft and expert usage balanced, except in the first layer of the $K=4$ model, where routing is nearly one-hot. Forcing this gate to uniform increases loss by $2.4$ nats on a diagnostic subset, indicating that its concentrated routing is functionally important. We further show that the architecture is exactly a dense SwiGLU with token-dependent, simplex-constrained group scaling and contains the dense baseline in its function class. These single-run results suggest a granularity sweet spot for always-active, softly gated FFNs at fixed width, with $K=2$ performing best among the configurations tested.

</details>

*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Jian Wan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Xin Chen*

TL;DR: 论文提出学生引导的教师蒸馏流水线，用 ModernBERT 一次前向生成 60 类任务路由的 top-k 候选，再由 DeBERTa-v3 零样本 NLI 教师重排候选并迭代蒸馏。最佳学生达到 77.5% 教师一致率，Coverage@16 初步为 91-100%，并指出截断 top-k 软目标会破坏 KL 蒸馏暗知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模零样本分类器逐标签计算成本随分类体系线性增长，如何在固定 60 类任务路由中以低推理成本获得高覆盖率的候选集并保持路由质量。

Method: 系统面向固定 60 类 LLM 任务路由。输入为用户请求文本。学生模型 ModernBERT 在一次前向传播中输出全部 60 类的概率分布，并据此检索 top-k 候选类别；随后较大的 DeBERTa-v3 零样本 NLI 教师模型仅对 top-k 候选进行重排，每个候选构造前提-假设对评分，避免对全部 60 类逐一评分。教师重排产生的标签被回传用于迭代训练学生，学生更新后生成更锐利的候选集，进入下一轮，形成学生引导的教师蒸馏闭环。训练目标涉及学生分类损失与蒸馏；文中特别指出若将截断 top-k 的教师分数直接当作全 60 类软目标做 KL 蒸馏，未被截断类别被置零会破坏软标签暗知识并引入系统性偏差。推理时服务的是同一学生模型，先单次前向得到候选，再由教师对 top-k 重排得到最终路由结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "教师一致率", "value": "77.5%", "setting": "最佳学生检查点，200 例评估集"}, {"metric": "Coverage@16", "value": "91-100%", "setting": "初步覆盖度测量，提示 top-k 保留大部分教师决策相关信息"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 学生引导的教师蒸馏流水线：紧凑 ModernBERT 学生一次前向预测全类别分布并检索 top-k，DeBERTa-v3 零样本 NLI 教师仅对 top-k 重排。；学生与教师迭代互促：教师重排标签用于继续训练学生，学生产生更锐利的候选，形成闭环。；候选生成器端到端在目标分类体系上训练，且与线上服务模型一致，区别于通用嵌入检索、聚类 shortlist 及路由到候选模型的做法。；指出截断 top-k 教师分数作为全 60 类软目标进行 KL 蒸馏会破坏暗知识并引入系统性偏差。

Abstract: Zero-shot classifiers are useful for routing user requests to specialized LLM tasks, but scoring every request against a large candidate set is expensive: a zero-shot NLI classifier must evaluate one premise-hypothesis pair per label, so cost scales linearly with taxonomy size. We study a student-guided teacher distillation pipeline for a fixed taxonomy of 60 LLM task categories: a compact ModernBERT classifier predicts the full category distribution in one forward pass and retrieves a small top-k candidate set, and a larger DeBERTa-v3 zero-shot NLI classifier reranks only those candidates rather than all 60 labels; the resulting teacher labels iteratively improve the student, which produces sharper candidates for the next round. Unlike generic embedding retrieval or clustering-derived shortlists used in extreme multi-label classification, our candidate generator is trained end-to-end on the target taxonomy and is the same model serving production traffic, distinguishing it from LLM-routing work that routes between candidate models, and from concurrent System-1 encoder-classifier proposals (e.g. TypeSafe AI's Jev and the open-source Laya project) whose training methodology is undocumented or RL-based. Our best student checkpoint reaches 77.5% teacher agreement on a 200-example evaluation set, and preliminary coverage measurements show Coverage@16 of 91-100%, suggesting top-k sets retain most of the teacher's decision-relevant information. We further show truncated top-k teacher scores should not be treated as full 60-class soft targets for KL distillation: zeroing untruncated classes destroys the dark knowledge soft-label distillation depends on, introducing systematic bias rather than a harmless sparse approximation. A complete evaluation, including coverage at multiple k on a held-out set, an embedding-retrieval baseline, and a larger human-reviewed test set, remains in progress.

</details>

*Zelin Zhao,Xinyu Guo,Jingyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为LLM智能体框架建立了资源受限的理论模型LAM，将上下文与持久内存的I/O、重计算、验证与检查点成本形式化，推导了通信、访问、重计算和可靠性四类下界与最优策略，并在GPT-6 Astra上进行了受控实验验证。对理解智能体系统的成本与可靠性权衡有理论指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何量化语言模型智能体框架（harness）所消耗的计算资源（上下文、记忆、重计算、验证），并建立资源受限的理论模型来指导系统设计与成本权衡。

Method: LAM将底层语义模型固定，智能体执行被抽象为一系列模型调用与上下文-持久内存之间的数据传输。输入为任务描述与程序化检查函数；主模块包括：上下文管理（容量C）和持久记忆（容量S）的访问接口、调用调度器（决定调用粒度与顺序）、验证器（可完美或不完美）。执行时，每次模型调用消耗上下文容量，中间状态可存入持久记忆以避免重复语义计算；数据传输受同时调用-传输预算约束。训练目标并非传统损失函数，而是资源消耗的最小化，通过红蓝博弈等价性推导I/O下界；可靠性分析给出阶段局部采样界、不完美验证成本及Young-Daly型检查点定律，输出最优验证间隔。推理流程：任务经链式分解，调度器按策略选择随机或顺序访问、确定检查点与验证频率，在给定的C和S下最小化总调用数与失败概率。该抽象用于分析何时记忆优于重计算、如何平衡调用粒度与可靠性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "通信与可靠性预测验证", "value": "受控与留出实验验证了通信预测、检查点最优、程序化检查下的策略选择、调用粒度与逻辑输入流量及可靠性的权衡", "setting": "GPT-6 Astra上的链式MATH任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出语言模型智能体机器（LAM）抽象，固定语义模型并将框架级资源显式计入成本；建立LAM执行与红蓝博弈（red-blue pebbling）在调用-传输预算下的实例等价性，将经典I/O下界迁移到上下文-内存流量；证明内存接口导致的渐近分离，例如指针追逐中随机访问与非投机顺序访问之间存在Θ(n)差距；推导位反转DAG在上下文容量C与持久内存容量S下的模型调用下界Θ(n^2/(C+S)+n)，量化存储中间状态何时避免重复语义计算；给出紧致的阶段局部采样界、不完美验证的精确成本，以及具有闭式最优验证间隔的Young-Daly型检查点定律

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on harnesses that manage bounded context, persistent memory, tools, verification, and repeated execution, yet existing notions of model capability do not quantify the computational resources these mechanisms consume. We introduce the Language Model Agent Machine (LAM), a resource-bounded abstraction that fixes the underlying semantic model while explicitly charging harness-level resources. We establish four classes of results. Communication: LAM execution is instancewise equivalent to red--blue pebbling under simultaneous call--transfer budgets, transferring classical I/O lower bounds to context--memory traffic. Access: memory interfaces induce asymptotic separations, including a $\Theta(n)$ gap between random and non-speculative sequential access on pointer chasing. Recomputation: bit-reversal DAGs require $\Theta(n^2/(C+S)+n)$ model calls with context capacity $C$ and persistent-memory capacity $S$, quantifying when stored intermediate state avoids repeated semantic computation. Reliability: we derive tight stage-local sampling bounds, exact imperfect-verification costs, and a Young--Daly-type checkpoint law with a closed-form optimal verification interval. Controlled and held-out experiments on GPT-6 Astra test communication and reliability predictions, including checkpoint optima, policy selection under programmatic checking, and tradeoffs among call granularity, logical input traffic, and reliability on chained MATH tasks. Together, these results provide a resource theory for the computational cost of language-model agent harnesses.

</details>


### [39] [Bandits via Additive Quantized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02440)
*Ami Tavory,Noam Touitou,Tal Sarig,Frank Cheng,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出残差量化（RQ）表示层，将连续上下文映射为多级离散质心，使加性上下文老虎机在严格有界内存和 O(1) 在线更新下获得非线性表达能力；在 13 个数据集中的 11 个超过非 RQ 变体，并以最多 1/1000 内存匹配 doubling-retrain XGBoost 与神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在保持 O(1) 有界内存与逐样本高效更新的同时，让老虎机模型具备非线性奖励表达能力，避免树模型/神经网络需周期重训和大回放缓冲区的问题。

Method: 整体架构分为三个层次。输入层：连续上下文向量 x 作为输入，先经过离线训练的残差量化（RQ）编码器；RQ 采用多级码本，第一级码本对 x 做量化得到最近质心并记录残差，后续各级对上一级残差继续量化，从而形成多级离散质心分配序列（即层级化的离散表示）。表示层：将上下文映射为多级离散分配后，每个质心（或层级组合）对应一组可学习的加性参数，影子机制（shadow mechanism）动态决定各层级/质心的启用与权重，使表示能适应在线数据分布变化而不需要重训整个模型。学习与推理层：在 RQ 表示之上叠加各类加性老虎机算法（如线性/加性打分形式），奖励模型为各质心贡献的加和，因而参数更新只涉及被激活的质心，实现逐样本 O(1) 更新与严格有界内存；在线决策时对每个动作计算加性打分并选择（含探索策略），无需回放缓冲区或周期性批量重训。优化目标为在线累积奖励最大化（如 regret 最小化），RQ 码本离线训练以提供稳定表示，影子机制在线调整层级使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对于非 RQ 对应变体的胜出数据集数", "value": "11/13", "setting": "13 个公开数据集上的离线老虎机评估"}, {"metric": "内存占用", "value": "最高减少至 1/1000", "setting": "与 doubling-retrain XGBoost 及神经网络基线对比，效果相当的前提下"}, {"metric": "奖励/效果", "value": "与 doubling-retrain XGBoost 和神经网络基线相当", "setting": "13 个数据集的离线评估，未披露具体数值与置信区间"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出残差量化（RQ）作为上下文表示层：离线训练的多级码本将连续上下文映射为多级离散质心分配；设计影子机制（shadow mechanism）动态设定各级质心/层级结构，以适配在线分布变化；构建可叠加的加性老虎机算法族，在 RQ 表示之上实现非线性表达且内存严格有界；以有界内存换取与 doubling-retrain XGBoost 及神经网络基线相当的效果

Abstract: Contextual bandits require balancing nonlinear reward modeling with online efficiency. Tree ensembles and neural methods capture nonlinearities but require periodic retraining and large replay buffers. Linear models update efficiently per observation with O(1) memory, but are fundamentally restricted to linear reward structures. We propose Residual Quantization (RQ) as a representation layer to bridge this gap. An offline-trained RQ codebook maps continuous contexts into discrete centroid assignments across multiple levels, set dynamically through a shadow mechanism. This enables a spectrum of additive bandit algorithms that achieve nonlinear expressivity with strictly bounded memory. Across 13 datasets, RQ variants beat their non-RQ counterparts on 11 of 13 datasets, often by wide margins, while matching doubling-retrain XGBoost and neural baselines using up to 1000 times less memory.

</details>


### [40] [Pivot-SD: Efficient Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03665)
*Seo Hyun Kim,Sunwoo Hong,Younwoo Choi,Chen-Hao Chao,Se-Young Yun,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pivot-SD 是一种面向掩码扩散语言模型（dLM）的高效离线自蒸馏后训练框架。它通过信息增益指标识别去噪轨迹中高影响力的“关键承诺”（pivots），对成功轨迹的关键承诺采用交叉熵训练，对失败轨迹的关键承诺采用定向反似然训练，其余部分保持不变。仅用 200 个问题和每个问题 4 条 rollout，Pivot-SD 在数学和代码基准上提升了 LLaDA-8B-Instruct，优于全序列 SFT 和同等预算的扩散 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) have a credit-assignment challenge: a few high-impact commitments during denoising sharply reduce uncertainty over remaining masked positions and shape much of the response, but existing post-training recipes train on final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps instead of these individual pivotal commitments.

Method: Pivot-SD is an offline self-distillation framework for masked diffusion language models (dLMs). It operates on denoising trajectories where a model iteratively unmasks tokens. Given a prompt, the model generates multiple rollouts. For each trajectory, an information-gain metric measures the uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions caused by each unmasking commitment; tokens with high information gain are selected as pivots. Pivots from successful trajectories are supervised with cross-entropy, reinforcing the commitments that led to success. Pivots from failed trajectories are supervised with targeted unlikelihood, suppressing those specific commitments while leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Training uses only 200 questions with four rollouts each, making it data-efficient. The inference path remains standard masked diffusion decoding; Pivot-SD only changes the post-training objective and data selection. This targeted credit assignment addresses the problem that a few early commitments shape most of the response, allowing more efficient learning than full-sequence SFT or whole-step reward assignment.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "improvement over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines", "value": "reported improvement (no exact numbers in abstract)", "setting": "LLaDA-8B-Instruct on math and code benchmarks; 200 questions, four rollouts each"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only high-impact commitments (pivots) in dLM denoising trajectories.；An information-gain metric that selects pivots by measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions.；Asymmetric supervision: pivots from successful trajectories use cross-entropy, while pivots from failed trajectories use targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched.；Demonstrates data efficiency: only 200 questions and four rollouts each suffice to improve LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines on math and code benchmarks.

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) offer a promising parallel alternative to autoregressive models for complex reasoning. However, they face a distinct credit-assignment challenge, since a few commitments during denoising sharply reduce the uncertainty over the remaining masked positions and shape much of the response. Most post-training recipes for dLMs do not use this signal to decide which tokens to train on: they typically train on the final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps, rather than selecting the individual commitments that shape the response. We introduce Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only these high-impact commitments (pivots). Pivot-SD selects pivots using an information-gain metric measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions. Pivots from successful trajectories are trained with cross-entropy, and pivots from failed trajectories with targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Using only 200 questions and four rollouts each, Pivot-SD improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines across math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [41] [Divergence controls entropy in distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03529)
*Nicolas Zucchet,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从熵的视角分析LLM蒸馏中的散度选择：证明前向KL抬高学生熵、反向KL压缩熵，插值散度在收敛时熵变突变，并将散度解释为隐式熵正则化器，在预训练与SFT中定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏目标中的散度选择如何影响学生模型预测分布的熵，以及这种熵效应与数据、条件信息之间的关系。

Method: 论文从熵的角度分析蒸馏目标：蒸馏目标由数据分布与所选散度定义，学生模型通过最小化教师分布与学生分布之间的散度进行训练。前向KL以教师分布为权重对学生分布做期望，作者证明其会抬高学生熵，且交叉熵训练为其特例，由此得到熵恒等关系，并在预训练与监督微调中定量验证。反向KL则相反，以学生分布为权重，会使学生熵收缩，直到学生与教师分布差距过大时收缩停止。两者线性插值的散度在训练早期使熵平滑变化，但在收敛阶段熵变化出现突变。论文进一步区分on-policy蒸馏的低熵来源：不是on-policy采样，而是token级反向KL；在自蒸馏中，条件于特权信息会降低熵，因此最优散度超参是能补偿这种熵收缩的取值。整体为理论推导与实验验证，不涉及具体模型架构、训练硬件或推理部署流程。

Abstract: Distillation has become a core primitive of large language model training, but its properties are not yet well understood. We take an entropic perspective, studying how the entropy of the student depends on the data and the divergence that define the distillation objective. We prove that forward KL inflates the entropy of the student above that of the teacher. Since cross-entropy training is a special case, this yields an identity that we verify quantitatively in pretraining and supervised finetuning. Other divergences come with no such guarantee: reverse KL deflates entropy until the gap between student and teacher gets too large, and interpolating between the two changes entropy smoothly early in training but abruptly at convergence. The lower entropy of on-policy distillation comes from token-level reverse KL, not from on-policy sampling. The divergence therefore acts as an implicit entropy regularizer, whose role is clearest in self-distillation: as conditioning on privileged information deflates entropy, the divergence hyperparameters that work best are those that compensate for it.

</details>

*Xin Wang,Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM Agent 使用稀疏结果奖励训练时，轨迹级目标过于粗糙，无法区分单个决策的贡献；而逐步信用分配估计又因后续动作、环境转移和轨迹长度而不可靠。

Method: AdaStep 针对长时程 LLM 智能体强化学习中的逐步信用分配问题。输入是智能体与环境交互产生的轨迹，包含状态、所选动作、由组内比较得到的局部优势以及轨迹级结果回报。核心模块为一个自适应收缩系数计算模块：它将每个状态下的逐步信用加权问题形式化为潜变量逐步优势的均方误差估计问题，并在显式条件采样假设下推导出最优的逐状态收缩系数。该系数具有信号与总方差比的解释，即当回报变异可归因于所选动作时保留局部信用，而当变异由下游随机性主导时抑制局部信用。最终，该系数用于控制组内导出的局部优势对轨迹级信号的修改强度。训练目标仍是轨迹级策略优化目标，但融入了自适应加权的逐步信用信号。推理/部署时仅需轻量标量计算，不引入 critic、额外 rollout 或额外模型推理。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are typically trained with sparse outcome rewards, making trajectory-level objectives too coarse to distinguish the contribution of individual decisions. Step-level credit assignment provides finer-grained supervision, but its estimates can be unreliable because observed returns also depend on subsequent actions, environment transitions, and trajectory length. We propose AdaStep, an Adaptive Step-credit weighting method that controls how strongly each group-derived local advantage modifies the trajectory-level signal. We formulate this weighting as a mean-squared-error estimation problem for the latent step advantage and, under an explicit conditional sampling assumption, derive an optimal per-state shrinkage coefficient. The coefficient admits a signal-to-total-variance interpretation: it preserves local credit when return variation is attributable to the selected action and suppresses it when variation is dominated by downstream randomness. AdaStep requires only lightweight scalar computation, with no critic, additional rollouts, or extra model inference. Experiments with three model backbones on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show consistent improvements over baselines at low computational cost.

</details>

*Jungseob Lee,Seungyoon Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Hyeonseok Moon,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出合并前崩溃评分与PRISM修复算子，通过任务向量方差预测LLM合并崩溃，并对高干扰层软阈值，在五个破坏性合并中全部修复，且不影响十五个无害合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享基座模型微调后的专家模型通过任务向量平均合并时，部分合并会崩溃且现有合并算子无法在评估前预警，需要预测并修复这种崩溃。

Method: 输入为多个从共享基座模型微调得到的专家模型任务向量（专家参数减去基座参数）。PRISM首先计算专家间任务向量的方差作为干扰度量，该方差等于平均操作移除的功率。基于噪声模型，合并引入的扰动随合并系数和干扰增长，由此得到合并前崩溃评分。合并时，先对任务向量做普通平均，然后对每一层按该层干扰水平设定的阈值进行软阈值操作，抑制高干扰层，保留低干扰层信息。仅当预合并评分超过阈值时应用PRISM，否则保留普通平均。该方法无需额外数据或调参，在四个模型家族二十二个合并配置上验证，五个破坏性合并均被修复。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "全部5个破坏性合并保持高于阈值且与基座模型评估噪声范围内", "value": "PRISM保持全部5个破坏性合并高于阈值", "setting": "四个模型家族、二十二个合并配置中的五个破坏性合并，无数据无调参"}, {"metric": "相比普通平均的改进", "value": "普通平均至少低于阈值14.4分或完全崩溃", "setting": "五个破坏性合并场景"}, {"metric": "预测准确率", "value": "14个预测中12个正确", "setting": "合并前预测，包括持续预训练导致的破坏性结果"}], "online_gains": []}

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned from a shared base can be merged by averaging their task vectors, but some merges collapse far below the base model, and common merge operators give no warning before evaluation. We show that one statistic of the specialists' task vectors both predicts this collapse and calibrates its repair. The power that averaging removes equals the variance of the task vectors across specialists, our measure of interference. Under a working noise model, the disturbance that a merge injects grows with the merge coefficient and with interference, yielding a pre-merge score. In our experiments on twenty-two merge configurations from four model families, only destructive merges exceed a threshold on this score. We find that statistics of sign conflict between specialists, a common target of existing merge operators, are anti-predictive. We then predicted the outcomes of fourteen merges before evaluating them, and twelve predictions were correct, including the destructive outcome of a specialist pair pushed past the threshold by continued pretraining. To address this collapse, we introduce PRISM, an operator that averages the task vectors first and then soft-thresholds each layer at a level set by the layer's interference. Without data or tuning, PRISM keeps all five destructive merges above the threshold within evaluation noise of the base model, where plain averaging falls at least 14.4 points below it or collapses entirely. We apply PRISM only above the threshold and keep the plain average for merges below it, which include all fifteen harmless ones. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>

*Zhengyu Fang,Seoyeon Hong,Jie Yang,Muyang Li,Koyoshi Shindo,Brandon Joseph Lwowski,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Latent-MOPD 提出首个面向 LLM 的表征级多教师在线策略蒸馏方法，同时利用教师输出分布与晚期隐藏状态，并通过共享投影桥接不同隐藏宽度、按域分组更新、监督从隐状态渐进过渡到 token 预测。在同族与跨族教师设置下，学生在数学、代码、逻辑共 9 个基准上均优于单通道与均匀平均基线，多数基准上超越最佳单教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多教师在线策略蒸馏仅通过教师输出分布（token 预测）传递知识，未利用教师内部隐藏状态所携带的表征信息，导致学生难以充分整合多个专家能力。

Method: Latent-MOPD 是一种在线策略蒸馏（OPD）框架：学生模型自主生成响应，多个已训练专家教师对这些响应提供监督。与仅使用教师 token 输出分布的传统多教师 OPD 不同，Latent-MOPD 同时接入教师用于计算预测的隐藏状态作为额外监督通道。其端到端流程为：1) 给定输入提示，学生生成响应序列；2) 将该响应序列分别输入每个教师及学生自身；3) 根据教师-学生关系选择教师的晚期层隐藏状态作为表征监督目标；4) 由于不同教师隐藏宽度不等，使用一个共享投影将各教师隐藏状态映射到统一空间，以便学生接收表征监督；5) 按域对更新进行分组，保证每次更新只包含同一教师域的数据，避免不同教师域在同一更新中交错导致表征-only 监督不稳定/崩溃；6) 监督信号在训练过程中从隐藏状态逐渐过渡到 token 预测，且两个监督通道路由至同一专家（同一教师），保证一致性；7) 学生同时优化输出分布匹配损失与隐藏状态匹配损失。推理时仅使用学生模型，不引入教师或投影模块，因此不增加部署开销。该设计解决的核心问题是：在不重新训练教师的前提下，让学生通过教师的输出与内部表征两条通道整合多个专家能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on the responses it generates. Existing LLM multi-teacher OPD transfers what specialists predict through their output distributions. We introduce Latent-MOPD, to our knowledge the first representation-level multi-teacher OPD method for LLMs. It integrates existing specialists through both their predictions and the hidden states used to compute them, without additional teacher training. To coordinate representation supervision from multiple specialists, we select late-layer targets according to the teacher-student relationship, bridge unequal hidden widths with a shared projection, and group updates by domain. Each teacher's supervision gradually shifts from hidden states to token predictions, with both channels using the same routed specialist. In our main same-family setting, Latent-MOPD outperforms the token-only, representation-only and uniform-averaging baselines on all nine benchmarks across math, code and logic. With the same parameter count as each teacher, the student also surpasses the per-benchmark best teacher on a majority of these benchmarks. With larger, separately developed cross-family teachers, Latent-MOPD outperforms both single-channel baselines on all benchmarks. A same-family all-layer representation-only control remains stable with domain-pure updates but collapses when teacher domains are interleaved within an update. Our results show that a single student can integrate capabilities from several specialists through both their output distributions and internal representations.

</details>

*Songtao Wei,Yi Li,Zhichun Guo,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Inherit-MAS提出在测试时通过工作流继承与执行继承演化LLM多智能体系统：元模型合成工作流，judge诊断缺陷，仅做验证式编辑，并在请求与上下文完全匹配时复用执行结果。在WorkBench与HotpotQA上以GPT-4o-mini和Qwen3-32B取得优于EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS的结果，并显著减少worker-token与总token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在测试时演化LLM多智能体系统的工作流，在利用执行反馈改进不足的同时，避免破坏已有有效组件，并减少重复执行带来的冗余模型与工具调用。

Method: Inherit-MAS由元模型、worker agents、judge与继承机制组成。元模型先合成一个工作流：定义各worker agent的角色、通信输入与工具权限。执行候选工作流后，单独prompted的judge对执行结果打分并诊断缺陷。在普通精化轮次中，工作流继承从最新完成的候选出发，可根据judge判定丢弃可移除且无帮助的节点，并应用一个经过验证的编辑来修复所诊断缺陷，从而避免大范围修改破坏已有有效组件。当新候选执行时，执行继承会检查完整解析后的请求与执行上下文是否完全匹配，仅在完全匹配时复用已存储的合格结果，跳过冗余的模型与工具调用。实验在WorkBench与HotpotQA FullWiki上使用GPT-4o-mini与Qwen3-32B workers评估完成率与联合F1，并对比EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS等演化式MAS基线；同时报告执行继承带来的worker-token与总token节省。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "completion", "value": "55.4%", "setting": "WorkBench，GPT-4o-mini workers，对比EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS"}, {"metric": "joint F1", "value": "49.7%", "setting": "HotpotQA FullWiki，GPT-4o-mini workers，对比EvoAgent、EvoMAS、TacoMAS"}, {"metric": "worker-token usage reduction", "value": "29.1%", "setting": "WorkBench，与关闭执行继承、相同控制器的重跑相比"}, {"metric": "worker-token usage reduction", "value": "34.6%", "setting": "HotpotQA，与关闭执行继承、相同控制器的重跑相比"}, {"metric": "total token usage reduction", "value": "5.3%", "setting": "WorkBench，与关闭执行继承、相同控制器的重跑相比"}, {"metric": "total token usage reduction", "value": "18.1%", "setting": "HotpotQA，与关闭执行继承、相同控制器的重跑相比"}, {"metric": "超过 evolving-MAS baselines", "value": "未给出具体数值", "setting": "WorkBench与HotpotQA FullWiki，Qwen3-32B workers"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将生物演化中的继承与选择显式化：工作流继承与执行继承；元模型合成带角色、通信输入与工具权限的worker agent工作流；独立prompted judge对执行候选打分并诊断缺陷，指导受限的验证式编辑；执行继承仅在所有解析请求与执行上下文完全匹配时复用已有结果，避免冗余模型/工具调用；在普通精化轮次中从最新完成候选出发，可丢弃判定无用的节点，而非全局重写

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from large language models coordinate specialized agents to tackle complex tasks, but effective workflows are difficult to design in advance. Test-time evolution refines workflows using execution feedback, yet broad revisions can disturb useful components, while re-executing unchanged requests can incur redundant computation. Inspired by the interplay of inheritance and selection in biological evolution, we introduce Inherit-MAS, which makes inheritance explicit at the workflow and execution levels. A meta-model first synthesizes a workflow of worker agents with declared roles, communication inputs, and tool permissions, and a separately prompted judge scores each executed candidate and diagnoses its deficiencies. In ordinary refinement rounds, \emph{workflow inheritance} starts from the latest completed candidate, may discard removable nodes judged unhelpful, and applies a validated edit to address the diagnosed deficiency. When the new candidate executes, \emph{execution inheritance} inherits eligible stored results only if the complete resolved request and execution context match, avoiding redundant model and tool calls. With GPT-4o-mini workers, Inherit-MAS achieves 55.4\% completion on WorkBench and 49.7\% joint F1 on HotpotQA FullWiki, outperforming EvoAgent, EvoMAS, and TacoMAS. With Qwen3-32B workers, it also exceeds these evolving-MAS baselines on both benchmarks. Compared with rerunning the same controller with execution inheritance disabled, execution inheritance reduces worker-token usage by 29.1\% on WorkBench and 34.6\% on HotpotQA, and total token usage by 5.3\% and 18.1\%.

</details>


### [46] [Why Does Adaptive Batching Help LLM Pretraining? A Perspective from Unbounded Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02355)
*Arda Fazla,Antesh Upadhyay,Ege C. Kaya,M. Berk Sahin,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从非凸优化中的无界方差视角解释LLM预训练中自适应增大batch size为何有效：提出广义BG-a噪声模型，给出增长相关的oracle复杂度下界与匹配上界，并据此设计自适应批次调度器，在OLMo2 1B模型、C4预训练上以更少迭代取得更低验证损失。
