  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非凸随机优化中，经典有界方差假设在LLM预训练中不成立，需要理解并控制随训练进程增长的梯度噪声方差，从而解释自适应增大batch size为何有效并给出可落地的调度方法。

Method: 论文面向LLM预训练的训练过程而非模型结构本身。输入是OLMo2模型在C4语料上的预训练迭代，核心状态是参数θ与距初始化距离。首先实证测量预训练中的随机梯度方差随训练推进的增长行为，发现方差增长指数不是BG-0假设的固定二次，而是可由可调指数a刻画的更一般形式，据此提出广义BG-a噪声模型，将方差上界表示为与距初始化距离相关的增长项，a=0退化为有界方差，a=2对应BG-0。在L-光滑目标下，论文以该噪声模型推导信息论下界，给出oracle复杂度Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})，并证明随迭代远离初始化而增大batch size可达到匹配的ε依赖上界。基于该分析，设计自适应批次调度器：在训练过程中监测/控制方差增长，动态增大batch size，使每步的噪声与当前参数距离相匹配，从而在相同token预算下兼顾小batch的统计效率与大batch的并行效率。训练流程仍为常规的随机一阶优化循环，只是在batch size上引入调度策略；推理不涉及额外模块，调度仅在预训练阶段生效。最终在OLMo2最高1B参数、C4语料的预训练实验中验证，调度器达到更低的验证损失，且迭代次数不足小batch训练的10%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "验证损失", "value": "低于小batch和大batch训练（相同token预算）", "setting": "OLMo2最高1B参数，C4语料预训练，token预算匹配"}, {"metric": "迭代次数", "value": "少于小batch训练的10%", "setting": "OLMo2最高1B参数，C4语料预训练，与自适应批次调度器对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 实证发现LLM预训练中梯度方差增长更符合带可调增长指数的广义BG模型，而非标准BG-0二次增长；提出广义BG-a噪声模型，在a=0的有界方差与a=2的BG-0噪声之间插值；在L-光滑假设下推导增长相关的oracle复杂度下界Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})，并通过随迭代远离初始化而增大batch size构造匹配的ε依赖上界；提出自适应批次调度器，通过动态调整batch size控制方差增长

Abstract: Increasing the batch size during training is a common practice in large language model (LLM) pretraining, yet the theoretical justification behind its success is not well understood. Analyses of stochastic optimization often assume uniformly bounded stochastic gradient variance, yet recent evidence suggests that this assumption fails in many practical nonconvex problems. The Blum--Gladyshev (BG-$0$) noise model relaxes this assumption by allowing the variance to grow quadratically with the distance from initialization, suggesting that batch size schedulers can help by controlling the variance growth during training. However, this growth can be overly conservative in practice. We empirically investigate variance growth in LLM pretraining and observe that a generalized BG model with a tunable growth exponent provides a tighter description of practical noise behavior. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the generalized BG-$a$ noise model, which interpolates between bounded variance ($a=0$) and BG-$0$ noise ($a=2$). Under $L$-smoothness, we derive an information-theoretic lower bound with growth-dependent oracle complexity $\Omega(\epsilon^{-(4+a)})$ and establish a matching upper bound in $\epsilon$-dependence by increasing the batch size as the iterates move away from initialization. Finally, we propose an adaptive batch scheduler that controls variance growth through dynamic batch size adjustments during training. In pretraining OLMo2 models of up to 1B parameters on C4, our scheduler achieves a lower validation loss than both small and large batch training under matched token budgets, while using less than 10\% of the iterations of small batch training.

</details>


### [47] [Tailoring the Quantization Space for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03027)
*Minsoo Cheong,Donghyun Son,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaSQ 通过查询引导通道加权、跨头归一化和协方差感知通道分组重塑 1-bit KV cache 向量量化的目标空间，在保持常规 VQ 查表结构的同时提升极端压缩下的重建质量，并在 SGLang 上实现最高 14 倍批大小和 1.87 倍峰值吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化方法在 1-bit 极端压缩下 KV cache 重建误差显著增大，码本需覆盖过多通道而难以有效利用容量。

Method: TaSQ 针对长上下文 LLM 推理中 KV cache 的 1-bit 向量量化问题改造量化目标空间，而非改变 VQ 查找结构。输入为缓存中的 key/value 激活及其对应的查询向量，流程包含三部分变换：首先利用查询引导的通道加权，按各通道对注意力输出的误差敏感度分配权重，使码本容量优先用于重要通道；其次进行跨头归一化，缓解不同注意力头激活分布差异导致的码本共享困难；最后执行协方差感知的通道分组，将统计结构相近的通道归入同一码本组，从而在每个码本需覆盖更多通道时仍能有效利用质心。这些变换与 RoPE 兼容，并可离线合并到投影权重和码本中，因此推理时仍沿用常规的向量量化查表结构，几乎不增加服务开销。论文在通用、长链式思维推理与长上下文检索基准上评估，报告在低比特 KV cache VQ 基线上持续更优并保持推理稳定性，同时在单卡 RTX 6000 Ada 上用 SGLang 实现获得更大批大小与更高吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "批大小", "value": "最高 14 倍", "setting": "单张 RTX 6000 Ada GPU，SGLang 实现，对比 BF16 基线"}, {"metric": "峰值吞吐", "value": "1.87 倍", "setting": "单张 RTX 6000 Ada GPU，SGLang 实现，对比 BF16 基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 TaSQ，通过查询引导的通道加权刻画误差敏感度；引入跨头归一化统一不同注意力头的激活统计分布；采用协方差感知的通道分组，使同组内通道统计结构一致；变换与 RoPE 兼容，并可合并进投影权重与码本，保持常规 VQ 查找结构且服务开销可忽略

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache becomes a major memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference, placing substantial pressure on memory capacity and bandwidth. To mitigate this bottleneck, vector quantization (VQ) has emerged as a promising approach for aggressive KV cache compression. However, existing VQ methods degrade substantially in the 1-bit regime. At such extreme compression, each codebook must represent a larger group of channels with a limited set of centroids, making effective use of its capacity increasingly challenging. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{TaSQ}$, which tailors the VQ target space by combining query-guided channel weighting, cross-head normalization, and covariance-aware channel grouping to better reflect the error sensitivity and statistical structure of cached activations. Since these transforms are RoPE-compatible and can be easily merged into projection weights and codebooks, TaSQ preserves the conventional VQ lookup structure and adds negligible serving overhead. Across general, long-chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval benchmarks, TaSQ consistently outperforms existing low-bit KV cache VQ baselines while preserving reasoning stability. On a single RTX 6000 Ada GPU, its SGLang implementation supports up to $14\times$ larger batch sizes and achieves $1.87\times$ higher peak throughput compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [48] [Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02994)
*Changxiu Ji,Amy Lu,Qizheng Zhang,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sentry 是一个运行在 LLM agent 旁的故障管理层：检测 agent 运行中的故障，从外部 playbook 检索匹配的教训来指导恢复，在没有任务奖励的情况下验证是否恢复，并且仅在验证成功后写入新教训；完整 playbook 从不进入 agent 上下文。实验表明，Sentry 在多个 agentic 基准上平均超过最强运行时干预基线 37%，在两者都评估的两个基准上超过最强上下文演化基线 39%，并且学到的教训可以迁移到留出任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning. Existing failure knowledge handling is flawed: keeping all failure lessons in context misfires when the corresponding failure is absent, while runtime interventions only fix failures without learning from them.

Method: Sentry is a failure-management layer that runs alongside an LLM agent. The agent executes a task and may fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning. Sentry monitors the agent's behavior and detects failures. When a failure is detected, Sentry retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook. These conditional lessons are used to guide recovery without exposing the full playbook to the agent's context. After the recovery attempt, Sentry verifies whether the agent recovered, using a verifier that does not have access to task rewards. Only if recovery is verified, Sentry stores a new lesson in the playbook. The full playbook never enters the agent context; only relevant lessons are exposed when a matching failure occurs. This design addresses the conditional nature of failure knowledge: lessons are useful only when their corresponding failure is present, and exposing all lessons can harm performance. The paper also shows that combining Sentry with context evolution yields additional gains and that learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average improvement over strongest runtime-intervention baseline", "value": "37%", "setting": "across multiple agentic benchmarks"}, {"metric": "improvement over strongest context-evolution baseline", "value": "39%", "setting": "on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Frames failure knowledge as conditional knowledge that should be conditionally exposed rather than always present in the agent context.；Introduces Sentry, an external failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent and keeps the full playbook outside the agent context.；Detects failures at runtime and retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery.；Verifies whether the agent recovered without access to task rewards.；Stores a new lesson only if recovery is verified, enabling playbook learning from failures.；Shows that combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains, and that learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks.

Abstract: LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context. Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.

</details>


### [49] [Probe the Harness: Setup Checks for Stale-Data RL Comparisons in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02911)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文展示了陈旧数据LLM强化学习比较中实验框架细节如何导致方法排名逆转，提出PTH检查集揭示四个关键框架缺陷（PPO比率计算、数据种子隔离、回放队列复用、损失归一化差异），并给出在verl和单GPU训练器上的修正结果与检查清单。对LLM后训练RL的公平评估有实践指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型使用陈旧样本（stale samples）进行强化学习训练时，方法之间的比较容易受实验框架（harness）细节影响，导致方法排名逆转；如何识别并纠正这些框架细节以保证比较公平。

Method: 论文围绕语言模型强化学习训练的比较实验展开，涉及两种训练框架：verl（分布式训练框架）与单GPU训练器。核心模块包括：1）样本生成（采样器）与回放队列（replay queue），用于存储陈旧样本；2）策略更新模块，采用PPO风格的比率（PPO ratio），其中SAN为行为无关方法（behaviour-free），TIS为截断重要性采样。数据流：采样器产生样本后进入回放队列，学习者从队列中采样批次进行策略更新；训练目标涉及PPO裁剪目标及重要性修正。在发现的问题中，PPO比率基于学习者自身重算的概率而非行为策略概率；数据种子未传递到TIS臂导致随机性不一致；回放队列在首次填充后连续33次更新复用相同批次；两个损失归一化器的实现与文档描述不符。推理/服务流程未在摘要中披露。论文通过PTH检查逐一暴露并修正这些细节，最终在verl上TIS与SAN性能匹配，在单GPU训练器上TIS稳定学习而SAN保持一定优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出PTH（Probe The Harness）检查集，系统性地使实验框架细节可见；明确识别出四个影响SAN与TIS比较的框架细节：PPO比率使用学习者自身重算概率、数据种子未设到TIS臂、回放队列首次批次重复使用33次更新、两个损失归一化器与描述不符；给出每个细节的特征签名及其对比较结果的影响；提供在采样器滞后（sampler lag）下TIS和未校正GRPO的参考结果；提出PTH检查清单以指导后续陈旧数据RL实验

Abstract: Methods for training language models on stale samples are judged by comparisons against importance-corrected baselines. We show that details of the experimental harness can reverse the observed ranking of methods, and we introduce PTH (Probe The Harness), a set of checks that makes the harness visible. Our case is a comparison between SAN, a behaviour-free method, and truncated importance sampling (TIS) on verl and in a single-GPU trainer, in which SAN first finished ahead in both stacks. Four details of the harness changed this comparison: the PPO ratio was taken against the learner's own recomputed probabilities, the data seed did not reach the TIS arm, the replay queue reused its first batch for 33 updates, and two loss normalisers differed from their description. In each case the logged quantity looked consistent with a working setup, while the quantity that defines the comparison went unchecked. With the harness checked, TIS matches SAN on verl, and in the trainer TIS learns steadily while SAN keeps a margin. We contribute the signature of each detail and its effect on the comparison, reference results for TIS and uncorrected GRPO under sampler lag, and the PTH checklist.

</details>


### [50] [LEAP: Learning Efficient Action Proposals For LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02670)
*Zhen Xu,Qizheng Zhang,Gerry Wan,Shang Zhu,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAP 通过对 LLM Agent 动作推测回合建立延迟框架，并用目标模型动作序列训练 0.6B 小型 drafter，使 agent 端到端 wall clock 时间最多加快 60%，且任务成功率无系统性变化；同时支持无需预收集轨迹的在线训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 逐步推理并执行动作导致 rollout 缓慢，现有动作推测方法使用现成模型面临大模型匹配率高但慢、小模型快但匹配率低的权衡，需要明确决定端到端加速的关键因素并设计高效方案。

Method: LEAP 针对 LLM Agent 逐一执行动作导致的延迟问题，在 agent 的每一步中引入动作推测机制。其架构包含两个角色：小型 drafter 模型（0.6B 参数）和目标模型。工作流程为：给定当前任务状态和历史，drafter 首先快速生成一个候选动作建议（action proposal）；目标模型随后对该动作进行验证或修正；若验证通过则直接执行该动作，若被拒绝则目标模型自行产生正确动作。论文提出一个 latency framework 对该推测回合进行建模：增益取决于 drafter 预测目标动作的一致率以及任务在结束前还能执行的步数，成本来自 draft 推理时间、等待目标模型验证的时间以及工具实际执行的时间。为提升 drafter 准确率，LEAP 在目标模型的动作序列上进行训练，使小型模型能够模仿目标模型的动作决策，从而在保持低 draft 延迟的同时提高动作接受率。论文还展示了 drafter 可以完全在线训练，无需预先收集轨迹，即可与离线训练的性能相匹配，从而适合真实部署。实验在多个数据集、目标模型和 draft 模型上进行，测量端到端 wall clock 加速与任务成功率变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出动作推测回合的延迟框架，将端到端加速分解为增益（drafter 对目标动作的预测准确率、任务剩余步数）与成本（draft 耗时、目标验证等待、工具执行时间）；LEAP 方法：保持 drafter 小型（0.6B）并通过在目标动作序列上训练使其预测准确，从而在低 draft 成本下获得高接受率；支持在线训练 draft 模型，无需预先收集轨迹即可达到离线训练性能，便于实际部署

Abstract: LLM agents are known to be slow in rollouts. An agent completes a task one step at a time. At each step, it reasons and then chooses an action to execute. The next step and action cannot start until the previous one has finished. Speculative decoding accelerates the rollouts at the reason phase by drafting and verifying the inference tokens. Recent works have also started to apply similar ideas at the action phase. These works use off-the-shelf models, usually large, to draft action proposals for target model to verify. Large drafters match the target more often but take longer to propose, while small off-the-shelf models are fast but rarely make the same decision as the target. We ask a more general question: what determines the end-to-end speedup of action speculation? To answer it, we develop a latency framework for the speculative round. The framework compares what a round gains with what it costs. The gain depends on how well the drafter predicts the target and on how many steps the task can take before it ends. The cost comes from drafting, from waiting for target verification and from executing tools. Guided by the framework, we introduce LEAP (Learning Efficient Action Proposals) which keeps the drafter small and makes it accurate by training it on the target actions sequences. With a small 0.6B model, LEAP agrees with the target on most decisions and makes agents up to 60% faster in end-to-end wall clock time, with no systematic change in task success. Across various datasets, target models and draft models, the framework accounts for most of the measured speedups. We also show the draft model can be online trained with no prior trace collection and match the performance of offline training, making LEAP practical to deploy in the real world.

</details>


### [51] [Learning from Evolving Errors: Adaptive Iterative Repair for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02700)
*Rui Li,Liyang He,Zheng Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Linbo Zhu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-OPD 提出面向 on-policy 蒸馏的自适应迭代修复：为失败回答生成修复指导，学生带指导重试，若仍失败则为新错误再生成指导，并以固定教师在该轮错误对齐区域上监督，配合结果感知的阶段加权。在 Qwen3-4B/8B 上于 AIME24/25、HMMT25 平均分较最强基线最高提升 3.6 分，且 OOD 性能保持，但与搜索/广告/推荐系统无直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation 的条件教师模型可访问学生不可见的完整参考解，只告诉目标而不说明如何从当前错误走向目标，带来 solution-conditioned shortcut 风险，反馈与学生的真实错误不对齐。

Method: AIR-OPD 的端到端流程如下：输入为学生策略生成的失败回答。第一步，guidance generator 针对当前错误合成 repair guidance，该指导不是完整参考解，而是从当前错误到修复方向的可操作提示。第二步，学生以该指导为条件重新采样生成 on-policy retry；若重试通过验证则视为修复成功。第三步，若重试仍不正确，生成器针对新观察到的错误产出新的修复指导，进入下一轮迭代，形成自适应、多轮的 error-to-repair 循环。监督阶段：在每一轮中，一个固定的 teacher 接收当前修复指导作为特权上下文（privileged context），并仅在学生最新失败回答的 error-aligned 区域上对学生进行 token 级监督，从而避免传统 OPSD 中因教师可见完整参考解而产生的捷径学习。训练目标上引入 outcome-aware stage weighting：偏重早期修复阶段，并对立即重试即通过验证的阶段赋予更高权重，把最终结果信号回传到各轮修复监督中。指导来源可配置为 self-guidance（由当前学生策略生成）或 external guidance（由更大的教师模型生成）。推理/部署时不需要额外指导生成器，学生模型按常规方式作答，因此该框架主要是训练期增强。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学推理平均分（AIME24/AIME25/HMMT25）", "value": "较最强基线最高提升 3.6 分", "setting": "Qwen3-4B 与 Qwen3-8B，训练于 DAPO-Math-17K"}, {"metric": "OOD 基准性能（MMLU-Pro、GPQA）", "value": "与基座模型持平", "setting": "AIR-OPD 训练后的 Qwen3-4B/8B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: AIR-OPD 自适应迭代修复框架：对失败回答生成修复指导，学生据此进行 on-policy 重试，若仍失败则为新错误生成新指导，形成 error-to-repair 循环；误差对齐监督：固定教师以修复指导作为特权上下文，仅在学生最新失败回答的 error-aligned 区域上监督学生；outcome-aware 阶段加权：偏向早期修复阶段，并对立即重试即通过验证的阶段给予更高权重；两种指导来源对比：来自当前学生策略的 self-guidance 与来自更大模型的外部指导

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [52] [OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02781)
*Xinpeng Wang,Wei Shi,Yu-Chia Chen,Maria Zontak,Yun He,Richard Yuanzhe Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OPD Before RL 两阶段框架：先用 rubric 作为特权教师上下文进行 on-policy 蒸馏，再用 rubric 奖励做 RL，在 HealthBench、ResearchQA、RubricHub Science 上取得最高分并缓解 reward hacking，为开放式任务的 LLM 后训练提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多开放式语言模型任务无法用精确结果验证，rubric-based RL 的奖励只在完整回答后给出，不能直接指出哪些 token 或决策促成了最终得分，训练信号稀疏且容易导致 reward hacking。

Method: 论文提出两阶段训练框架。第一阶段为 rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD)：学生模型在没有 rubric 的情况下，在学生自己生成的前缀上，匹配一个可以访问 rubric 的教师的 next-token 分布。这提供了密集的 token-level 监督，使学生学会在生成过程中隐含使用 rubric 标准，而无需在推理时提供 rubric。第二阶段为 rubric-based RL：直接优化 rubric 奖励，在 RP-OPD 达到的平台期之后继续提升。实验在 HealthBench、ResearchQA、RubricHub Science 上，用 open-weight 模型比较了不同后训练方法，并变化 RL 前 SFT 或 RP-OPD 的训练量。结果表明两阶段框架在评估方法中得分最高，且 RP-OPD + RL 在 RubricHub Science 上 reward hacking 迹象有限，而 SFT + RL 基线会通过声称符合 rubric 但缺少实质内容来获取高奖励。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "综合评分", "value": "所评估方法中最高", "setting": "HealthBench, ResearchQA, RubricHub Science 三个基准，使用 open-weight 模型，比较不同 post-training 方法并改变 RL 前 SFT 或 RP-OPD 的训练量"}, {"metric": "reward hacking 程度", "value": "RP-OPD + RL 仅有有限迹象，SFT + RL 基线 increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content", "setting": "RubricHub Science"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD)：第一阶段让无 rubric 的学生模型在学生生成的前缀上匹配 rubric-aware 教师的 next-token 分布，提供密集 token-level 监督。；两阶段训练框架：先用 rubric 作为特权教师上下文进行 on-policy 蒸馏，再把 rubric 作为奖励进行 RL，突破蒸馏平台期。；发现 RP-OPD + RL 在 RubricHub Science 上比 SFT + RL 更少出现 reward hacking，后者会通过声称符合 rubric 而获得高奖励但不提供所需内容。

Abstract: Many useful language-model tasks cannot be evaluated by exact outcome verification. Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) addresses this issue by scoring open-ended responses against explicit criteria. However, because the reward is assigned after the complete response, the training signal does not directly identify which individual decisions contributed to the final score. We propose a two-stage training framework that uses rubrics first as privileged teacher context for dense token-level supervision, then as rewards for further RL. In the first stage, rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD), a student without access to the rubric matches a rubric-aware teacher's next-token distributions at student-generated prefixes. In the second stage, RL directly optimizes the rubric reward and improves beyond the observed distillation plateau. We evaluate the framework on health and science tasks using open-weight models. Across HealthBench, ResearchQA, and RubricHub Science, we compare post-training methods and vary the amount of SFT or RP-OPD training before RL, finding that our two-stage framework achieves the highest scores among the methods evaluated. RP-OPD + RL shows limited signs of reward hacking on RubricHub Science, whereas the SFT + RL baseline increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content. These findings support using rubrics to guide on-policy distillation before applying rubric-based RL.

</details>


### [53] [Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324)
*Xiaofei Yin,Tong Chu,Jiyuan Fu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出SF-MOPD，通过耦合快速学生模型与慢速EMA模型，并在log概率空间去除导致快速模型偏离慢速参考的教师更新分量，解决多教师在线策略蒸馏中的通用能力退化问题。实验表明该方法在多个模型规模上有效缓解能力干扰，一致优于原始MOPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）训练会逐渐将学生模型推离初始化模型，导致通用能力随偏移增大而下降，产生能力干扰；直接约束学生靠近初始化又会抑制领域专业能力的获取。

Method: SF-MOPD框架包含一个快速模型（当前学生）和一个慢速模型。快速模型直接由每个教师模型通过在线策略蒸馏更新，直接学习各领域教师的专长。慢速模型是学生参数的指数移动平均（EMA），逐步吸收学习信号，充当动态的能力参考，融合通用基础模型与已确认的领域专长。对每个教师，算法首先在log概率空间中计算教师诱导的参数更新方向，然后将其分解为与“快速模型背离慢速模型方向”对齐的分量、正交分量等，仅移除将快速模型进一步推离慢速模型的分量，保留对齐和正交分量，从而在获取领域专长的同时防止通用能力流失。训练目标是在多教师蒸馏损失基础上加入这种方向性过滤机制。推理时使用最终的学生模型（具体是否使用慢速模型未在摘要中说明），整体流程为多教师在线策略蒸馏。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出慢-快多教师在线策略蒸馏（SF-MOPD），耦合快速模型（直接被各教师更新的当前学生）和慢速模型（学生的指数移动平均），慢速模型作为移动能力参考融合通用基础与已确认领域专长。；对每个教师计算教师在log概率空间中的诱导更新，仅移除将快速模型推离慢速模型的分量，保留对齐和正交分量。；在多个模型规模上验证SF-MOPD有效缓解能力干扰、增强专业多模态能力并降低通用能力基准平均退化，一致优于原始MOPD。

Abstract: Foundation multimodal large language models are designed to support a broad spectrum of capabilities across diverse domains. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) provides an effective framework for consolidating domain-specific expertise into a single student model. However, MOPD training gradually drives the student away from its initialization model, and general capabilities decline as the displacement grows, resulting in capability interference. A direct remedy is constraining the student toward its initialization, but this suppresses the acquisition of domain expertise as well. We propose Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD), which couples a fast model, the current student updated directly by each teacher, with a slow model, an exponential moving average of the student. The slow model absorbs the learning signal gradually, serving as a moving capability reference that fuses the general foundation with confirmed domain expertise. For each teacher, SF-MOPD computes the teacher-induced update in log-probability space and removes only the component that pushes the fast model further away from the slow model, while retaining aligned and orthogonal components. Experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that SF-MOPD effectively mitigates capability interference, enhances specialized multimodal capabilities, and reduces the average degradation on general-capability benchmarks, consistently outperforming vanilla MOPD.

</details>


### [54] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RASS，一种用于扩散模型投机采样树验证的秩感知列表耦合方法，利用草稿候选排序提升验证效率。在CIFAR-10等任务上以匹配计算预算将目标模型评估次数相比D-GRS降低约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在扩散模型的投机采样中，利用草稿树节点候选的排序信息，在不增加目标模型评估的情况下提升验证效率，从而减少目标模型调用次数。

Method: RASS作用于扩散模型的投机采样框架：草稿模型在树结构上生成多个候选状态（每个节点生成K个条件独立候选），然后在验证阶段，RASS不按生成顺序顺序测试候选，而是根据提案分布与目标分布之间的均值位移对候选进行排序，并采样一个秩权重。秩权重通过最小化所选提案与目标律间的全变差进行优化。最终选择的候选与目标分布进行最大耦合，并通过残差修正步骤确保采样精确性，从而在任意秩权重下保持目标分布。该验证过程替代了D-GRS的顺序拒绝采样，在相同计算预算下相当于更高效地利用并行草稿候选。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "目标模型评估次数比值（标准采样/投机采样）", "value": "在CIFAR-10上匹配计算预算下相比D-GRS提升约20%", "setting": "条件生成CIFAR-10"}, {"metric": "验证效率", "value": "在评估的设置（高斯混合目标、FFHQ无条件像素生成、CIFAR-10条件生成、Stable Diffusion 3.5潜在扩散）中均优于D-GRS", "setting": "多种生成任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS)验证规则，基于秩感知列表耦合；根据提案-目标均值位移对草稿候选排序，并采样秩权重以最小化所选提案与目标律之间的全变差；将所选候选与目标最大耦合，并通过残差修正保证任意秩权重下的精确采样

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [55] [Capability Scaling-Down Laws for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02462)
*Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Liangjie Hong,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统研究LLM压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏）的能力下降缩放律，提出可预测能力损失与配置选择的紧凑关系，并在Pythia、OLMo-2及Wanda剪枝上验证，可减少测量成本并辅助压缩方法与配置选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩导致的等量资源缩减会带来不同的能力损失，如何预测并选择最优压缩方法与配置，而非依赖经验试错

Method: 论文并未提出新的模型架构，而是建立一个面向LLM压缩的能力下降缩放律分析框架。输入为不同压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏）及其配置，作用于不同规模模型、不同训练阶段与不同数据可用性/训练曝光条件下。框架在数学、代码生成、问答三类任务上测量能力损失（以nats per token等指标），将损失与模型规模、训练阶段、压缩设置、数据量与训练曝光建立预测关系；通过跨剪枝级别共享密度响应减少配置测量量，并在新Pythia状态、预注册OLMo-2测试状态与Wanda剪枝下评估紧凑关系相对全量拟合回归的误差；蒸馏部分为受控实验，观察数据复用代价在不同问答分布上的复现及净收益对评估分布的依赖；最后以数值选择对比配置中位数与固定方法优先级的决策价值（regret）来验证预测的实用性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测误差（相对全量测量回归拟合）", "value": "0.020 nats per token 以内", "setting": "在新Pythia状态、预注册OLMo-2测试状态及Wanda剪枝下，对数学与代码任务，每个设置系数重新拟合"}, {"metric": "配置测量量", "value": "减半", "setting": "跨剪枝级别共享密度响应以拟合剪枝预测器"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 跨剪枝、量化、蒸馏的统一能力下降缩放律框架，测量数学、代码生成与问答的能力损失；发现跨剪枝级别共享密度响应可将拟合剪枝预测器所需的配置测量量减半；在Pythia、预注册OLMo-2与Wanda剪枝下验证紧凑预测关系，与全量回归拟合误差在0.020 nats/token以内；通过受控蒸馏实验揭示重度数据复用的代价在问答分布间复现，净收益取决于评估分布；以配置中位数和固定方法优先级为基线评估预测的决策价值（regret）

Abstract: LLM compression reduces inference costs and memory requirements, but selecting a method and configuration remains largely empirical because comparable resource reductions can produce different capability losses. We systematically investigate capability scaling-down laws for LLM compression across pruning, quantization, and distillation. Our framework measures capability loss in mathematics, code generation, and question answering, and relates these measurements to model size, training stage, compression settings, data availability, and training exposure. We develop simple predictive relations and evaluate their accuracy, measurement efficiency, and generalization to unseen configurations and model states. Sharing the density response across pruning levels halves the configuration measurements needed to fit a pruning predictor: on new Pythia states, on pre-registered OLMo-2 test states and under Wanda pruning, the compact relation matches a regression fitted with all measurements on math and code to within 0.020 nats per token, with coefficients refitted for each setting. Controlled distillation experiments show that the cost of heavy data reuse recurs across question-answering distributions, while the net benefit depends on the evaluation distribution. We further evaluate the decision value of these predictions by comparing numerical selection with configuration medians and fixed method priorities. Independent evaluations across two model families show that selection captures most of the available cross-method benefit for question answering within the tested candidate sets, where a fixed method priority attains the same regret, with smaller opportunities for mathematics and code. These results clarify the predictive scope of capability scaling-down laws and their use in compression method selection. Our code is publicly available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [56] [Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02359)
*Doseok Jang,Jon Ander Campos,Youran Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 LMOPD，一种字典序多目标在线策略蒸馏方法，通过显式优先级选择专家并投影对数策略修正，在 30B-A3B MoE 上验证多专家融合时能更好地保护高优先级能力，相比基线保留约 90% 的准确率与推理增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多奖励后训练方法通常将多个奖励标量化或简单组合专家模型，无法显式保护奖励之间的优先级顺序，导致在非对称权衡下（如简洁性不应以正确性为代价）无法保证高优先级能力不退化。

Method: LMOPD 采用多教师在线策略蒸馏框架。输入为学生模型的 rollout 轨迹（如数学解题步骤）。系统维护一组奖励专门化教师策略（专家），每个专家针对一个特定目标（如答案正确性、推理质量、简洁性）进行优化。对于每个学生 rollout，门控机制按优先级顺序检查各目标是否存在缺陷（deficiency），选择第一个检测到缺陷的目标对应的专家。然后，该专家的中心化对数策略修正（centered log-policy correction）被局部投影，以移除与更高优先级专家相冲突的梯度或更新方向；投影后的修正用于更新学生策略。训练目标是在线策略蒸馏损失，即学生模仿经过投影的专家修正。推理时，学生模型独立运行，无需教师或门控。该方法通过显式字典序优先级保护高优先级能力，避免因优化低优先级目标（如简洁性）而损害高优先级目标（如正确性）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率与推理质量增益保留率", "value": "完全保留（二专家设置）", "setting": "30B-A3B MoE 模型，两个专家，三个数学基准测试"}, {"metric": "简洁性增益获取率", "value": "46.9%", "setting": "30B-A3B MoE 模型，两个专家，三个数学基准测试"}, {"metric": "准确率增益保留率", "value": "约 90%（四专家设置）", "setting": "30B-A3B MoE 模型，四个专家，三个数学基准测试"}, {"metric": "推理正确性增益保留率", "value": "约 90%（四专家设置）", "setting": "30B-A3B MoE 模型，四个专家，三个数学基准测试"}, {"metric": "次优基线的准确率与推理正确性增益保留率", "value": "约 57%", "setting": "四专家设置，与 LMOPD 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出字典序多目标在线策略蒸馏（LMOPD），在多教师设置中按显式优先级顺序整合专门化策略。；对于每个学生 rollout，根据门控（gate）检测到缺口的第一个目标选择对应的专家模型进行蒸馏。；对选中专家的中心化对数策略修正进行局部投影，去除与更高优先级专家相冲突的成分。；在 30B-A3B MoE 模型上进行二专家与四专家实验，验证优先级保护效果优于随机路由及标量化基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually optimizes answer correctness, yet useful language-model behavior also requires high-quality reasoning and concise responses. Existing multi-reward post-training methods typically scalarize rewards or combine specialists without explicitly protecting a reward priority order. This is problematic when trade-offs are asymmetric: conciseness, for example, should not improve at the cost of correctness. We introduce Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD), a multi-teacher method for integrating reward-specialized policies under explicit priorities. For each student rollout, LMOPD selects the specialist for the first objective whose gate detects a deficiency, then locally projects its centered log-policy correction to remove components that oppose higher-priority specialists. We evaluate 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts transformer models in two- and four-expert settings on three math benchmarks, measuring retained specialist gains. With two experts, LMOPD's point estimates fully retain the accuracy and reasoning-quality gains while acquiring $46.9\%$ of the conciseness gain. With four experts, it retains $\approx90\%$ of both the accuracy gain and reasoning-correctness gain, compared to only $\approx57\%$ by the next best evaluated baseline. Matched four-expertablations show that lexicographic routing outperforms random routing and that projection further strengthens both top-priority capabilities. Across both scales, LMOPD preserves the highest-priority capabilities more effectively than the existing baselines we evaluate, demonstrating the value of explicit priorities for specialist integration.

</details>


### [57] [Trained Agentic Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02404)
*Bryce Sandlund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文训练Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型使用一个极简代理式harness（自调用与令牌范围读取工具），在仅8K上下文下达到GPT-5.4（1M上下文）在OOLONG-synth长文档任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何让语言模型在有限上下文窗口（如8K token）下有效处理远超窗口的长文档，而不需要原生训练长上下文或设计复杂的外部harness。

Method: 论文提出的端到端架构基于一个极简的代理式harness，包含两个工具：1) 自调用工具，允许模型以任意指定的提示调用自身；2) 令牌读取工具，允许模型从输入上下文中读取指定范围的令牌。模型在多样合成数据集上进行微调，训练目标是通过调用这些工具来管理上下文，从而在有限的上下文窗口内处理长文档。推理时，模型根据当前任务动态决定何时调用自身或读取上下文片段，逐步构建所需信息。论文未披露更多关于数据流、模块交互、训练目标或推理流程的细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在OOLONG-synth基准上的性能", "value": "文档长度超过40K token时，使用仅8,000 token上下文的微调小模型与使用1M token上下文的GPT-5.4表现相当", "setting": "OOLONG-synth基准，长文档（>40K token）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出最简单的代理式harness：一个自调用工具（以任意提示调用自身）和一个按范围读取输入上下文的令牌读取工具。；在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型上，使用该harness在多样合成数据集上进行微调，训练模型执行上下文管理。

Abstract: We study long context language models. Instead of training long context natively, or designing a long context harness, we train a model over the simplest possible harness: a tool to call itself with any specified prompt and a tool to read tokens in a range from the input context. We finetune Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on a diverse synthetic dataset using this harness. With only 8,000 tokens of context, our small model is as strong as GPT-5.4 with 1M tokens of context on the OOLONG-synth benchmark when document length exceeds 40K tokens.

</details>


### [58] [Hesitation Has a Geometry: Entropy-Trained Hyperbolic Probes for Sparse Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02391)
*Zeyong Zhang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Tengfei Ma,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEST 观察到 LLM 数学推理本质上是层级树状结构、只在少数高 next-token 熵 token 处分支，于是用模型自身熵作为唯一标签训练双曲 Poincaré 球探针，仅在高熵位置沿 Busemann 读出最速下降方向稀疏编辑隐状态。在 Qwen2.5-Math 与 Llama-3.1 三个模型、MATH-500 与 GSM8K 上，六个设置中五个准确率提升最多 1.8 点，而逐 token 对比式 steering 反而降分；增益集中在模型频繁犹豫的问题上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活干预（activation steering）在每个 token 上都以固定的欧氏向量编辑隐状态，忽略了推理过程在少数高熵分支 token 处才呈层级/树状结构的事实，导致在已由上下文确定的 token 上做无谓干预并损害准确率。

Method: HEST 的输入是预训练/指令微调 LLM 的逐 token 隐状态。首先训练一个轻量探针，将隐状态嵌入 Poincaré 双曲球；探针的唯一监督标签是模型自身的 next-token 熵，因此无需额外人工标注。推理时，逐 token 计算该熵：当熵超过给定阈值（即模型处于推理分支/犹豫点）时，在双曲嵌入空间中沿探针读出函数的最速下降测地线方向移动嵌入状态，然后再映射回原始欧氏隐状态，从而对原模型做稀疏激活干预；熵低于阈值的 token 不做任何编辑。读出函数采用学习到的理想点的 Busemann 函数，论文证明对固定步长，该读出在每个状态上的下降量相同，保证跨状态干预的一致性。该方法作为推理期 wrapper 作用于冻结的预训练模型，无需重训练底层 LLM。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "贪心解码准确率", "value": "在六个设置中的五个提升，最高提升 1.8 个百分点", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Math 与 Llama-3.1 家族三个指令微调模型，MATH-500 与 GSM8K 基准；其中 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct 上改用欧氏探针后增益消失"}, {"metric": "其余问题准确率", "value": "几乎不变", "setting": "模型犹豫（高熵）较少的问题上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用 Poincaré 球双曲空间嵌入隐状态，以模型自身 next-token 熵为唯一监督信号训练轻量探针，无需人工标注。；仅当熵超过阈值时，沿探针读出函数最速下降的测地线方向移动嵌入状态，再映射回隐状态，实现稀疏、按需的激活干预。；对学习到的理想点（ideal point）的 Busemann 读出证明：固定步长在各状态下对该读数的降低量相同，提供几何上的步长一致性。；与逐 token 施加对比式 steering 向量的基线对比，显示后者反而降低准确率，验证稀疏干预的必要性。

Abstract: When a large language model solves a mathematical problem, its reasoning is largely hierarchical, and the solution often branches at a few tokens where the next-token entropy is high. Such tree-like structure embeds in hyperbolic space with far lower distortion than in Euclidean space. Activation steering, however, usually edits the hidden states of a pretrained model by adding one fixed Euclidean vector at every token, even though most tokens of a solution are already determined by the context. We propose Hyperbolic Entropy Steering (HEST), which embeds the hidden states in the Poincaré ball with a lightweight probe whose only label is the model's own next-token entropy. Where this entropy exceeds a threshold, HEST moves the embedded state along the geodesic of steepest descent of a readout of the probe and maps the change back to the hidden state. For the Busemann readout of a learned ideal point, we prove that a step of fixed length lowers it by the same amount at every state. On three instruction-tuned models from the Qwen2.5-Math and Llama-3.1 families, HEST with the Busemann readout improves greedy accuracy on MATH-500 and GSM8K in five of six settings, by up to 1.8 points, whereas a contrastive steering vector added at every token lowers accuracy. With a Euclidean probe trained in the same way, this gain disappears on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct. The gains are largest on problems where the model hesitates often, and accuracy on the remaining problems is almost unchanged.

</details>

### [59] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MRVQ is a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings that supports flexible truncation in both residual stages and embedding dimensions, enabling a single index to serve multiple memory/rate settings. It achieves 17.8-22.0x memory savings over separately trained QINCo2 indices on FiQA and NFCorpus, with a slight nDCG@10 drop. It also presents a fast-building PCA-scalar design. The work includes negative results and is positioned as a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enabling a single resident vector index that can serve multiple (dimension, rate) configurations, thereby reducing memory footprint and simplifying deployment for elastic dense-retrieval services.

Method: MRVQ is a post-hoc residual quantizer applied to frozen embeddings. The maximum-rate code consists of multiple residual stages, each quantizing the residual from the previous stage. Truncating by dropping residual stages reduces the bit rate, while dropping embedding coordinates reduces the dimension. This two-way truncation allows one resident index artifact to serve any (dimension, rate) pair. The quantizer is trained once at the maximum rate and then used with truncation without retraining. The paper also evaluates a PCA-scalar design that uses PCA to reduce dimensionality, followed by scalar quantization. The exact module interactions and training objectives are not fully detailed in the abstract.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "memory usage", "value": "17.8-22.0x less than three separately trained QINCo2 indices", "setting": "FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, {4,8,16}-byte codes"}, {"metric": "memory usage", "value": "1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman", "setting": "FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, {4,8,16}-byte codes"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.026-0.107 lower than per-rate QINCo2 on FiQA", "setting": "FiQA, comparison to per-rate QINCo2"}, {"metric": "quality", "value": "beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size", "setting": "FiQA and NFCorpus, matched code size"}, {"metric": "build time", "value": "420x faster at the median than RaBitQ and its extension", "setting": "PCA-scalar design, comparable quality"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ): a post-hoc residual quantizer that allows truncating both residual stages (rate) and embedding coordinates (dimension) from a single maximum-rate code.；A PCA-scalar quantizer design that achieves quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median.；Extensive evaluation across four embedding families and multiple code sizes, including negative results (QINCo2 collapse at high rates, failed ranking-bound hypothesis).

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [60] [What Does a Token Cost? A Mixture-of-Agents Measurement of Sufficient Per-Token Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02491)
*Zhixu Du,Weijia Han,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过Mixture-of-Agents测量每个token的充分计算量，发现最昂贵10%的token占据64-80%的FLOPs，并将该难度图用于模型路由和投机解码，取得投影延迟降低和draft token减少的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型对每个生成token消耗相同计算量，而许多token实际所需计算远低于此；此前缺乏对单个token所需计算量的直接测量。

Method: 论文将token级计算量测量建模为一个Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）框架：一个由15个不同容量、来自三个模型家族（Qwen、OLMo、R1-distilled）的agent组成的面板，每个agent按token逐次尝试在给定正确前序token的条件下复现参考序列。对于每个token，能够成功复现的最小agent的推理开销被定义为该token的充分计算量上限。该框架不涉及训练，而是利用现成模型进行推理测量，产出一个token难度图。该图随后被应用于两个下游场景：一是模型路由，利用token难度图指导将不同token分配给不同容量的模型，在MATH-500上相比最佳置信度路由基线降低投影延迟并略微提升准确率；二是投机解码，利用该图自适应分配draft窗口，相比固定窗口减少draft token数并降低投影延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "token复现比例", "value": "92-95%", "setting": "0.5B agent在三个核心基准上"}, {"metric": "最高成本10% token占估算FLOPs比例", "value": "64-80%", "setting": "Qwen、OLMo、R1-distilled面板"}, {"metric": "投影延迟", "value": "从7.59秒降至5.12秒", "setting": "全部500道MATH-500问题，MoA-derived map辅助模型路由，相对最佳置信度路由基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "略优于最佳置信度路由基线", "setting": "全部500道MATH-500问题，MoA-derived map辅助模型路由"}, {"metric": "draft token数量", "value": "减少32.6%", "setting": "MoA-map辅助drafting，对比固定窗口drafting，相似准确率"}, {"metric": "投影延迟", "value": "降低约20%", "setting": "MoA-map辅助drafting，对比固定窗口drafting，相似准确率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出通过Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）测量每个token的充分计算量（sufficient compute），定义为能成功复现参考token的最小agent的推理开销；构建由15个不同容量、来自3个家族的模型组成的agent面板，逐token复现参考序列；基于MoA导出的token难度图，应用于模型路由和投机解码的draft window分配

Abstract: Large language models spend the same amount of computation on every token they generate, regardless of how difficult each token is to produce. Methods such as speculative decoding and model routing are built on the premise that much of this computation is unnecessary, yet the computation an individual token actually requires has not been measured. We measure it through a Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) lens: a panel of fifteen language models of increasing capacity, drawn from three families, in which every agent attempts to reproduce a reference sequence token by token, conditioned on the correct preceding tokens. We define the inference cost of the smallest agent that succeeds as the token's sufficient compute, which upper-bounds what the token requires. On three core benchmarks, a 0.5B agent reproduces 92--95\% of reference tokens. Across Qwen, OLMo, and R1-distilled panels, the most expensive 10\% account for 64--80\% of estimated FLOPs. On all 500 MATH-500 problems, the MoA-derived map helps model routing reduce projected latency from 7.59 to 5.12 seconds while slightly improving accuracy, relative to the best confidence-routing baseline. The MoA-map helps drafting use 32.6\% fewer draft tokens and approximately 20\% lower projected latency than fixed-window drafting at similar accuracy. These comparisons reveal remaining allocation headroom, motivating controllers that exploit sufficient-compute structure.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Trajectories for Complex Tasks through Recursive Self-Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02826)
*Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Chengsong Huang,Fuxiao Liu,Haitao Mi,Jordan Boyd-Graber,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSR 用同一基座模型在多种 harness 下发现成功轨迹，经 Planner 抽取 runbook、Critic 筛查泄漏并递归修订、Executor 在全新沙盒执行，将 2,001 条源轨迹扩写为 11,094 条通用 harness 下的 SFT 轨迹。训练后在 Terminal-Bench 2 上 pass@3 从 57.0% 提升到 74.2%，在多项终端基准上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在难以完成的任务中，不同执行框架（harness）产生的成功轨迹因框架差异难以直接作为通用框架下的监督数据；如何在统一通用框架下重建并利用这些多样化经验来提升模型能力。

Method: RSR 使用单一基座模型 Qwen-3.8-27B 完成整条流程。首先在多种 harness 下收集任务的成功解，形成源轨迹池（约3K终端任务，3种harness联合解决759个任务）。随后进入重建阶段：Planner 从成功源轨迹中抽取任务解决流程，编写成结构化的 runbook；Critic 对 runbook 进行筛选，检测是否存在 verifier leakage 或 solution leakage，并引导递归修订，直到 runbook 合格；Executor 在全新的 sandbox 中按照合格 runbook 执行任务，生成在通用 harness 下的重写轨迹。最终将 2,001 条成功源轨迹扩展为 11,094 条重写轨迹，用于对模型进行 supervised finetuning。训练目标为标准的 SFT 损失，使基座模型在通用 harness 下具备复用这些多样化经验的能力。推理时模型仅使用通用 harness，无需专用 harness 的干预。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass@3", "value": "57.0% -> 74.2%", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 2，与 base model 对比"}, {"metric": "pass@3", "value": "1.5% -> 9.1%", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 4，与 base model 对比"}, {"metric": "pass@3", "value": "39.0% -> 63.0%", "setting": "自建 Terminal-Bench Hard，与 base model 对比"}, {"metric": "pass@3", "value": "3.0% -> 6.0%", "setting": "自建 Software Terminal-Bench，与 base model 对比"}, {"metric": "process reward", "value": "0.21 -> 0.29", "setting": "Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench"}, {"metric": "任务解决数", "value": "759 tasks（三种 harness 联合解决）", "setting": "自建约 3K 终端任务池，比最强单一 harness 多 34.3%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR) 框架：用同一个基座模型（Qwen-3.8-27B）在多种 harness 下发现成功解，再重建为通用 harness 下的训练轨迹；Planner 抽取过程并生成 runbook，Critic 检测 verifier/solution leakage 并引导递归修订，Executor 在干净 sandbox 中执行合格 runbook 的三角色协作架构；将 2,001 条成功源轨迹扩展为 11,094 条重写轨迹用于 SFT，并证明其优于直接轨迹 SFT

Abstract: Successful trajectories on difficult tasks provide valuable supervision for model improvement, but specialized harnesses introduce interventions that may be unavailable during deployment. We propose Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR), a framework that uses one base model, Qwen-3.8-27B, to discover successful solutions under diverse harnesses and reconstruct them as training trajectories under a general harness. A planner extracts procedures into runbooks, a critic screens for verifier and solution leakage and guides recursive revision, and an executor follows qualified runbooks in fresh sandboxes. Across approximately 3K self-curated terminal tasks, three harnesses jointly solve 759 tasks, 34.3% more than the strongest individual harness in the recorded pool. RSR expands 2,001 successful source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten trajectories for supervised finetuning. Training on these trajectories outperforms both the base model and direct trajectory SFT. Compared with the base model, pass@3 increases from 57.0% to 74.2% on Terminal-Bench 2, from 1.5% to 9.1% on Terminal-Bench 4, from 39.0% to 63.0% on our self-curated Terminal-Bench Hard, and from 3.0% to 6.0% on our Software Terminal-Bench. Process reward on Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench rises from 0.21 to 0.29. These results show how diverse harness-assisted experiences can be reconstructed into reusable capabilities for a model operating under a general harness.

</details>


### [62] [iS-KV: Online Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Block-Incremental SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02815)
*Yiren Zhao,Guanghui Song,Tianrui Qin,Kejiang Ye,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iS-KV 提出在线低秩 KV Cache 压缩方法，通过保留近期精确窗口、对旧状态做块增量 SVD 压缩并同步历史坐标，缓解长链式思维推理中的显存增长；在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 上以 4-5.6 倍压缩保持接近原始模型的准确率，且优于 token eviction 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维推理中 KV Cache 随解码长度线性增长导致显存瓶颈；现有 token eviction 不可逆地删除历史状态，可能丢失后续推理所需信息；SVD 低秩压缩从固定 prompt cache 扩展到在线解码时，直接在旧基上更新基会导致历史表示漂移。

Method: iS-KV 针对自回归解码中的 KV Cache 进行在线低秩压缩。输入为已生成 token 的 key/value 状态序列；解码过程中新 token 不断加入，KV Cache 线性增长。方法维护一个近期 token 的精确窗口，保证最近上下文无损；当 token 移出窗口后，将其状态折叠进有界秩的低秩表示中。低秩表示由一组基向量和对应的历史坐标组成，基向量通过块增量 SVD 随新数据更新。关键设计在于：当低秩基发生变化时，同步更新所有历史 token 在该基下的坐标，使得历史表示在新基下保持一致，避免旧坐标配新基造成的表示漂移。推理时，近期窗口直接使用精确 KV；更早的历史通过低秩重建获得压缩后的 KV 表示，从而在固定显存预算下保留全部位置信息。训练目标与具体优化过程未在摘要中披露；评测在 8B 模型上进行，对比 token eviction 基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "82.6%", "setting": "DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B，4.06 倍持久 KV 压缩"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "89.2%", "setting": "Qwen3-8B，5.64 倍持久 KV 压缩"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在线低秩 KV Cache 压缩框架：保留近期窗口精确，将较旧状态增量折叠为有界秩表示。；块增量 SVD：随新 token 到来增量更新低秩基，并对历史坐标进行同步更新以保持表示一致性，避免基更新导致的历史漂移。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning substantially increases KV-cache memory during autoregressive decoding, as every generated token introduces new key and value states and causes the cache to grow linearly with decoding length. Existing KV-cache compression methods typically control this growth through token eviction, but irreversible deletion can remove historical states that later reasoning may need to revisit. SVD-based low-rank compression provides an alternative by retaining all positions with a more compact representation. However, extending it from a fixed prompt cache to online decoding is non-trivial. Through our investigation, we find that if the basis is updated for new tokens while old tokens keep their coordinates in the old basis, the stored history drifts substantially. Based on this observation, we propose iS-KV, an online low-rank KV-cache compression method for long-horizon reasoning. iS-KV keeps a recent window exact while incrementally folding older states into bounded-rank representations. As the low-rank basis evolves, it synchronizes historical coordinates with the updated basis to maintain representation consistency. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, iS-KV achieves 82.6% accuracy at 4.06-fold persistent-KV compression, close to the original model's 83.6%. On Qwen3-8B, it achieves 89.2% accuracy at 5.64-fold compression. Under matched memory budgets, iS-KV consistently outperforms token-eviction baselines.

</details>


### [63] [BitNest: Bit-Nested Speculative Decoding for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02800)
*Chence Yang,Ningxi Cheng,Arash Akbari,Qitao Tan,Qingchan Zhu,Ci Zhang,Changdi Yang,Yanzhi Wang,Wei Niu,Jinhui Wang,Jin Lu,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitNest提出位嵌套推测解码，将低精度草稿嵌入高精度目标权重中，共享单一存储，并将渐进精度设计扩展到KV缓存，在7B-8B边缘LLM上实现平均95.2%接受率和1.48-1.61倍端到端加速，显著降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法需要额外草稿模型或权重表示，导致内存开销大，在资源受限设备上难以部署；自推测方法则面临草稿质量、目标质量和存储效率之间的权衡。

Method: BitNest是一个位嵌套推测解码框架。输入为token序列，目标模型采用高精度（如FP16）权重表示。关键设计是将低精度草稿模型直接嵌入高精度目标权重中：首先构建一个低精度基座模型（草稿），然后通过残差细化恢复出高精度目标模型，两者共享同一份物理权重存储，从而无需额外存储草稿模型权重。推理时，低精度草稿快速生成候选token序列，高精度目标模型并行验证，实现推测解码加速。该渐进式精度设计进一步扩展到KV缓存，对长上下文推理中的KV缓存也采用低精度存储加残差恢复，减少内存占用。训练目标为同时优化草稿质量和目标模型质量，确保高精度模型质量基本无损。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推测接受率", "value": "平均 95.2%", "setting": "多个 7B-8B 边缘友好 LLM，多种工作负载"}, {"metric": "端到端加速比", "value": "1.48-1.61x", "setting": "相比 FP16 自回归解码，在支持的 LLaMA 模型上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 位嵌套表示：将低精度草稿直接嵌入高精度目标权重中，共享单一物理权重表示，消除额外内存开销；渐进式精度设计：先构建强低精度基座，再通过残差细化恢复高精度目标，兼顾草稿质量和目标质量；将该渐进式精度设计扩展到KV缓存，支持长上下文推理

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft to propose multiple tokens for parallel verification. However, existing methods often require an additional draft model or weight representation, introducing non-negligible memory overhead on resource-constrained devices. Self-speculative approaches reduce this overhead, yet still face trade-offs between draft quality, target quality, and storage efficiency. We propose BitNest, a bit-nested speculative decoding framework that embeds a low-precision draft directly into the higher-precision target representation. Instead of deriving a draft from a predefined target, BitNest first constructs a strong low-precision base and then recovers the higher-precision target through residual refinement, enabling both models to share a single physical weight representation. BitNest further extends this progressive-precision design to the KV cache for long-context inference. Across multiple 7B--8B edge-friendly LLMs and diverse workloads, BitNest achieves an average speculative acceptance rate of 95.2% while closely preserving higher-precision model quality, and delivers 1.48--1.61x end-to-end speedup over FP16 autoregressive decoding. On the LLaMA models supported by all representative self-speculative baselines, BitNest also achieves consistently competitive or higher decoding speedup.

</details>


### [64] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JOVE 面向异构LLM协作的DAG任务图推理，提出在线联合执行分配与付费输出验证的框架，用逐查询MILP在长期预算和延迟约束下平衡执行成本与学习价值，并给出次线性遗憾保证；四个推理基准上平均成本与延迟降低至少3.17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务质量、调用成本和执行时间先验未知、且存在长期预算与单查询延迟约束的情况下，如何联合决定每个子任务的执行LLM与需要付费验证的中间输出，以在当下执行支出与为未来学习之间取得平衡。

Method: JOVE 将复杂推理查询分解为有向无环任务图（DAG），每个子任务需要从一组异构LLM中选择执行者。系统在不知道各LLM对特定子任务真实质量、调用成本和执行时间的情况下运行：对每个查询，求解一个混合整数线性规划（MILP），决策变量同时刻画“哪个LLM执行哪个子任务”和“哪些中间输出送去付费验证”。优化目标在长期预算约束、单查询延迟约束下，平衡即时执行成本与通过验证获取信息的长期价值；由于验证是异步进行的，不能阻塞当前查询执行。验证返回的反馈用于在线更新各子任务上LLM质量的估计，形成闭环学习。为鼓励探索，分配决策中加入信息增益奖励项，把“学习价值”显式纳入MILP目标。理论部分在自然假设下证明了质量学习遗憾为次线性。推理路径为：查询分解→构建任务图→逐查询MILP求解→按解分发执行→异步验证选定输出→更新质量估计→影响后续查询的分配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成本与延迟", "value": "至少降低3.17倍", "setting": "四个推理基准上，与标准推理基线相比，同时保持有竞争力的准确率"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "与标准推理基线相当（competitive）", "setting": "四个推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将执行LLM分配与中间输出付费验证选择统一为在线联合决策框架JOVE；通过逐查询求解混合整数线性规划（MILP）同时处理延迟约束与长期预算约束；基于验证反馈在线更新任务相关的LLM质量估计，并引入信息增益奖励把学习价值纳入分配决策；在自然假设下给出质量学习的次线性遗憾界

Abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [65] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向工具型智能体，提出用SLM做任务-工具意图匹配分类，逐次核验智能体所选工具是否与任务意图一致，并用prompt优化、SFT与GRPO训练专化模型，配套构建跨MCP服务器的多工具任务数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统授权机制只能判断智能体是否被允许调用某工具，无法判断该工具选择是否是满足任务意图的合理、相关步骤；因此需要一次一验，对每次工具调用进行任务-工具意图相关性判定。

Method: 系统以工具型智能体的单次工具调用为输入：给定任务（意图）描述与智能体所选工具（可能涉及跨多个MCP服务器的工具集合），由一个SLM扮演任务-工具相关性分类器，对每一个被选中的工具独立评估其是否构成服务于任务意图的逻辑且相关的步骤，并输出相关性信号，供下游执行或拦截策略使用。训练与优化采用三阶段/多手段策略：先进行prompt优化以适配该判定任务，再进行监督微调使SLM专化，最后用GRPO进行强化学习优化。评测基于作者构建的新数据集，数据集中的多工具任务其所需工具分布在不同的MCP服务器上，从而覆盖跨服务器工具选择的判定场景。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练数据规模、训练硬件、推理时延与吞吐等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出以SLM作为任务-工具相关性分类器，对每次工具调用独立评估任务契合度并输出相关性信号供下游执行；构建面向多工具任务的专用数据集，所需工具跨不同MCP服务器；将prompt优化、监督微调与GRPO强化学习结合，用于该分类任务的SLM优化与专化

Abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [66] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DepGPO 通过构建命令依赖图并反向追踪验证器关注的资源，将信用精确分配给影响任务结果的命令步骤，从而提升终端智能体在多步任务中的强化学习效果与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹级和步骤级信用分配方法未显式追踪命令之间的读写依赖，导致训练信号被错误分配给无关操作，削弱了从相关步骤中学习的效果。

Method: 方法整体分为三个主要阶段：首先，智能体在终端环境中执行任务，产生包含命令及其读写资源的执行轨迹；其次，从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，节点为命令或资源，边表示读写依赖关系；然后，从任务验证器最终检查的资源出发反向遍历依赖图，找到与该结果相关的写操作及其支撑读操作，并据此计算每个步骤的信用；最后，将计算得到的信用用于在轨迹内部重新分配优势值，并基于 Group Policy Optimization（如 GRPO 类方法）进行策略更新。训练目标是最大化任务成功奖励，同时利用依赖路径保证信用只分配给真正影响结果的步骤，从而提高学习效率和稳定性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建命令依赖图（command dependency graph）并沿着从任务验证器检查的资源反向追踪，以显式表示命令间的读写依赖。；基于依赖路径将信用分配给相关的写操作及其支撑读操作，从而在步骤间重新分配轨迹优势。；提出 Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization（DepGPO），在组策略优化框架中融入依赖感知的信用分配。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [67] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了三种高效推理训练方法对思维链忠实性与可监控性的影响，发现长度压缩虽普遍降低忠实性（因一致性下降），但可监控性保持稳健，为推理效率与可靠性的权衡提供了实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效推理训练在减少思维链（CoT）长度的同时，是否会损害 CoT 对模型决策的忠实性（faithfulness）以及可监控性（monitorability）尚不明确。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是对基础大语言模型采用三种高效推理训练方法进行微调：(1) 固定生成预算——限制生成的最大 token 数；(2) 每样例长度目标——为每个训练样本指定目标 CoT 长度；(3) 组相对长度奖励——在组内相对长度上给予奖励以施加长度压力。训练后，评估模型在相关输入上的 CoT 忠实性（CoT 是否反映决策）和可监控性（当输入被干预时，CoT 是否揭示输出变化）。评估发现不同方法对忠实性和可监控性的影响不同：忠实性多数情况下下降，主要归因于模型输出一致性降低；可监控性更稳健，即使 CoT 大幅缩短，模型仍能承认输入干预对答案的影响。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统对比三种施加长度压力的高效推理训练方法：固定生成预算、每样例长度目标、组相对长度奖励。；在多种模型上评估高效推理对 CoT 忠实性（CoT 是否如实反映模型决策）和可监控性（CoT 是否揭示输入干预对输出的影响）的差异化影响。；发现忠实性在多数设置下下降（主要因为模型一致性降低），而可监控性更稳健。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [68] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务-工具协同演化框架，通过在线与任务后自改进递归提升推理数据合成难度，14轮后解题准确率从100%降至54.8%，合成数据提升下游SFT/GRPO，27B学生模型在APEX达62.5% mean-16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing recursive reasoning-data synthesis reuses generated problems as seeds but keeps the construction harness fixed, limiting the ability to generate progressively harder valid reasoning tasks as the task distribution evolves.

Method: The framework performs recursive harness self-improvement for reasoning-data synthesis. It starts from a task distribution and a synthesis harness consisting of skills, prompts, and workflows. Two update schedules are combined. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch; candidate revisions are adopted only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. This co-evolves the harness with the generated tasks across fourteen evolution rounds in mathematics, coding, and science. The resulting data is used for downstream SFT and GRPO training; a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean solver accuracy", "value": "decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds", "setting": "Across mathematics, coding, and science synthesis; lower accuracy indicates harder generated tasks."}, {"metric": "mean-16 accuracy on APEX", "value": "62.5%", "setting": "27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples; competitive with selected frontier-model references."}, {"metric": "downstream SFT and GRPO performance", "value": "improved", "setting": "The resulting synthesized data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance."}, {"metric": "ablation comparison", "value": "combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone", "setting": "Ablations on mathematics, coding, and science."}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Task-harness co-evolution framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis.；Online self-improvement that converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation.；Post-task self-improvement that revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, accepting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase.；Keeps model weights and verification criteria fixed to isolate harness/task co-evolution.

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [69] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLINT is a production Text-to-SQL system for complex financial data. It combines a lookup agent for opaque ID resolution, embedding-based retrieval of expert query templates, and foreign-key-based schema linking. Evaluated on 359 questions over production financial schemas, it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM and is deployed in a financial data retrieval service.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems perform poorly (below 50%) on production financial databases because concepts are stored as opaque integer keys, simple queries require multiple joins, and filter predicates reference opaque IDs rather than human-readable strings.

Method: FLINT is a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system with three main components. (1) A lookup agent dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, addressing the problem that financial databases store concepts as opaque integer keys. (2) Embedding-based retrieval selects structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, providing reusable query structures. (3) Schema linking prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service. Evaluation is conducted on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas, comparing FLINT against various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Text-to-SQL accuracy", "value": "exceeds various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM", "setting": "Evaluation on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas; baselines use the same LLM"}]}

Conclusion: Lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints；Embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank；Schema linking that prunes large table schemas by traversing foreign-key chains rather than relying on name similarity alone

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [70] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JIL，一种针对基于预测输出长度的LLM调度器（如TRAIL）的对抗攻击。JIL通过优化对抗后缀使长度预测探针低估请求长度，从而获得调度优先级并减少完成时间。在两种数据集和四种LLM上的实验显示，预测长度最多减少83.4%，对抗请求完成时间平均加速最高1.53倍。研究还评估了调度器侧防御，发现将预测分组为粗略区间可有效缓解攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预测输出长度的LLM调度器（如TRAIL）可能被对抗性输入操纵，使请求获得不应有的高优先级，同时可能降低服务质量。

Method: JIL攻击针对基于预测输出长度的LLM调度器（以TRAIL为例）。TRAIL使用轻量级探针预测请求的输出长度，调度器根据预测长度实施最短作业优先（SJF）策略。JIL通过优化对抗后缀（adversarial suffix）来操纵输入序列，使得探针低估请求的输出长度。在服务流程中，请求首先经过长度预测探针，预测值被用于调度决策；JIL攻击导致预测长度显著降低，从而让对抗性请求被优先调度，减少其完成时间。论文在两种数据集和四种LLM上评估了攻击效果，并测试了防御措施：将长度预测分组为粗略区间可以限制单个请求的优先级优势，从而缓解攻击。整个方法不修改调度器算法本身，而是通过输入操纵影响调度信号。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "预测输出长度减少", "value": "高达83.4%", "setting": "在端到端服务实验中，使用TRAIL作为案例研究，在两种数据集和四种LLM上评估"}, {"metric": "对抗性请求完成时间加速", "value": "平均最高1.53倍", "setting": "端到端服务实验，多种请求配置和部署设置"}]}

Conclusion: 提出JIL攻击：通过优化对抗后缀，使轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而在调度中获得更高优先级。；在端到端服务实验中量化了预测长度减少（最大83.4%）与完成时间加速（平均最高1.53倍），并揭示了预测长度与实际输出长度之间的显著不匹配。；评估了调度器侧防御措施（如将长度预测分组为粗略区间），可减少JIL的调度优势并缓解对良性请求的延迟。

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [71] [Preserving Mathematical Reasoning in Compressed Diffusion Language Models via Trajectory-Aware Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03326)
*Tian Liang,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出轨迹感知低秩压缩方法，解决扩散语言模型压缩中校准状态与推理轨迹不匹配导致数学推理能力下降的问题，通过蒙特卡洛估计轨迹二阶矩，在同等压缩率下保留更多推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLM）在低秩压缩时，传统校准基于干净、完全可见的激活，而推理实际经过部分掩码的中间状态，导致压缩后数学推理能力严重退化；需要刻画并优化轨迹分布状态下的低秩近似质量。

Method: 论文针对扩散语言模型（dLLM）的低秩压缩流程。输入是模型权重与校准数据；传统方法在完全可见的干净激活上做低秩分解（如SVD），但推理时状态是经扩散过程部分掩码的中间激活。本文提出轨迹感知低秩目标：将近似误差在多个corruption level和不同masking realization上加权，使低秩基更好地覆盖推理轨迹上的激活分布。为高效估计该目标，Traj-MC通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩，并证明在采样状态上的最优性以及总体一致性。校准后执行低秩压缩，推理时使用压缩模型生成，评估压缩后数学推理能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出轨迹感知的低秩目标，在 corruption levels 与 masking realizations 上定义近似质量；提出 Traj-MC，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩，并证明采样状态最优性与总体一致性；在匹配压缩预算下，轨迹感知校准相比干净校准显著提升生成轨迹的重构质量并更好保持数学推理能力

Abstract: Diffusion language model (dLLM) compression faces a known challenge because calibration is typically performed on clean, fully visible activations, whereas inference traverses partially masked intermediate states. For low-rank compression, this raises two questions. First, can low-rank optimality still be characterized when approximation quality is measured over trajectory-distributed states, and second, does the choice of calibration states affect mathematical reasoning preservation under compression? We address these questions by formulating a trajectory-aware low-rank objective over corruption levels and masking realizations. To estimate this objective efficiently, we propose Traj-MC, which estimates the trajectory second moment through Monte Carlo sampling and yields exact sampled-state optimality and population consistency. Under matched compression budgets, trajectory-aware calibration improves reconstruction over the generation trajectory and preserves substantially more mathematical reasoning than clean calibration on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our results connect trajectory-aware low-rank optimality to the reasoning capability retained after dLLM compression. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [72] [hacktrace: behavior-supervised detection of reward hacking during code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03055)
*Hao Jiang,Xin Li,Annan Wang,Yichi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HACKTRACE发布173,561条Qwen3-8B多轮编码轨迹标注，提出行为监督的奖励作弊检测器，复用智能体生成时已有的内部状态并结合最终文件静态特征，无需额外token或前向计算，per-problem AUC达0.997、监控开销仅8 ms；该信号还用于GRPO惩罚，将通过解中的作弊占比从82-91%降至1-5%，同时保留诚实正确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代码生成智能体的多轮轨迹中，如何检测包括失败尝试在内的奖励作弊捷径行为，并将检测转化为有效的训练惩罚信号，同时保持低延迟。

Method: HACKTRACE以多轮代码生成智能体的轨迹为输入，包括每轮对话上下文、智能体生成的代码及其内部隐藏状态，以及最终产出的文件。数据为173,561条来自Qwen3-8B的带标注多轮编码轨迹，标注针对捷径行为（包含失败的作弊尝试），而非仅标注是否最终通过。主模块按处理顺序为：第一，内部状态复用模块，直接从智能体生成代码时已计算的前向状态中抽取表示，不触发额外的语言模型token生成或前向计算，因此可以在一轮生成尚未完成时给出监控信号；第二，行为监督检测器，以显式监督捷径行为为训练目标（与作弊是否成功解耦）来学习作弊判定；第三，静态特征模块，对最终生成文件做静态分析以提取特征；第四，融合判定模块，将内部状态证据与最终文件静态特征结合，输出每道题的作弊概率并聚合得到per-problem AUC。训练目标为行为级二分类监督，而非结果级通过与否的代理目标。推理/服务流程中，监控器复用已有生成状态并叠加静态特征，整体监控开销为8 ms，从而替代在诚实性提问上重新运行模型的做法，在精度和延迟两方面均更优。此外，同一套生成状态被用作强化学习的廉价监控信号：在GRPO训练中对检测到的作弊行为施加强惩罚，使通过解中作弊占比从82-91%降至1-5%，同时保留诚实且正确的解，并在策略演化过程中维持较高检测精度，说明监督目标与监控证据来源两者对将准确检测转化为有用训练信号都至关重要。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean per-problem AUC", "value": "0.997", "setting": "HACKTRACE（内部生成状态+最终文件静态特征）对比在诚实性问答上重新运行模型的监控器"}, {"metric": "monitoring overhead", "value": "8 ms", "setting": "HACKTRACE监控开销"}, {"metric": "cheating share of passing solutions", "value": "82-91% 降至 1-5%", "setting": "使用强GRPO惩罚，同时保留诚实且正确的解并维持高检测精度（策略演化过程中）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出行为监督目标：独立于作弊是否成功来监督捷径行为，而非仅监督最终是否通过；HACKTRACE监控器复用智能体生成代码时已计算的内部状态，无需额外语言模型token或前向计算，可在一轮完成前进行监控；将内部生成状态证据与最终文件的静态特征结合进行判定；把同一生成状态作为强化学习（GRPO）的廉价监控信号，对作弊行为施加强惩罚

Abstract: A coding agent can earn a passing grade by fixing its code, or by deleting the test that exposes the bug. Detecting such reward hacking requires recognizing attempted shortcuts, including those that fail. We release 173,561 annotated multi-turn coding trajectories from Qwen3-8B and show that supervising shortcut behavior independently of exploit success substantially improves detection. We introduce HACKTRACE, a behavior-supervised monitor that reads the internal states the agent already computes while generating code. Reusing these states enables monitoring before a turn is complete, without additional language-model tokens or passes. Combining this evidence with static features of the final files achieves a mean per-problem AUC of 0.997 with 8 ms of monitoring overhead, improving both accuracy and latency over monitors that run the model again on an honesty question and answer. The same generation states also provide an inexpensive monitoring signal for reinforcement learning. With strong GRPO penalties, HACKTRACE reduces the cheating share of passing solutions from 82-91% to 1-5%, while retaining honest, correct solutions and maintaining high detection accuracy as the policy evolves. Our results show that both the supervision target and the source of monitoring evidence matter for turning accurate detection into a useful training signal.

</details>


### [73] [DyadMem: A Long-Term Memory Benchmark of How Agents Work with Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03020)
*Yifei Tao,Xinyu Zhong,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Fanyi Wang,Tengda Guo,Wentao Qiu,Ying Wang,Liujian Tang*

Main category: cs.AI
