TL;DR: DyadMem 提出 User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM) 定义，并构建包含 3,065 episodes、50,961 sessions 与 61,210 QA 的双域全流水线长期记忆基准。通过 Gold-Memory 与 Full-Pipeline 两种 QA 设置，揭示当前 LLM 在记忆捕获、召回与安全删除上的系统瓶颈，并验证 URAM 对 20 个模型均有正向效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准主要监督用户事实与偏好，或可跨用户复用的经验，却忽略了“特定智能体应如何与该用户协作”这种关系特定记忆；同时多数评估仅用最终答案 QA 衡量模型，评估不完整且不可靠。

Method: DyadMem 以多会话人机交互轨迹为输入，沿同一轨迹联合标注两类记忆：用户侧记忆（用户事实与偏好）与 URAM（关系特定的智能体工作记忆）。数据集包含 3,065 个 episode、50,961 个 session 与 61,210 个 QA 实例，共 6 类记忆。评测流程分为会话级的 Capture 与 Update 金标注释、查询级的 Recall 支持，以及两种 QA 设置：Gold-Memory QA 直接提供金标记忆以评估端到端问答能力；Full-Pipeline QA 则要求模型自行完成记忆捕获、更新、召回并最终回答，从而暴露级联误差。实验覆盖 16 个开源权重模型与 4 个专有模型，对比结果显示 Gold-Memory 下表现稳定，而 Full-Pipeline 下显著下降，说明记忆流水线中的 Capture 召回不足、Recall 不完整以及不安全删除是主要瓶颈。论文进一步设计严格实验验证 URAM 定义的有效性，观察到对全部 20 个模型的正向效果。整体架构强调双域、全流水线的记忆监督与评估，而非单点最终答案匹配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Gold-Memory QA 表现", "value": "consistent strong across 20 models (16 open-weight + 4 proprietary)", "setting": "DyadMem Gold-Memory QA setting"}, {"metric": "Full-Pipeline QA 表现", "value": "drops sharply compared with Gold-Memory QA", "setting": "DyadMem Full-Pipeline QA setting"}, {"metric": "URAM effectiveness", "value": "positive effects for all 20 models", "setting": "rigorous validation experiment on DyadMem"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM) 新定义，区分用户侧记忆与关系特定智能体记忆；构建 DyadMem 基准，在同一多会话轨迹上联合标注用户侧记忆与 URAM，共 6 类记忆；提供会话级 Capture 与 Update 金标注释、查询级 Recall 支持，以及 Gold-Memory 与 Full-Pipeline 两种 QA 设置；通过 16 个开源权重模型与 4 个专有模型揭示 Gold-Memory QA 强而 Full-Pipeline QA 显著下降的差距，暴露低 Capture 召回、Recall 不完整与不安全删除问题；通过严格实验验证 URAM 对全部 20 个模型均有正向效果

Abstract: Long-term agents must remember not only what is true about a user, but also how a particular agent should work with that user as their shared history evolves. Existing benchmarks primarily supervise user facts and preferences or experience reusable across users, leaving this relationship-specific agent memory implicit. Additionally, most prior works measure the model solely with final-answer QA over long interaction histories, making the assessment still incomplete and unreliable. To this end, we introduce DyadMem with the proposed new definition User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM). DyadMem jointly annotates user-side memory and URAM along the same multi-session trajectories, resulting in 6 memory categories. To summarize, it includes 3,065 episodes, 50,961 sessions, and 61,210 QA instances, with extensive session-level Capture and Update gold annotations, query-level Recall support, and two QA settings: Gold-Memory and Full-Pipeline. Across 16 open-weight and 4 proprietary models, Gold-Memory QA is consistently strong, yet Full-Pipeline QA drops sharply. Such a gap explicitly supports our fine-grained evaluation design. Additionally, several quantitative results further reveal low Capture recall, incomplete Recall, and unsafe-deletion issues arising from even the frontier LLMs. We further conduct a rigorous experiment to validate the effectiveness of our URAM and observe the positive effects for all 20 models. In summary, DyadMem is a dual-domain, full-pipeline memory benchmark with extensive annotation efforts for advancing the domain's development.

</details>


### [74] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PROVE-REC，一种可验证偏好推理框架，通过两阶段生成偏好证明并仅基于证明进行推荐，配合 grounding 与 influence 验证及排序保持目标，在多个真实数据集上优于强基线，最高提升 7.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的推荐理由可能无法反映实际影响推荐结果的信息，存在 grounding-influence gap，即选择的证据对偏好主张支撑不足，或偏好主张对最终排序影响微弱。

Method: PROVE-REC 是一个两阶段框架。输入为用户完整的历史交互序列及评论文本。Pass A 将完整的 pre-target 历史转换为紧凑的偏好证明，证明由正向偏好声明和规避偏好声明组成，每条声明链接到所选的证据条目。Pass B 仅使用该证明及其所选证据来预测下一物品，从而防止推荐器绕过推理路径。为了验证证据到证明的 grounding，框架比较掩码所选证据与掩码一个可比对照条目的效果差异。为了验证证明到推荐的 influence，框架移除一条偏好声明并测量目标物品排序边际的下降。此外，训练中引入 ranking-preservation 目标，以保留完整历史中的有用信息并维持排序质量。推理时，模型先根据历史生成偏好证明，再基于证明和证据直接输出下一物品的推荐得分或排序。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对性能提升", "value": "最高 7.45%", "setting": "在多个真实世界数据集上，与强序列、生成式及LLM增强基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出两阶段可验证偏好推理框架 PROVE-REC：Pass A 生成与证据链接的偏好证明，Pass B 仅凭证明和证据进行推荐。；设计 grounding 验证：通过对比掩码所选证据与掩码对照证据的效果差异来检验证据对证明的支撑。；设计 influence 验证：移除某偏好主张并测量目标物品排序边际的下降，以检验证明对推荐的影响。；引入 ranking-preservation 目标，在利用完整历史信息的同时保持排序质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [75] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE 提出一套测量协议与在线算法，用于决定计算机使用智能体何时将重复 GUI 过程编译为程序以节省 token。它记录成功/失败的编译成本，在匹配输入上比较 agent 与程序执行成本以估计回收次数，并用预算约束在线决策。仿真显示相比 ReAct 降低 17.3% token 成本，相比 AutoRPA 和 ToolPro 适配分别降低 24.9% 和 17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译 GUI 过程能否回收成本存在两重不确定性：编译尝试可能失败或需要修复导致成本不确定；未来任务是否还会到来、GUI 是否漂移导致程序失效也不确定。核心问题是如何测量回收期并在线决定何时编译。

Method: PACE 由测量协议与在线编译算法组成。输入是重复到达的 GUI 任务流以及可选的动作：用源 agent 执行任务，或把 GUI 过程编译为程序后执行。测量协议记录每次编译尝试的结果与成本，包括成功与失败的尝试，并在匹配的任务输入上分别测量 agent 执行成本和程序执行成本，据此估计每次使用节省和回收次数。在线算法维护历史任务到达和编译结果，用这些信息估计未来节省，并与包含失败尝试的编译成本比较；在执行或编译前，检查累计执行和编译开销是否超过由已观测任务到达确定的累计预算，只有当估计的未来节省足以覆盖成本且不违反预算时才允许编译。该预算约束在设定的动作成本假设下保证每次任务到达后的总成本不超过全用 agent 执行成本的最优值的 1+ε 倍。推理/服务流程是：任务到达后，系统先根据当前统计估计是否值得编译，若满足条件则触发编译并记录结果，否则直接用 agent 执行；已编译程序在后续任务中复用，若 GUI 漂移导致失效则再次回退到 agent 或重新编译。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "估计回收次数（不含源 agent 运行）", "value": "2-16 次使用", "setting": "针对成功的编译尝试"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "token 成本降低", "value": "17.3%", "setting": "使用记录的任务到达进行仿真，对比 ReAct，ε=0.25"}, {"metric": "token 成本降低", "value": "24.9%", "setting": "使用记录的任务到达进行仿真，对比 AutoRPA 适配版本，ε=0.25"}, {"metric": "token 成本降低", "value": "17.3%", "setting": "使用记录的任务到达进行仿真，对比 ToolPro 适配版本，ε=0.25"}]}

Conclusion: 提出测量协议：记录成功与失败的编译成本，并在匹配的任务输入上比较 agent 执行成本与程序执行成本，以估计单次使用节省和回收次数。；提出在线编译算法：基于历史任务到达和编译结果，比较估计的未来节省与包含失败尝试的编译成本。；在允许执行或编译前，检查累计执行与编译开销是否超出由观测到的任务到达确定的累计预算。；给出理论保证：在设定的动作成本假设下，每次任务到达后的总成本至多是最优全用 agent 执行成本的 1+ε 倍。

Abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+\epsilon$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($\epsilon=0.25$).

</details>


### [76] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作把LLM智能体安全监控的误报审计建模为受监控器选择偏差影响的PU排序问题，提出信任感知PU监督、可靠性门控排序蒸馏与共识引导结构精炼的两阶段框架。框架无需报警安全标签、不修改监控器，在多种主流安全监控器上取得0.6444 macro AUPRC，较八个PU基线高5.27–16.98个百分点，并在5%评审预算下比PULDA多找回33.3%的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全监控器产生的报警与监控分数中，真实误报（被错误标记的安全轨迹）与真实风险交错，导致仍需大量人工核验才能确定可靠阈值；同时只有少量已验证安全的非报警轨迹可用、报警无标签，且观测到的安全样本因被监控器接受而产生选择偏差，无法代表需要找回的隐藏安全报警。

Method: 框架为两阶段设计。第一阶段为带信任感知的PU监督：输入是少量已验证安全的非报警轨迹（观测正样本）与无标签的报警轨迹，通过对安全参考样本进行域适配，将其分布向报警域靠拢，缓解监控器接受行为造成的选择偏差；同时对可能属于隐藏误报的报警施加受保护处理，避免被当作纯负样本过度压制。第二阶段整合排序信号：Reliability-gated Rank Distillation 以可靠性门控加权，把多个PU参考模型给出的成对/排序偏好蒸馏进单一学生模型，从而在不依赖报警安全标签的情况下获得稳定排序；随后 Consensus-guided Structural Refinement 在预测阶段利用分层安全参考支撑结构、报警之间的相互关系以及参考模型共识，对学生排序做结构性精修。整个训练过程不需要报警安全标签，监控器原始分数与监控模型保持不变，仅在监控之后作为后处理排序层输出可用性的报警排序，用于在给定评审预算下优先返回疑似误报。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro AUPRC", "value": "0.6444", "setting": "在主流安全监控器上评估，优于8个PU基线5.27–16.98个百分点"}, {"metric": "false alarms recovered at 5% review budget", "value": "比最强PU基线PULDA多33.3%", "setting": "5%人工评审预算下的误报找回量对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将误报审计形式化为PU排序问题，并识别监控器诱导的选择偏差；Trust-aware PU Supervision：把安全参考样本向报警域适配，同时对可能的误报施加受控的负向压力保护；Reliability-gated Rank Distillation：依据可靠性门控，将多个PU参考模型的排序偏好蒸馏到单一学生模型；Consensus-guided Structural Refinement：利用分层安全参考支撑、报警间关系与预测参考共识来精炼学生排序；框架拟合不需要报警安全标签，且不改变底层监控器

Abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [77] [Frequency Is Not Sensitivity Identifying Safety-Sensitive Experts in Sparse MoE LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02910)
*Md Nurul Absar Siddiky,Liuwan Zhu,Yingfei Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对稀疏 MoE LLM，提出用路由器梯度敏感性替代激活频率来识别安全敏感专家。在五个 MoE 模型上，梯度选择在多数条件下更有效地降低拒绝率，OLMoE 上拒绝率从 34/100 降至 9/100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏 MoE LLM 中，识别对安全行为影响最大的专家，以通过抑制少量专家来削弱模型安全行为（安全测试视角）。通常使用激活频率，但频率反映使用而非影响，本文探讨路由器梯度敏感性是否更能识别安全敏感专家。

Method: 本文不提出新的模型架构，而是对现有稀疏 MoE LLM 进行专家级分析。输入为提示（500 良性+500 恶意）和模型；使用路由器梯度敏感性：对每个专家，计算序列损失对门控权重的梯度，作为该专家对损失的敏感度分数。同时计算激活频率基线。根据分数排序专家，在两种预算下抑制选定的专家：等专家数（每层相同数量）和等恶意路由流量（1%-5%）。评估在 100 条保留恶意提示上的拒绝率。无训练过程，仅推理阶段修改路由。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "拒绝率（下降）", "value": "从 100 条提示中 34 条拒绝降至 9 条（相对下降 73.53%）", "setting": "OLMoE 模型，等专家数预算，100 条保留恶意提示"}, {"metric": "梯度选择优于激活选择的次数", "value": "24/25 条件（等预算下），25/25 优于随机均值", "setting": "五种 MoE 架构，500 条恶意提示和 500 条良性提示"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出路由器梯度敏感性度量专家对序列损失的敏感度，替代激活频率；在五种 MoE 架构、不同预算（等专家数、等恶意路由流量）下系统评估；发现梯度选择在多数条件下比激活选择更有效地降低拒绝率；匹配每层专家数后梯度选择仍优于激活选择；探索性发现恶意与良性样本的集中度差距与峰值梯度效应相关（rho=0.90, p=0.083）

Abstract: Suppressing a small set of routed experts can weaken the safety behavior of a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model without retraining. Which experts to suppress is therefore a security question, and the usual answer is activation frequency, but frequency measures use, not influence. We test an alternative: router-gradient sensitivity, the sensitivity of the sequence loss to the gate weights that select an expert. Across five MoE architectures, we rank experts by each signal on 500 benign and 500 malicious prompts and measure refusal on 100 held-out malicious prompts under two budgets: equal expert counts and equal nominal malicious routing traffic (1%-5%). Under each of the two budgets, router-gradient selection reduces refusals more than activation in 24 of 25 conditions, and more than a ten-trial random mean in all 25. The largest effect is in OLMoE, where refusals fall from 34 to 9 of 100 prompts (73.53% relative) with no degraded outputs, indicating substantive compliance rather than broken generation. After matching expert counts in every layer, gradient selection still produces greater refusal reduction than activation in 23 of 25 conditions, with two ties. An exploratory cross-model analysis links larger malicious-versus-benign concentration gaps to greater peak gradient effects (rho = 0.90; exact two-sided p = 0.083, n = 5). Together, the results support gradient selection under the tested budgets.

</details>


### [78] [Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858)
*Moonseok Choi,Taehong Moon,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD)：在固定 harness 的智能体环境中，把蒸馏重点放在教师相对 harness 的增量能力上，通过动作偏好对比和有效性校验构造监督信号，无需任务奖励或成功标签，并在多个长时程智能体基准上优于 on-policy 蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将带有 harness（管理上下文、工具与反馈的软件层）的语言模型智能体蒸馏为小模型时，harness 保持不变，学生模型真正需要学习的是 harness 无法提供的教师特有行为能力，而标准蒸馏把 harness 当作输入的一部分并模仿教师完整输出，因而学不到这些能力。

Method: HAD 以标准 on-policy distillation 为基础：学生模型在固定 harness 环境中执行任务，harness 负责管理其上下文、工具调用与反馈。核心由两个组件构成。其一为动作偏好（action preference）：在固定 harness 下，对同一教师模型分别提供和不提供 harness 信息，得到两组动作，在学生模型自身推理之后对这些动作进行打分，形成偏好对，从而显式对比出教师相对于 harness 的增量能力，而不是让学生简单模仿教师完整输出。其二为有效性校验（validity check）：利用 harness 记录检查偏好对中被偏好的动作是否与 harness 状态/记录矛盾，丢弃矛盾样本，避免引入错误监督信号。整个流程不需要任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息，训练目标即用上述偏好对驱动 on-policy 蒸馏。推理/部署时 harness 保持不变，学生只需在 harness 提供状态信息的情况下正确行动；分析表明学生能把可学习的反馈保留在模型权重中，而状态信息仍从 harness 读取。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长时程智能体基准性能", "value": "在多个长时程智能体基准和多种模型上优于使用相同固定 harness 的 on-policy 蒸馏基线（摘要未给出具体数值）", "setting": "多种长时程 agent 基准，与同 harness 的 on-policy 蒸馏基线对比"}, {"metric": "无效循环次数", "value": "相比基线进入更少的无产出循环", "setting": "长时程智能体任务分析"}, {"metric": "错误恢复能力", "value": "相比基线更频繁地从错误中恢复", "setting": "长时程智能体任务分析"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD)，把蒸馏目标聚焦于教师相对 harness 的增量能力；动作偏好机制：在学生自身推理之后对比同一教师在有/无 harness 信息下的动作；有效性校验：丢弃偏好动作与 harness 记录相矛盾的偏好对；无需任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息，即可在固定 harness 下进行 on-policy 蒸馏

Abstract: Language model agents are deployed with a harness, the software around the model that manages its context, tools, and feedback. When such an agent is distilled into a smaller one, the harness stays in place, so the student mainly needs the teacher-specific abilities that the harness cannot provide, such as acting correctly on harness information. Standard distillation, however, imitates the teacher's full outputs and treats the harness as part of the input. We propose Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD), which focuses distillation on what the teacher adds beyond the harness. HAD complements on-policy distillation with two components: an action preference that contrasts the same teacher's actions with and without the harness information, scored after the student's own reasoning, and a validity check that drops preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts the harness records. We show that the contrast gives the student information that imitating the teacher alone cannot provide, and HAD needs no task rewards, success labels, or future information. Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness. Our analysis shows that HAD enters fewer unproductive loops and recovers from errors more often than the baselines, and suggests that it adaptively keeps learnable feedback in its weights while reading state information from the harness.

</details>


### [79] [AMBER: Multi-View Adaptive Budget Allocation for Listwise Vision-Language Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02831)
*Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Congmin Zheng,Teng Wang,Junjie Wu,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMBER提出一种预算自适应的多视角VLM重排序框架，通过Elo更新和两级动态分配（候选视角与查询调度）在有限VLM调用下优化全局排序，在多个多模态检索基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM作为列表式重排序器时推理成本高，现有多次调用策略使用固定调度，导致在无信息量的候选对和简单查询上浪费昂贵的VLM调用。

Method: AMBER是一个在线、有预算限制的多视角重排序框架。输入是多模态检索的候选列表和查询。它将碎片化的列表式VLM输出视为局部锦标赛，通过连续Elo更新维护一个轻量级的全局排序状态，从而整合多次VLM调用的结果。在每次迭代中，AMBER在两个层级分配计算预算：1）候选视角级：动态构建具有高分数模糊度的候选视图，即优先评估模型排名不确定性高的候选对；2）查询级：调度查询以最大化期望信息增益，优先处理信息量大的查询。Elo更新被证明等价于在Bradley-Terry对数似然上的随机梯度上升步骤，查询级分配策略具有子模信息论动机。推理时，在给定的VLM调用预算下，动态选择候选对进行VLM评估，更新Elo分数，并更新全局排序，最终输出重排序结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "overall performance", "value": "strongest among compared multi-call VLM reranking methods under comparable VLM-call budgets", "setting": "CIRR, CIRCO, PhotoBench datasets"}, {"metric": "effectiveness in lower-budget settings", "value": "remains effective", "setting": "lower VLM-call budgets"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将碎片化的列表式VLM输出视为局部锦标赛，用连续Elo更新维护轻量级全局排序状态；在候选视角层面动态构建高分数模糊度的候选视图；在查询层面通过期望信息增益调度查询；理论证明每个Elo更新对应Bradley-Terry对数似然上的随机梯度上升步骤；为查询级分配策略提供子模信息论动机

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful listwise rerankers for multimodal retrieval, but high inference costs restrict them to evaluating small local candidate views. Existing multi-call strategies rely on fixed schedules, wasting expensive VLM calls on uninformative candidate pairs and easy queries. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-view Budgeted Elo Reranking (AMBER), an online, budgeted multi-view reranking framework that dynamically optimizes global resource allocation. AMBER treats fragmented listwise VLM outputs as local tournaments, using continuous Elo updates to maintain a lightweight global ranking state. Building on this, it allocates computation at two levels: dynamically constructing candidate views with high score ambiguity, and scheduling queries to maximize expected information gain. We show that each Elo update corresponds to a stochastic gradient ascent step on the Bradley-Terry log-likelihood, and provide a submodular information-theoretic motivation for the query-level allocation strategy. Experiments on CIRR, CIRCO, and PhotoBench demonstrate that AMBER achieves the strongest overall performance among the compared multi-call VLM reranking methods under comparable VLM-call budgets, while remaining effective in lower-budget settings. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [80] [On the Chain-of-Thought Monitorability of Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02741)
*Han Wang,Ishwar B Balappanawar,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个系统评估循环语言模型 CoT 可监控性的工作。在 MonitorBench 的 8 项任务上，标准与压力测试对比显示更深循环深度在部分逻辑/科学/工程线索任务中降低可监控性，但该下降不能完全由任务难度、验证通过率或生成长度解释；跨模型对比未发现 LoopLM 系统性更难监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型（LoopLM）重复应用共享 Transformer 层以增加有效计算深度，但循环架构对思维链（CoT）可监控性的影响尚不明确。

Method: 论文研究循环语言模型（LoopLM）的架构特性：模型反复应用共享的 Transformer 层，在参数量不增加的情况下增加有效计算深度，从而允许额外的潜在计算。评估设置包括：(1) 在同一 LoopLM 家族内改变循环深度，以隔离额外循环计算对 CoT 可监控性的影响；(2) 将 LoopLM 与非循环语言模型在参数量、Transformer 层数或有效深度上匹配后进行比较，以研究 LoopLM 是否系统性地更难以监控。评估覆盖 MonitorBench 的 8 项任务，并在标准设置与压力测试设置下进行。诊断分析考察任务难度、验证通过率、生成 token 长度等因素，并辅以定性样例分析更深循环模型如何显式使用或归因所提供的线索。研究未披露具体训练目标、推理服务流程或硬件资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次系统评估循环语言模型中的 CoT 可监控性；通过同一 LoopLM 家族内改变循环深度隔离额外循环计算的影响；在参数量、Transformer 层数或有效深度匹配条件下对比 LoopLM 与非循环语言模型；诊断表明可监控性下降不能完全由任务难度、验证通过率或生成长度解释，并发现更深循环模型对提示线索的使用/归因方式变化

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring provides a promising approach for detecting undesirable model behavior. Looped language models (LoopLMs) repeatedly apply shared transformer layers, increasing effective computational depth and enabling additional latent computation without increasing model size. However, the effect of looped architectures on CoT monitorability remains largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of CoT monitorability in LoopLMs. We study two complementary settings: (1) varying the loop depth within the same LoopLM family to isolate the effect of additional recurrent computation, and (2) comparing LoopLMs with non-looped language models matched by parameter size, transformer-layer count, or effective depth to study whether LoopLMs are less monitorable. Across eight tasks from MonitorBench and both standard and stress-test settings, we observe task-dependent reductions in CoT monitorability under stress tests on specific Logic/Science/Engineering \texttt{Cue Answer} tasks, while other tasks exhibit weaker or qualitatively different trends. Our diagnosis suggests that these declines are not fully explained by task difficulty, verification pass rate, or generated token length; qualitative examples further suggest changes in how deeper-loop models explicitly use or attribute provided cues. Our cross-model comparison finds no evidence that LoopLMs are systematically less monitorable than non-looped language models matched on size or depth. Overall, our results suggest that deeper loop depth can reduce CoT monitorability in some tasks under stress tests, but looped transformer architecture alone does not necessarily imply lower monitorability.

</details>


### [81] [Learning to Revise Reasoning with Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02703)
*Yuxiang Zhang,Ding Cao,Shuting Cui,Lei Wang,Weijieying Ren,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seg-OPD 针对 token 级在线策略蒸馏无法显式给出连贯改写步骤、退化前缀会强化错误推理的问题，基于不确定性选择学生推理片段并用教师重写，训练学生偏好教师重写同时保留 token 级 OPD 监督。在数学推理与竞赛编程上，改写成功率优于基线，推理准确率平均相对提升 5.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）按 token 提供密集监督，但未显式给出如何改写学生中间推理步骤以改善后续推理的连贯替代方案；且当学生产生退化推理前缀时，后续教师监督仍以该前缀为条件，可能强化错误推理模式。

Method: Seg-OPD 建立在在线策略蒸馏（OPD）框架之上。输入为学生模型自身生成的推理轨迹（rollout），教师模型对这些轨迹提供监督。流程包含两部分：1）段级选择与重写：使用不确定性度量从学生 rollout 中选取候选推理片段（segment），并让教师模型针对这些片段生成重写（redraft）；2）偏好训练：构造教师重写片段与其配对学生片段的比较对，训练学生模型偏好教师重写，从而学习『改写推理』的能力；同时保留原有的密集 token-wise OPD 监督，使学生在 token 级别仍对齐教师分布。训练目标因此由 token 级蒸馏损失与段级偏好目标共同组成。推理时学生模型按常规自回归方式生成，不依赖额外教师调用。该方法旨在解决 token 级 OPD 缺乏连贯替代步骤、以及退化前缀导致错误被强化的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理准确率（平均相对提升）", "value": "5.22%", "setting": "与当前最优基线在多种模型和任务（数学推理与竞赛编程）上对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过受控推理干预实验发现：用教师重写片段替换学生片段可提升后续推理准确率，为『学习改写推理』提供依据。；提出 Seg-OPD：基于不确定性度量选择学生推理片段，并获取对应的教师重写（redraft）。；训练学生偏好教师重写而非配对学生片段，同时保留密集 token-wise OPD 监督。；在数学推理与竞赛编程任务上验证改写成功率与推理准确率提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves large language model reasoning by training students on their own rollouts with dense token-wise supervision from the teacher. However, token-wise OPD does not explicitly provide a coherent alternative reasoning step showing how the student's step could be revised to improve subsequent reasoning. Furthermore, this paradigm can become less effective when the student produces a degenerate reasoning prefix, as subsequent teacher supervision remains conditioned on that prefix and may reinforce poor reasoning patterns. In this work, we focus on learning reasoning revision with segment-wise OPD to rework intermediate reasoning steps and better support subsequent reasoning. Through controlled reasoning interventions, we find that replacing student segments with teacher redrafts improves subsequent reasoning accuracy. Therefore, we address the problem of turning teacher redrafts into explicit supervision for learning to revise reasoning. We propose Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation (Seg-OPD), which selects student segments based on an uncertainty metric and obtains corresponding teacher redrafts. Seg-OPD trains the student to prefer teacher redrafts over their paired student segments while retaining dense token-wise OPD supervision. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and competitive programming tasks show that Seg-OPD-trained students achieve higher revision success rates than baselines. Seg-OPD consistently outperforms the compared state-of-the-art baselines in reasoning accuracy with an average relative improvement of 5.22% across diverse models and tasks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [Decoupling Memory from Context: Structured Memory for Token-Efficient Test-Time Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02687)
*Yehya Farhat,Michael Desmond,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GraphMemory，将智能体记忆更新形式化为上下文优化过程，并用轻量图结构累积可复用策略；每查询仅检索相关子图，使检索记忆量不随样本数增长，在保持下游性能的同时减少约 81-85% 记忆构建 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线上下文适应通常需要昂贵的试错过程，且查询独立处理导致经验无法跨交互累积；持续向共享上下文追加信息又带来 token 成本增长、上下文窗口限制和性能退化。

Method: GraphMemory 是一个轻量级图结构记忆系统，用于 LLM 智能体的测试时持续学习。系统将可复用策略作为节点，通过图结构累积、精炼、组织并连接这些策略。输入为用户的查询以及跨交互累积的历史策略；处理流程上，首先构建并更新记忆图，将新策略写入图中并建立与已有策略的关联；对于每个新查询，系统不将全量记忆塞入上下文，而是检索与当前查询相关的子图，仅将相关子图作为上下文输入给 LLM，从而实现在线上下文适应。论文将记忆系统更新解释为对模型上下文的一种优化更新过程，以此统一形式化上下文优化问题。在训练目标方面，本文不涉及模型权重更新，而是通过上下文工程在推理时改善模型行为。推理路径为：查询进入 → 相关子图检索 → 仅暴露相关子图作为上下文 → LLM 生成回答并将新经验/策略回写图记忆。由于采用有界检索，检索到的记忆量随已处理样本数增长保持恒定，因此避免了共享上下文持续膨胀带来的 token 成本、窗口限制与性能退化问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "memory-construction tokens", "value": "减少约 81-85%", "setting": "与论文所设基线对比，具体数据集与模型规模未在摘要中披露"}, {"metric": "downstream performance", "value": "competitive（与基线相当）", "setting": "下游任务表现，具体指标未在摘要中披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将智能体记忆系统更新统一形式化为对模型上下文的优化更新过程，为记忆设计提供原则性框架；提出 GraphMemory：轻量级图结构记忆，累积、精炼、组织并连接可复用策略；每查询仅检索相关子图，实现无需暴露全量记忆的在线上下文适应；在有界检索下，检索到的记忆量随处理样本数增长保持恒定；相比基线减少约 81-85% 的记忆构建 token 消耗

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise, scientific, and medical applications, where agents must incorporate domain-specific knowledge and adapt from experience. Context engineering offers a practical alternative to weight updates by improving model behavior through instructions, strategies, and evidence supplied at inference time. However, adapting context online typically requires a costly trial-and-error process, while queries are often processed independently, preventing useful experience from carrying forward. Memory systems address this limitation by retaining information across interactions, but approaches that continually append information to a shared context face increasing token costs, context-window limits, and performance degradation as the context expands. We introduce a unified formulation of context optimization and show that an agent memory system update can be interpreted as an optimization update procedure over the model's context. This perspective attempts to provide a principled framework for studying memory design and its efficiency. We then propose GraphMemory, a lightweight graph-based memory that accumulates, refines, organizes, and connects reusable strategies. For each query, GraphMemory retrieves only the relevant subgraph, enabling online context adaptation without exposing the model to the entire memory. Under bounded retrieval, the amount of retrieved memory remains constant as the number of processed examples grows. Experiments show that GraphMemory achieves competitive downstream performance while using approximately 81-85% fewer memory-construction tokens than our baselines.

</details>


### [83] [Spend Teacher Tokens Where They Matter: Success-Referenced On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02678)
*Xiang Chen,Futao Su,Kong Wang,Jiayi Chen,TanLin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SR-OPD 通过让学生成功 rollout 作为参考，优先监督与成功轨迹持续分歧的失败 rollout，从而将 on-policy distillation 的教师输入 token 降至 Vanilla OPD 的 3.46–5.02%，并保持可比的数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation 需要为每个 student rollout 提供 token 级 teacher 监督，导致教师计算成本过高；如何在固定教师输入预算下，选择哪些 prompt 和 rollout 值得接受教师监督，从而在保持推理性能的同时显著降低教师 token 开销。

Method: SR-OPD 面向 on-policy distillation 流程：student 模型对给定 prompt 生成多个 rollout；系统判断同一 prompt 下是否同时存在成功 rollout 与失败 rollout。若存在，成功 rollout 被用作自然参考。对失败 rollout，方法比较其隐藏状态轨迹与成功参考的隐藏状态轨迹，识别出现持续分歧的失败 rollout，并优先将其纳入教师监督范围。同时，选择过程会考虑估计的教师输入成本，在固定教师输入预算下决定哪些 prompt 与 rollout 接受 teacher token 级监督。未被选中的 rollout 不消耗教师前向计算。训练目标仍为 teacher 对学生 rollout 的 token 级蒸馏监督，但监督分配由成功参考与分歧程度驱动。推理或部署路径与标准 student 模型一致，教师仅用于训练阶段的监督分配，因此不增加线上推理开销。该方法在 one-pass 设置下通过减少教师输入 token 实现与 Vanilla OPD 可比的数学推理性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "teacher-input token 使用比例", "value": "3.46–5.02% of Vanilla OPD", "setting": "one-pass 设置，三个 teacher-student 对，六个数学推理 benchmark，维持可比推理性能"}, {"metric": "控制实验支持两个设计选择", "value": "在匹配 Vanilla OPD 5% teacher-input 预算下，支持聚焦同时含成功与失败 rollout 的 prompt，以及用成功 rollout 指导失败选择", "setting": "受控实验，固定教师输入预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 成功参考的 on-policy 蒸馏（SR-OPD）：当同一 prompt 下 student 同时产生成功与失败 rollout 时，用成功 rollout 作为选择失败 rollout 的自然参考。；基于隐藏状态轨迹持续分歧的失败 rollout 优先级：优先监督与成功参考隐藏状态轨迹出现持续偏离的失败 rollout。；结合估计的教师输入成本进行监督分配，在有限教师输入预算下选择 prompt/rollout。；在 one-pass 设置下，仅使用 Vanilla OPD 3.46–5.02% 的教师输入 token 即可保持可比数学推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but providing such supervision for every rollout requires substantial teacher computation. We introduce Success-Referenced On-Policy Distillation (SR-OPD), which reduces this cost by selecting which prompts and rollouts receive teacher supervision. When the student produces both successful and failed rollouts for the same prompt, a successful rollout can serve as a natural reference for selecting failed rollouts. SR-OPD therefore focuses on such prompts and prioritizes failed rollouts whose hidden-state trajectories show sustained divergence from a successful reference, while accounting for estimated teacher-input cost. Across three teacher-student pairs and six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SR-OPD uses only 3.46-5.02% of the teacher-input tokens required by Vanilla OPD in the one-pass setting while maintaining comparable reasoning performance. Under a controlled setting matched to 5% of Vanilla OPD's teacher-input budget, further experiments support both key design choices: focusing supervision on prompts with both successful and failed rollouts, and using successful rollouts to guide failure selection. These results indicate that a student's own successful behavior can serve as a useful reference for allocating teacher supervision under a fixed teacher-input budget.

</details>


### [84] [Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03185)
*Han Cui,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Hongbo Zhang,Zhizhang Fu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从强化学习视角解释 on-policy distillation 的性能增益与崩溃现象：教师隐式奖励学生行为，当奖励与质量对齐时提升采样效率，错位时则引发 reward hacking 导致生成过长重复。通过屏蔽不健康回答和 SFT 初始化可缓解崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从强化学习视角解释 on-policy distillation 为何有时提升性能、有时却崩溃为过长且重复的生成，并找出缓解崩溃的方法。

Method: 论文从强化学习角度分析 on-policy distillation（OPD）。OPD 的流程为：学生模型在自身策略下生成 rollouts，教师模型对这些 rollouts 提供反馈（token 级分布或偏好信号），学生通过最小化与教师分布的差异来更新。作者将教师的反馈视为一个隐式奖励模型，奖励学生行为中教师偏好的部分，即使教师本身很少生成该类文本。在这种设定下，若隐式奖励与生成质量一致，OPD 使学生更易采样到正确回答，表现为性能提升；若偏好与质量错位，则出现 reward hacking，模型放大过长、重复的 student rollouts，导致崩溃。基于该诊断，作者提出两种缓解措施：训练时屏蔽不健康回答（如过长的低质量 rollout）和使用 SFT 初始化。实验验证了这些措施能有效缓解崩溃，并指出 OPD 的效果更多取决于教师评估学生 rollouts 的可靠性，而非教师生成质量本身。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 on-policy distillation 形式化为教师隐式奖励学生行为的过程，即使教师自身很少展示该行为；发现 OPD 提升性能通常不扩展学生能力，而是使正确回答更易被采样；当隐式奖励与质量错位时发生 reward hacking，放大过长、重复的 rollout；提出在训练中屏蔽不健康回答以及使用 SFT 初始化来有效缓解崩溃

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [85] [Learning What to Investigate Next: Meta-Reasoning for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02525)
*Ankur Samanta,Yonathan Efroni,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA将长时程研究智能体拆分为外层元推理器与内层执行器：元推理器从持久研究记录中整理上下文并下达工作指令，执行器逐条完成调查。决策边界被用作信用分配单元，用以训练生成式critic预测剩余回报，并获得可跨环境迁移的部分进展估值先验；MIRA-AC进一步用该先验初始化生成式actor-critic，无需独立critic即可联合预测回报与选择下一步调查，把策略优化集中在元推理决策上。在定理证明与开放式神经网络架构研究等四个autoresearch环境中提升gold表现，展示了把元推理学习为显式策略来指导长时程自主研究的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程研究智能体在证据逐步累积时，需要同时决定“如何调查”和“下一步调查什么”；这类元决策在长执行轨迹中信号稀疏，其后果往往在多次调查之后才显现，导致难以学习。

Method: MIRA采用分层结构，将“研究分配”与“执行”分离。外层元推理器维护一份持久的研究记录（research record），在每一步从该记录中整理出上下文，然后输出一份工作指令（work order），指定下一次调查内容，或者决定结束整个回合。内层执行器是全新的（fresh）实例，每次只负责执行当前工作指令，因此执行过程本身构成了两个元推理动作之间的状态转移。该设计使元推理决策边界天然成为信用分配单元：在每个边界处训练一个生成式critic，从部分状态预测期望剩余回报，作者报告其效果优于token级替代方案；跨环境预训练进一步提升回报预测与适应能力，形成一个可迁移的部分进展估值先验。MIRA-AC则用这一先验初始化一个生成式actor-critic，联合训练它预测剩余回报并选择下一次调查，无需单独critic模型，从而把策略优化集中在元推理决策上，避免直接优化其引发的更长执行轨迹。训练信号来自模型自身的代理爬山信号，actor可借助跨环境价值初始化进行迁移。推理阶段：元推理器基于研究记录生成工作指令，交由新的内层执行器执行，执行结果写回研究记录，循环推进直至元推理器选择结束回合。该架构通过分离分配与执行、显式建模部分进展价值，解决了长时程研究中决策稀疏、后果延迟的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: MIRA分层架构：将研究分配（外层元推理器）与执行（内层执行器）解耦，元推理器从持久研究记录中整理上下文并写出工作指令或结束回合，每次执行构成元推理动作之间的状态转移；以元推理决策边界作为自然信用分配单元，在每个边界训练生成式critic预测部分状态下的期望剩余回报，优于token级替代方案；跨环境预训练提升剩余回报预测与适应能力，得到可迁移的“部分进展估值”先验；MIRA-AC：用上述先验初始化生成式actor-critic，联合预测剩余回报并选择下一步调查，无需单独的critic模型，将策略优化集中在元推理决策而非长执行轨迹上；在模型自身的代理爬山信号（proxy hill-climbing signals）上训练MIRA-AC，提升四个autoresearch环境的gold performance，且actor可跨环境迁移

Abstract: Long-horizon research agents must decide both how to investigate and what to investigate next as evidence accumulates. This is hard to learn because such decisions are sparse in long execution traces, and their consequences may emerge several investigations later. We introduce Meta-reasoning for Iterative Research Agents (MIRA), a hierarchical architecture separating research allocation from execution. An outer-loop meta-reasoner curates context from a persistent research record, then writes a work order for the next investigation or ends the episode. A fresh inner-loop executor carries out each work order, making execution part of the transition between meta-reasoning actions. Without policy training, MIRA improves long-horizon inference and allocates additional compute more effectively in theorem proving and open-ended neural-architecture research. Its decision boundaries also provide natural units for credit assignment. At each boundary, we train a generative critic to forecast expected remaining return from partial states, outperforming token-level alternatives. Cross-environment pretraining improves forecasting and adaptation, yielding a transferable prior for valuing partial progress. We use this prior to initialize MIRA-AC, a generative actor-critic jointly trained to forecast remaining return and choose the next investigation, without a separate critic model. MIRA-AC concentrates policy optimization on meta-reasoning decisions, enabling efficient long-horizon reinforcement learning without directly optimizing the longer execution traces they initiate. Training MIRA-AC on the model's own proxy hill-climbing signals improves gold performance across four autoresearch environments; the actor transfers with cross-environment value initialization. Together, these results show that meta-reasoning can be learned as an explicit policy for directing long-horizon autonomous research.

</details>


### [86] [Mitigating Social Sycophancy via Pluralistic Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02568)
*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Myra Cheng,Xiaoxuan Hou,Qian Hu,Rahul Gupta,Natasha Jaques,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PlurPO，让模型自行模拟多方利益相关者并据此构造偏好数据，无需真值即可缓解个人建议场景的社会性谄媚，在四个数据集与四个模型族上将伤害意图认可率平均降低 89%，并把一般建议的认可率差距从 17.8% 缩小到 8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会性谄媚：在无客观真值的个人建议场景，语言模型过度认可用户立场，造成用户过度自信并降低修复关系意愿。

Method: PlurPO 以人际冲突描述为输入，先让语言模型识别并模拟受用户行为影响的相关利益相关者，再基于模型自身对这些回复的评判生成偏好对，训练模型偏好并生成对所有利益相关者可接受的回复。训练不依赖人工真值标签，仅利用模型对自身输出产生的信号构造偏好数据，随后进行偏好优化。推理时模型按训练目标生成兼顾多方视角的回复。8B 模型构建的偏好数据集可迁移到 32B 模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在表达伤害意图的陈述上的认可率降低", "value": "平均降低 89%", "setting": "四个模型族、四个数据集平均"}, {"metric": "一般建议问题上的认可率差距缩小", "value": "从 17.8% 降至 8.0%，缩小超过一半", "setting": "四个模型平均，目标为匹配人类回复认可率"}, {"metric": "偏好数据集跨规模迁移效果", "value": "有效缓解 32B 模型谄媚", "setting": "8B 模型构建的偏好数据用于 32B 模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: PlurPO 让模型自行识别并模拟受用户行为影响的相关利益相关者，训练其偏好对各方都可接受的回复；仅使用模型对自身输出产生的信号，无需人工真值标签构造偏好数据；将 8B 模型构建的偏好数据集迁移到 32B 模型，验证跨规模泛化

Abstract: Personal advice, including relationship advice, now ranks among the most common uses of generative AI. But language models (LMs) exhibit sycophancy: they affirm users much more often than humans do, which can make people overconfident and less willing to repair their relationships after a conflict. Prior work on mitigating sycophancy has focused on factual settings where a response can be checked against a ground truth answer, while mitigations for social sycophancy (e.g., personal advice, where there is no ground truth) have relied on simple prompting and post-training methods with limited effectiveness. Our insight is that social sycophancy occurs in part because LMs overly center on the user and fail to consider the perspectives of other stakeholders impacted by the user's behavior. To address this problem we propose Pluralistic Preference Optimization (PlurPO): given inputs describing interpersonal conflicts, the LM identifies and simulates the relevant stakeholders, and is then trained to prefer and generate responses acceptable to all stakeholders. PlurPO uses only signals the model produces about its own outputs, without ground-truth labels. PlurPO substantially reduces social sycophancy across four datasets and four model families compared to prior methods. For example, on statements of intent to cause harm, where the users' actions should not be endorsed, PlurPO reduces the endorsement rate by 89% on average across four models. On general advice questions, where the target is to match the endorsement rate of human responses, it closes the gap by more than half, from 17.8% to 8.0% on average. The preference dataset constructed by PlurPO for an 8B model also effectively transfers to mitigating sycophancy in a larger (32B) model. Our results indicate that social sycophancy can be reduced by leveraging a model's own capabilities to simulate a plurality of relevant perspectives.

</details>


### [87] [FSPO: Policy-Consistent Risk and Pareto-Feasible Control for Budgeted LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02828)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FSPO提出了一种用于预算化LLM RL后训练的自适应控制器，通过策略一致的风险模型、决策条件轨迹校准和帕累托资源延续证书，联合解决风险估计不一致、校准漂移和多资源可行性问题，在匹配的GRPO资源下提升了准确率并降低了校准误差和轨迹失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应LLM强化学习后训练中，多个训练执行器（如rollout温度、group大小、裁剪、KL正则、验证器分配和更新预算）在线变化时，存在三个耦合问题：风险模型与部署控制器不一致、选择性动作导致分数校准漂移、独立资源最低成本无法保证多资源可行延续。

Method: FSPO是一个用于预算化LLM RL后训练的反馈状态控制器。它学习一个策略一致的风险到go模型，其Bellman目标使用与未来决策相同的冻结控制器，从而确保风险估计与部署策略一致。同时学习一个长时域效用模型。决策条件轨迹校准（DCTC）在由临时控制器选择的动作生成的交叉拟合轨迹上校准风险，以应对选择性动作导致的校准漂移。帕累托资源延续证书（PRCC）在允许动作之前，检查剩余时域内是否存在非支配的累积预留，从而保证多资源可行性。训练目标涉及风险模型和效用模型的优化，推理时根据当前反馈状态选择动作。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out accuracy", "value": "66.11%", "setting": "matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO"}, {"metric": "OOD accuracy", "value": "59.43%", "setting": "matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO"}, {"metric": "held-out accuracy", "value": "64.47%", "setting": "matched GRPO resource envelope, PB2 (strongest evaluated adaptive baseline)"}, {"metric": "OOD accuracy", "value": "57.03%", "setting": "matched GRPO resource envelope, PB2 (strongest evaluated adaptive baseline)"}, {"metric": "selected-decision ECE", "value": "0.053", "setting": "high behavior-deployment mismatch, FSPO with policy-consistent risk (baseline 0.108)"}, {"metric": "selected-decision ECE", "value": "0.022", "setting": "matched acceptance, DCTC (baseline 0.039)"}, {"metric": "false-feasible admissions", "value": "0.000", "setting": "18-action catalog, PRCC (baseline 0.197)"}, {"metric": "trajectory failure", "value": "0.083", "setting": "factorial ablation, all three components enabled (baseline 0.181)"}, {"metric": "held-out accuracy gain over contextual bandit", "value": "+2.42 percentage points", "setting": "three paired training seeds"}, {"metric": "OOD accuracy gain over contextual bandit", "value": "+3.19 percentage points", "setting": "three paired training seeds"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 策略一致的风险到go模型，其Bellman目标跟随用于未来决策的同一冻结控制器；长时域效用模型；决策条件轨迹校准（DCTC），在临时控制器选择的动作生成的交叉拟合轨迹上校准风险；帕累托资源延续证书（PRCC），仅当剩余时域内非支配累积预留可行时才允许动作

Abstract: Adaptive LLM reinforcement-learning post-training changes multiple training actuators online, including rollout temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, and update budget. Three coupled issues remain unresolved. A future-risk model trained from behavior trajectories need not estimate the risk induced by the controller that will be deployed; a score calibrated on logged state-action pairs can become miscalibrated after selective action choice; and independent per-resource minimum costs do not in general certify a feasible multi-resource continuation. We introduce FSPO, a feedback-state controller for budgeted LLM RL post-training that addresses these issues jointly. FSPO learns a policy-consistent risk-to-go model whose Bellman target follows the same frozen controller used for future decisions, together with a long-horizon utility model. Decision-conditioned trajectory calibration (DCTC) calibrates risk on cross-fitted trajectories generated by actions selected by provisional controllers. A Pareto resource continuation certificate (PRCC) admits an action only when a non-dominated cumulative reservation remains feasible over the residual horizon. Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO reaches 66.11% held-out and 59.43% OOD accuracy, compared with 64.47% and 57.03% for PB2, the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline. Three paired training seeds give gains of +2.42 and +3.19 percentage points over the contextual bandit on held-out and OOD evaluation. Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.

</details>


### [88] [VERSE: Verified Self-Evolving Optimizer for Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02616)
*Zekai Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Zekun Wang,Hai Wang,Yuhui Xu,Joshua Frandsen,Shancong Fu,Ashia C. Wilson,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERSE introduces a verified self-evolving optimizer that improves LLM agent harnesses by iteratively testing edits, replaying failures, and tracking regressions, without altering model weights. It outperforms baselines on held-out SWE-rebench and out-of-distribution tasks across five languages.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whether an optimizer can improve an LLM agent harness more effectively by also improving how it diagnoses failures, develops edits, and verifies their effects, without updating model weights.

Method: VERSE is a verified self-evolving optimizer for agent harnesses. The optimizer and executor model weights remain fixed. The optimizer improves an executor agent by evolving the executor harness (prompts, tools, workflow) and simultaneously evolving its own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes. Key to its operation is an execution-based verification loop: the optimizer can test draft edits, replay failures, and perturb suspected steps before final submission. It tracks fixes and regressions across optimization rounds, using this feedback to revise both the executor and itself. Five executors were studied; self-evolving optimizers built their own tools for failure analysis, verification, training audits, and workflow control. Evaluation followed a shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks. Best validation-selected harness results were 42.3% on held-out SWE-rebench and 37.7% on newer out-of-distribution tasks across five languages.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "42.3% (validation-selected harness) vs. 39.2% strongest baseline", "setting": "held-out SWE-rebench tasks"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "37.7% (validation-selected harness) vs. 29.3% strongest baseline", "setting": "newer out-of-distribution tasks in five languages"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Verified self-evolving optimizer that tests draft edits, replays failures, and perturbs suspected steps before submission；Tracking of fixes and regressions across rounds to revise both the executor harness and the optimizer's own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes；Execution-based verification as a critical enabler for optimizer self-evolution；Shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks across five executors

Abstract: Harness evolution improves an LLM agent's prompts, tools, and workflow, while the optimizer's own tools and procedures often remain fixed. We study whether an optimizer can improve another agent more effectively by also improving how it diagnoses failures, develops edits, and tests their effects. Two observations guide our design. In a controlled study, optimizer self-evolution fails to improve performance without execution-based verification, but achieves the best result of that study when verification is available. Across five executors, self-evolving optimizers build their own tools for failure analysis, verification, training audits, and workflow control. Motivated by these findings, we introduce VERSE, a Verified Self-Evolving optimizer for agent harnesses. VERSE lets the optimizer test draft edits, replay failures, and perturb suspected steps before submission, while tracking fixes and regressions across rounds. Using this feedback, the optimizer revises both the executor harness and its own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes, while the weights of the optimizer and executor models stay fixed. Under a shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks, VERSE improves all four evaluated harness optimizers on held-out SWE-rebench tasks and newer out-of-distribution tasks in five languages. Its best validation-selected harness reaches 42.3% and 37.7% accuracy, respectively, against 39.2% and 29.3% for the strongest baselines. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [Choosing Before Acting: Comparative Value Estimation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02330)
*Yu Li,Zheng Zhang,Xin Liu,Shengtian Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CITA 提出在执行前对候选工具调用做比较式长程价值估计，训练 Comparative Inference Model（CIM），融合观测轨迹、贝叶斯工具图模拟器监督和 LLM 语义比较，以改善长程工具使用智能体的信用分配，在三个工具使用基准和多个骨干 LLM 上一致提升 Tool F1 与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程工具使用中，仅靠最终结果奖励难以对中间工具调用进行有效信用分配；而步级奖励通常依赖人工/LLM 判断或额外 rollout 来估计下游影响，成本高且不完备。论文主张在执行前比较估计下一次工具调用的长程价值。

Method: 论文提出的 CITA 架构包含一个 Comparative Inference Model（CIM）。输入是当前对话/任务上下文以及候选的下一次工具调用；CIM 学习输出该候选调用在当前上下文下支持最终任务成功的可能性，即步级长程价值估计。训练信号由三部分配对构成：1）实际观测到的工具行为（logged trajectories 中真实执行的调用）；2）来自贝叶斯工具图模拟器的可扩展监督，用于生成替代调用及其下游效果；3）LLM 基于语义比较的判断。这些配对信号使 CIM 能够在同一上下文下对替代工具调用进行相对比较，而不仅仅是模仿已执行的单一轨迹。训练目标为比较式学习，使 CIM 能对候选调用排序/打分。推理/决策流程中，智能体在执行前先用 CIM 对可能的下一步工具调用进行价值估计和比较，再据此选择执行哪一个调用，从而改善长程工具使用中的信用分配。摘要未披露具体网络结构、参数量、训练数据规模、硬件或推理延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Tool F1", "value": "在三个工具使用基准、多个 LLM 骨干上一致提升（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "离线基准评估"}, {"metric": "task success", "value": "在三个工具使用基准、多个 LLM 骨干上一致提升（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "离线基准评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents（CITA），在执行前估计候选下一次工具调用的长程价值；训练 Comparative Inference Model（CIM），从成对信号中学习同一上下文下替代工具调用的比较监督；将观测到的工具行为、基于贝叶斯工具图模拟器的可扩展监督、以及 LLM 语义比较判断相结合构建训练对

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on long-horizon tool invocation sequences for complex tasks, where each invocation can alter the task state and condition subsequent decisions. In long-horizon tool use, final-outcome rewards provide weak credit assignment over long interaction traces. Step-level rewards can offer more targeted feedback, but obtaining reliable step supervision often requires human or LLM judgment, or additional rollouts to estimate the downstream effect of an intermediate decision. In this paper, we argue that effective tool-use agents should estimate the long-horizon value of a possible next tool invocation before executing it. This objective requires comparative supervision over alternative invocations under the same context, while logged trajectories only contain the invocation that was actually taken. Therefore, we propose Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents (CITA). CITA trains a Comparative Inference Model (CIM) from paired signals that combine observed tool behavior, scalable supervision from a Bayesian tool-graph simulator, and semantic judgments from LLM-based comparison. The resulting CIM learns to estimate how likely a possible next tool invocation is to support final task success under the current context. Across three tool-use benchmarks and multiple backbone LLMs, CITA consistently improves Tool F1 and task success. Additional analysis shows that CIM learns accurate step-level value estimates for comparative tool choices.

</details>

### [90] [When History Misleads: Asymmetric Margin Supervision for Instruction-Guided LLM Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02600)
*Ming Yin,Yuhan Yang,Chen Chen,Xinyu Lin,Wentao Shi,Fangcong Yin,Chaofei Yang,Chao Yang,Jiyan Yang,Hui Zhang,Ning Jiang,Yiran Chen,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AIMS 将历史事件移除对排序的因果效应转化为裕度监督，通过冻结参考模型选择请求特定的删除并施加非对称损失，在保留完整历史输入的同时提升目标与竞争物品的排序裕度。在六个LLM骨干、工业数据集和两个公开基准上改善了 Recall 与 NDCG，推理无需额外搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史事件对推荐的影响不能直接作为正确监督信号：最有影响的删除事件未必支持目标物品；删除误导事件虽提升目标分数，却可能更大幅提升竞争物品分数，因此仅提高目标分数不等于更好的排序。

Method: AIMS 构建于指令引导的生成式推荐框架之上。输入包括用户当前请求、完整交互历史以及候选物品集合；模型（LLM 骨干）生成推荐排序分数。训练时，引入一个冻结的参考模型，针对每个请求通过删除单个历史事件来评估对目标物品分数及竞争物品裕度的影响。若某请求已被正确排序，则寻找能同时提升目标分数并增大对截断点附近竞争物品裕度的删除，将该裕度作为监督目标。完整历史仍作为模型输入，不进行实际删除。训练目标由交叉熵损失与一个非对称辅助损失组成：辅助损失惩罚实际裕度低于目标裕度的情况，且梯度仅通过竞争物品的分数反向传播，避免直接扰动目标分数。推理阶段与基线模型完全一致，无需搜索或编辑历史，保持低延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Recall", "value": "提升", "setting": "六个LLM骨干，工业数据集与两个公开基准，与强基线对比"}, {"metric": "NDCG", "value": "提升", "setting": "六个LLM骨干，工业数据集与两个公开基准，与强基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将个体历史事件移除的效应转化为排序裕度监督（Asymmetric Intervention-Guided Margin Supervision, AIMS）；使用冻结参考模型为每个请求识别能同时提升目标分数及其对截断点附近竞争物品裕度的删除事件，并将该裕度作为训练目标；训练时保留完整历史作为输入，仅用辅助损失传递监督，推理无需编辑或搜索历史；设计非对称辅助损失：惩罚裕度不足，并将梯度仅路由至竞争物品分数

Abstract: In instruction-guided generative recommendation, LLM-based recommenders need to balance two goals: responding to the user's current request and aligning with the preferences in their interaction history. When the two conflict, history events can override the request. We show that turning the effect of individual history events into supervision faces two obstacles. First, the events that most influence a recommendation are not necessarily the ones that support the target item. Second, removing a misleading event can raise the target's score but a competing item's score even more, so a higher target score alone does not guarantee a better ranking. We propose Asymmetric Intervention-Guided Margin Supervision (AIMS), which converts the effect of removing individual history events into ranking supervision. For training requests already ranked correctly, a frozen reference model identifies request-specific deletions that improve both the target's score and its margin over a competitor near the recommendation cutoff. These margins serve as training targets, while the complete history is retained as input. Training combines cross-entropy with an asymmetric auxiliary loss that penalizes margin shortfalls and routes its gradient only through the competitor score. Inference is unchanged, requiring no history editing or deletion search. Across six LLM backbones on an industrial dataset and two public benchmarks, AIMS improves Recall and NDCG over strong baselines. Ablations support request-specific margins and asymmetric supervision, and the selected deletions preferentially remove constraint-violating history.

</details>


### [91] [Learning Query Encoders Can Be Hard Even When Vector Retrieval Is Geometrically Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02749)
*Anders Wikum,Nina Mishra,Amin Saberi,Tal Wagner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究冻结文档索引下向量检索的几何容量上限与查询编码器可学习性之间的差距。在多个真实检索基准上，单向量查询编码器的召回率远低于文档索引可支持的理论上限；同时给出理论证据，证明学习查询编码器在统计查询模型下需要指数级查询，表明检索基准中存在大量未利用的几何容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定文档索引下，学习到的单向量查询编码器能否达到该索引可支持的最大召回率；若不能，查询编码器学习的计算难度是什么。

Method: 论文未提出完整生产系统架构，而是围绕向量检索的两个部分展开：冻结的文档索引（document index）与待学习的查询编码器（query encoder）。文档索引通过向量相似度支持检索；查询编码器将查询映射到同一嵌入空间，使查询向量靠近其期望文档。论文首先在多个真实世界检索基准上测量固定文档索引所能支持的最大召回率，并与实际学习到的单向量查询编码器的召回率对比。理论部分构造一个检索任务：该任务存在一个完美召回的查询编码器，且可由小规模单隐层 ReLU 网络表示；但任何统计查询学习器要达到优于随机基线 k/n 的非平凡召回，需要指数级数量的统计查询。由此论证检索基准中可能存在大量未实现的几何容量，并将查询编码器可学习性作为嵌入检索的潜在障碍。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出以冻结文档索引下的最大召回率作为几何容量定义，并实证显示单向量查询编码器常远低于该上限；构造一个检索任务，证明存在完美召回且可由小规模单隐层 ReLU 网络表示的查询编码器；证明任何统计查询学习器要达到非平凡召回优势需要指数级统计查询，从计算学习角度解释查询编码器不可学性

Abstract: Efficient vector retrieval requires both a corpus geometry that supports retrieving the right documents through vector similarity, and a query encoder that can embed queries near their desired documents in the embedding space. Recent work has studied geometric capacity through the lens of the minimum embedding dimension needed to realize all top-$k$ answer sets of $n$ documents. We study a different notion of geometric capacity--the maximum recall achievable for a frozen document index--and explore whether learned query encoders can reach this ceiling. On several real-world retrieval benchmarks, we show that retrieval quality of single-vector query encoders often lies far below what the document indices can support. Motivated by this observation, we give theoretical evidence that learning query encoders can be computationally hard. In particular, we construct a retrieval task that (1) admits a query encoder with perfect recall which is representable by a small one-hidden-layer ReLU network, but (2) any statistical-query learner (a class capturing learners that access training data through aggregate statistics) provably requires exponentially many statistical queries to achieve non-trivial recall advantage over the random baseline $k/n$. Taken together, our results suggest substantial unrealized geometric capacity in retrieval benchmarks and establish query encoder learnability as a possible barrier in embedding-based retrieval.

</details>


### [92] [Benchmarking Literature Retrieval for a Model Organism: A Dictyostelium Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03130)
*Yun Wang,Gad Shaulsky,Tomaž Curk,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为 Dictyostelium 模式生物构建了一个基于 dictyBase 策展查询的文献检索基准，并实证比较交叉编码器重排、基因感知查询扩展以及全文与摘要检索的效果，为领域特定检索系统设计提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文献稀疏、术语高度领域特定的模式生物（Dictyostelium）知识库中，如何有效评估并利用交叉编码器重排、基因感知查询扩展以及全文与摘要检索来提升文献检索质量。

Method: 论文提出一个面向 Dictyostelium 的文献检索基准和方法研究。数据来自 dictyBase 策展人编写的生物查询、对应的 PubMed 索引文章以及结构化基因注释。检索流程从查询开始，可选地使用基因注释进行查询扩展以缓解词汇不匹配；随后基于摘要或全文分块进行候选检索；再应用交叉编码器对候选文档进行重排。研究对这三个因素（重排、查询扩展、摘要与全文）进行对比评估，报告了它们在候选召回和 top-rank 性能上的选择性增益。未披露具体的模型架构、参数规模、训练目标或推理服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发布来自 dictyBase 的检索基准，包含策展人编写的生物查询与 PubMed 文章链接及结构化基因注释；系统评估交叉编码器重排对领域特定检索的增益，发现重排效果依赖于模型与生物证据匹配的适配程度；提出基因感知的查询扩展方法，利用策展注释缓解紧凑生物查询的词汇不匹配问题；比较摘要与全文分块检索，证实当摘要缺失支持证据时全文分块显著提升候选召回与排序性能

Abstract: Biological literature retrieval systems are often developed and evaluated using broad biomedical corpora and general-purpose search tasks. However, many curated knowledge bases operate in narrower model-organism domains, where the literature is sparse and terminology is organism-specific. We introduce a retrieval benchmark from dictyBase for Dictyostelium, a model organism in cell and developmental biology. The benchmark consists of curator-generated biological queries linked to PubMed-indexed articles, together with structured gene annotations. Using this benchmark, we study three factors in niche biological retrieval: cross-encoder reranking, gene-aware query expansion, and abstract-only versus full-text retrieval. We report that reranking and gene-aware query expansion improve retrieval selectively: reranking is most useful when the model is well suited to biological evidence matching, whereas curated annotations help clarify compact biological queries by reducing vocabulary mismatch. Full-text chunks substantially improve retrieval when abstracts omit supporting evidence, increasing both candidate recall and top-rank performance, although these cases are harder than queries supported by abstracts. Data and code are publicly available at this https URL , and the benchmark dataset is additionally archived on Zenodo.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive Sparsity Optimization with Learnable Soft Top-K and Per-Term Thresholding for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02572)
*Wentai Xie,Parker Carlson,Shanxiu He,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对 LLM 神经稀疏检索中向量过长导致的效率问题，提出可学习软 Top-K、逐项阈值化和 FLOPs 正则化协同的自适应稀疏优化方案，在 MS MARCO 和 BEIR 上显著降低查询/文档长度和检索延迟，同时保持竞争力相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的神经稀疏检索中，文档和查询向量因词汇量大而过于冗长，导致检索时间和空间效率低下。

Method: 论文提出一种自适应稀疏优化方案，应用于 Lion-SP 神经稀疏检索模型。输入为查询和文档文本，经过 LLM 进行语义词项扩展，生成高维稀疏表示。核心模块包括：1）可学习的软 Top-K 机制，通过可微的排序操作动态选择最重要的 K 个词项；2）逐项阈值化，对每个词项的权重进行阈值过滤以去除低贡献项；3）FLOPs 正则化，在训练目标中加入计算量惩罚，鼓励模型输出更稀疏的向量。训练时联合优化相关性损失与稀疏性约束。推理时，模型仅保留超过阈值的非零项，从而生成更短的查询和文档向量，直接减少倒排索引的存储占用和检索时的计算量。整个方案旨在不牺牲相关性的前提下，显著提升检索效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均查询和文档长度", "value": "显著减少", "setting": "Lion-SP 模型在 MS MARCO 和 BEIR 数据集上"}, {"metric": "检索延迟", "value": "大幅缩短", "setting": "基于上述稀疏化方案"}, {"metric": "存储成本", "value": "降低", "setting": "基于上述稀疏化方案"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 可学习的软 Top-K 稀疏化；逐项阈值化（per-term thresholding）；FLOPs 正则化以促进稀疏性；自适应策略协同优化模型稀疏度

Abstract: Recent work on neural sparse retrieval has demonstrated strong relevance by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic term expansion. However, learned models paired with previous sparsification techniques still yield overly long document and query vectors partly due to a large LLM vocabulary, imposing a serious challenge to retrieval time and space efficiency. This paper proposes a scheme for optimizing model sparsity through a synergy of adaptive strategies, including learnable soft top-K, per-term thresholding, and FLOPs regularization to increase the sparsity of query and document vectors. Experimental results with Lion-SP model on the MS MARCO and BEIR datasets demonstrate that the proposed scheme can outperform the baselines by significantly reducing the average query and document lengths. Our scheme can achieve much shorter retrieval latency and lower storage cost while maintaining highly competitive relevance.

</details>
